On MDPBench, GRPO reduces the mean share of pages with repetition loops for the 2B model from 3.2% to 0.7%, with a smaller and statistically uncertain score gain for the 0.8B model. We release the model weights, Python package, and evaluation suite.

</details>


### [177] [The Assistant Persona Differs Across Languages in Activation Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27504)
*Daniel Yoo,Naama Rozen,Evan Harris*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Qwen3-32B中"助手人格方向"在31种语言里并不一致：与英文轴余弦相似度仅0.378–0.911，且方向与模长随语言资源水平变化，说明模型不存在跨语言的单一助手表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在低资源语言中安全性更差，这一行为差距已被广泛观察，但机制层面尚不清楚。已有工作通常只从英文提取激活空间方向（如助手人格方向），并默认其可跨语言泛化，本文质疑这一假设。

Method: 在Qwen3-32B中为31种语言分别提取助手人格方向（激活空间方向），计算各语言轴与英文轴的余弦相似度及模长，并将这些量与语言资源水平做相关分析；同时测量模型默认助手状态到"有帮助助手"极点的距离，并进行针对不同语言轴（本语言轴 vs 英文轴）的反向引导（steering）越狱实验。

Result: 各语言助手人格轴与英文轴余弦相似度为0.378–0.911，差异显著；语言轴方向与模长均与语言资源水平相关；低资源语言下模型默认助手状态距有帮助助手极点更远；针对本语言自身轴做反向引导，比针对英文轴更能放大基于人格的越狱攻击。

Conclusion: 模型并不携带跨语言统一的助手表征；助手人格可能需要成为多语言对齐的显式优化目标，仅依赖英文提取的方向不足以保障多语言安全。

Abstract: Large language models behave differently across languages, and are noticeably less safe in lower-resource languages. This gap is well established behaviorally, but not mechanistically. Directions in activation space, including the assistant persona direction, are almost always extracted from English and assumed to generalize across languages. We extract the assistant persona direction in 31 languages in Qwen3-32B, and find that the resulting axes differ substantially: their cosine similarity to the English axis ranges from 0.378 to 0.911. Moreover, each language's assistant persona direction and its magnitude are correlated with the language's resource level. We also find that the model's default assistant state is farther from the helpful assistant pole in lower-resource languages. Finally, steering against a language's own axis amplifies persona-based jailbreak more than steering against the English axis. These results indicate that the model does not carry a single assistant representation across languages. More broadly, the assistant persona may need to be an explicit target of multilingual alignment.

</details>


### [178] [How Many Bytes Has a BPE Model Seen? Estimating Byte-Token Exposure in Pretraining from Corpus Statistics and Embedding Norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27506)
*Liwei Zhang,ZhiMing Tong,Mochen Guan,Linzhe Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: The paper estimates how much training the 256 single-byte fallback tokens of byte-level BPE vocabularies actually receive, using two independent routes: corpus tokenization across 40 domains/33 languages and embedding-row-norm analysis of four open models.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Byte-level BPE vocabularies keep 256 single-byte fallback tokens that initialize pure byte-level models' embeddings, but it is unknown how much training exposure these tokens have received, which matters for understanding and converting such models.

Method: Route 1: tokenize 540 MB of public corpora (40 domains, 33 languages) and combine per-domain token shares with assumed pretraining mixtures. Route 2: analyze embedding and output-matrix row norms for four open models, using untrained padding rows as a zero-exposure baseline and frequent ordinary tokens for calibration.

Result: Single-byte tokens are 13–36% of tokens (mostly ASCII); genuine UTF-8 fallback bytes (0x80–0xFF) are only 0.15–0.27% under mainstream mixtures (~2B occurrences per trillion pretraining tokens). Fallback rates span four orders of magnitude across languages. In all four models, the 13 invalid-UTF-8 bytes stay at initialization, while legal high bytes sit in a high-frequency band; for Qwen3.8 the norm-based estimate matches the corpus estimate within 4x.

Conclusion: Byte tokens' exposure is highly uneven: ASCII bytes are well-trained, rare UTF-8 fallback bytes barely so. Norm-based inversion is model-specific and only order-of-magnitude reliable because the norm-frequency relation folds.

Abstract: Byte-level BPE vocabularies retain 256 single-byte fallback tokens. When converting such a model into a pure byte-level model, these tokens provide the starting embeddings. How much training have they received? We estimate byte-token exposure in Qwen3.8 using two independent routes. First, we tokenize 540 MB of public corpora covering 40 domains and 33 languages, then combine per-domain token shares with assumed pretraining mixtures. Single-byte tokens account for 13 to 36% of tokens, mostly ASCII; genuine UTF-8 fallback bytes (0x80-0xFF) account for 0.15 to 0.27% under mainstream mixtures, or about 2 billion occurrences per trillion pretraining tokens. Fallback rates vary by four orders of magnitude across languages, reflecting how well each script is covered by the vocabulary. Second, we analyze embedding and output-matrix row norms for four open models, using untrained padding rows as a zero-exposure baseline and ordinary tokens with known frequencies for calibration. In all four models, the 13 bytes that cannot occur in valid UTF-8 remain at initialization, while every legal high byte lies in a high-frequency band. For Qwen3.8, the norm-based estimate agrees with the corpus estimate within a factor of four. The norm-frequency relation eventually folds, so inversion is useful only as a model-specific, order-of-magnitude estimate. No private data were used.

</details>


### [179] [Open CDLM: A Benchmark and Library for Continuous Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27536)
*Hao Jiang,Ruixi Chen,Jiayi Han,Yulei Sun,Yujian Chen,Gen Li,Brian. K Chen,Guang Cheng,Weijian Luo,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Open CDLM 基准与开源库，用于可靠评估连续扩散语言模型（CDLM）及其采样器：澄清似然类指标的适用边界，并引入 Kernel Text Discrepancy（KTD）等样本级诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CDLM 评估存在两类缺陷：似然评估常用离散负对数似然的变分上界，其松弛量会干扰比较；样本评估常把生成困惑度与序列内 unigram 熵结合，但即使采样器每次都生成相同文本，这两项仍可能表现良好，无法揭示多样性崩塌。

Method: 构建 Open CDLM 基准与配套开源库；似然方面澄清报告的困惑度上界能支持何种模型拟合比较及其对评估流程的依赖；样本方面采用 Kernel Text Discrepancy（KTD）比较生成文本集合与真实文本集合，并辅以多样性、有效性与覆盖度诊断；在 OpenWebText 上做受控实验，包括用单一选段副本替换文本集合等扰动。

Result: 在受控 OpenWebText 实验中，将文本集合替换为单一选段副本后，传统生成指标（生成困惑度与 unigram 熵）反而同时改善，而 MAUVE 能检测到这种崩塌；MAUVE 对其他扰动的响应则依赖文本表示方式与扰动类型。

Conclusion: Open CDLM 作为开源项目启动 CDLM 研究，将持续维护与扩展并欢迎社区贡献；评测应同时关注模型拟合与生成分布，并采用不易被退化采样"欺骗"的指标。

Abstract: Reliable comparisons of continuous diffusion language models (CDLMs) require evaluating both model fit and the distribution of generated text. Two common practices leave this goal only partially addressed. Likelihood-based evaluation often reports a variational upper bound on discrete negative log-likelihood, whose slack can confound comparisons. Generated-sample-based evaluation commonly combines generative perplexity with within-sequence unigram entropy; both scores can remain favorable even when a sampler produces the same text on every draw. We introduce \textbf{Open CDLM}, a benchmark for evaluating CDLMs and their samplers, and describe the design of an accompanying open-source library to support reproducible comparisons. For likelihood-based evaluation, we clarify which comparisons of model fit are supported by reported perplexity bounds and how those conclusions depend on the evaluation procedure. For sample-based evaluation, we use \textit{Kernel Text Discrepancy} (\textbf{KTD}) to compare collections of generated and real texts, alongside diagnostics for diversity, validity, and coverage. In controlled OpenWebText experiments, replacing a collection of texts with copies of a single selected passage improves both conventional generation scores. MAUVE detects the resulting collapse, and its response to other perturbations depends on the text representation and the type of perturbation. This work marks the beginning of Open CDLM as an open-source project for CDLM research. We will maintain and extend the library over time and welcome contributions from the community.

</details>


### [180] [Alliance Rupture and Repair in Simulating Cognitive Behavioral Therapy Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27544)
*Jian Chai,Yitian Yang,Siqi Liu,Yi-Chia Wang,Yi-Chieh Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ALLY是一个CBT对话模拟框架，引入隐藏的联盟状态，使来访者出现联盟破裂，咨询师需察觉、修复并重新规划会话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CBT模拟器只模拟来访者抗拒并遵循固定治疗计划，很少追踪联盟状态，也无法多轮修复破裂并据此调整计划。

Method: 每个来访者拥有隐藏联盟状态，决定其开放程度；咨询师智能体仅能看到对话，须自行察觉来访者退缩或抵触，进行修复并基于所学重新规划后续会话。

Result: 自动评估显示ALLY咨询师在策略流程和情感动态上比现有公开合成数据集更接近真实CBT咨询师；在咨询质量(CTRS)和人际技能(FIS-T)上得分最高。20位持证治疗师的专家评估认为模拟破裂合理、修复与重规划大体恰当。

Conclusion: 将联盟破裂—修复—重规划纳入CBT模拟能显著提升模拟真实性与咨询质量，并获得临床专家认可。

Abstract: In cognitive behavioral therapy (CBT), routine work can strain the client-counselor working alliance, and the counselor must then notice the rupture, repair it, and adapt the work that follows. Existing CBT simulators model client resistance and follow structured treatment plans, but rarely track alliance state or allow counselors to repair ruptures over multiple turns and revise their plans accordingly. We present ALLY, a simulation framework that brings alliance rupture, repair, and replanning into ongoing CBT dialogue. Each client has a hidden alliance state that governs how openly the client engages. Like a real counselor, the counselor agent sees only the dialogue. It must notice when the client pulls back or pushes against the work, repair the rupture, and replan the rest of the session around what it has learned. Automatic evaluation shows that ALLY's counselors are closer to real CBT counselors than those of existing public synthetic datasets in strategy flow and emotional dynamics, and that ALLY is rated highest overall in counseling quality (CTRS) and interpersonal skill (FIS-T). An expert evaluation with 20 licensed therapists also finds the simulated ruptures plausible and the repairs and replanning largely appropriate.

</details>


### [181] [Tasklets: A Unit-Test Benchmark for Language Model Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27607)
*Zayd Muhammad Kawakibi Zuhri,Munachiso Samuel Nwadike,Kentaro Inui,Alham Fikri Aji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Tasklets 基准，汇集小型合成任务以统一评测语言模型序列混合层的计算能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有架构研究广泛使用小型合成任务（如回忆、序列操作、状态跟踪）来评估序列混合层，但任务选择、形式与评测方式不一致，导致跨研究比较困难。

Method: 构建 Tasklets 基准，按所测能力组织任务，提供统一设置；用小型模型进行受控实验，比较多种层类型。

Result: 通过受控实验对广泛的层类型进行了比较（摘要未给出具体量化结论）。

Conclusion: Tasklets 支持可复现、低成本的新架构提案测试，代码与实现已开源。

Abstract: We introduce Tasklets, a benchmark of small synthetic tasks for evaluating the computational capabilities of language model sequence-mixing layers. These tasks probe foundational capabilities such as recall, sequence manipulation, and state tracking. Such tasks are widely used in architecture research, but differences in task selection, formulation, and evaluation make comparisons across studies difficult. Tasklets brings these tasks together, organizing them by the capabilities they test and providing a shared setup for evaluating how different architectural layer types learn fundamental computations. We compare a broad range of layer types through controlled experiments with small models. Our task implementations and evaluation code support reproducible, low-cost testing of new architectural proposals: https://github.com/zaydzuhri/flame/tree/tasklets.

</details>


### [182] [Auditing Post-Training Unlearning in Diffusion Language Models using Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27652)
*Yunha Seo,Changhoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在扩散语言模型 LLaDA-8B-Instruct 上首次系统探究机器遗忘，利用 top-k 稀疏自编码器与去噪状态回放比较"静态特征抑制"与"阶段自适应特征抑制"两种策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私保护需要去除指定数据影响、同时保留应有行为。自回归 LLM 的遗忘已有较多研究，但扩散语言模型（DLM）几乎空白；DLM 通过迭代去噪生成，中间被掩码状态的内部表示随阶段变化，由此产生 DLM 特有问题：能否借"识别遗忘数据相关特征 + 按去噪阶段自适应抑制"来改进选择性遗忘？

Method: 在 LLaDA-8B-Instruct 上进行受控实验；以 WMDP 作为遗忘域基准（对应预训练可能编码的知识）；用 top-k 稀疏自编码器定位遗忘数据相关特征，并通过去噪状态回放，比较静态抑制与阶段自适应抑制的效果。

Result: 观察到遗忘数据相关特征在去噪各阶段存在显著漂移；但仅跟踪这种动态并不能在所评估的工作点上稳定改善"遗忘—保留"权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究为将机器遗忘发展为 DLM 后训练隐私机制提供了初步的实证基础，并提示"阶段动态特征"并非自动带来更优遗忘效果，需进一步设计。

Abstract: Machine unlearning is an approach to privacy protection: it aims to remove the influence of designated data while preserving the model's behavior on information that should be retained. While a growing body of work investigates this objective in autoregressive large language models, machine unlearning remains underexplored in diffusion language models (DLMs). DLMs generate text through iterative denoising, exposing partially masked states whose internal representations change throughout the generation process. This raises a DLM-specific question: can selective forgetting improve by identifying forget-data-associated features and adapting their suppression across denoising stages? We conduct a controlled investigation of this question in LLaDA-8B-Instruct. To evaluate the unlearning of knowledge plausibly encoded during pre-training, we use WMDP as our forget-domain benchmark. Using top-k sparse autoencoders and denoising-state replay, we compare static and stage-adaptive feature suppression. We find substantial shifts in forget-data-associated features across denoising stages, but tracking these dynamics alone does not reliably improve the forget-retain trade-off at the evaluated operating points. Together, our study provides an initial empirical foundation for developing and evaluating machine unlearning as a post-training privacy mechanism in DLMs.

</details>


### [183] [Hy-MT3: More Accurate, Robust and Practical Multilingual Translation Models in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27673)
*Mao Zheng,Zheng Li,Fangxu Liu,Tao Chen,Bingxin Qu,Bo Lv,Mingrui Sun,Hongyu Li,Hong Huang,Jinlong Song,Decheng Wu,Guanghua Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Hy-MT3 是腾讯混元第三代多语言翻译模型家族（1.8B/7B/30B-A3B MoE），在误译、漏译增译和指令遵循错误上分别降低 43%、74%、40%，语言覆盖从 38 扩至 102 种；30B-A3B 仅激活 3B 参数即在非思考模式综合最优，并针对游戏本地化与图像翻译优化，开源 TransRubric 评测套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向真实场景的多语言翻译需要在准确性、完整性和用户约束遵循上进一步提升，同时覆盖更多语种，并解决游戏本地化、图像翻译等工业落地需求，以及现有自动评测不够稳定、与人工评价一致性不足的问题。

Method: 发布三代模型系列（Dense 1.8B/7B 与 MoE 30B-A3B）；用高质量游戏领域数据与《王者荣耀》团队的专业评测体系优化游戏本地化，并提供开源工具链接入研发流水线；针对图像翻译增强上下文理解与纠错以容忍 OCR 错误；提出基于评分量表（rubric）的自动评测套件 TransRubric。

Result: 真实场景数据上误译样本占比降 43%，漏译/增译降 74%，指令遵循错误降 40%，语种 38→102；Hy-MT3-30B-A3B 激活 3B 参数时在非思考模式整体最佳，优于 Qwen3.8-Max、Kimi-K3.1-Pro、DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash，接近 GPT-6.1-Sol、Gemini-4-Argon，并超越推理算力超其 30 倍以上的模型；游戏本地化核心指标持平或超过 Gemini 3.1 Pro；图像翻译在同规模模型中最佳；TransRubric 在 WMT26 metrics 测试集上优于其他评测方案且与人工评价更稳定一致。

Conclusion: Hy-MT3 以较小激活参数取得领先的通用翻译性能，并通过领域数据与评测体系覆盖游戏本地化、图像翻译等工业场景，同时以开源 TransRubric 推动翻译评测标准化。

Abstract: Hy-MT3 is the third generation of the Hunyuan multilingual translation model family, including \textbf{Hy-MT3-1.8B}, \textbf{Hy-MT3-7B} and \textbf{Hy-MT3-30B-A3B (MoE)}. Compared with Hy-MT2, Hy-MT3 translates \textbf{more accurately, more completely and more faithfully to user constraints}: on real-world scenario data, the share of samples with mistranslations is reduced by \textbf{43\%}, omissions or additions by \textbf{74\%}, and instruction-following errors by \textbf{40\%}, while language coverage is expanded from \textbf{38 to 102} languages. With only 3B activated parameters, \textbf{Hy-MT3-30B-A3B achieves the best overall performance among all evaluated models in non-thinking mode}, including flagship models such as Qwen3.8-Max, Kimi-K3.1-Pro and DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash, and approaches the strongest closed-source thinking models such as GPT-6.1-Sol and Gemini-4-Argon, while surpassing models that consume \textbf{more than 30$\times$} its inference compute. Beyond general translation, Hy-MT3 is optimized for two industrial scenarios. For \textbf{game localization}, built with high-quality game-domain data and a professional evaluation system from the Honor of Kings team, Hy-MT3-30B-A3B matches or outperforms strong closed-source models such as Gemini 3.1 Pro on all core metrics, and can be plugged into game development pipelines through an open-source toolchain. For \textbf{image translation}, Hy-MT3 improves context understanding and error correction to better tolerate OCR errors, achieving the best result among models of comparable size. Finally, we open-source \textbf{TransRubric}, a rubric-based automatic evaluation suite for translation, which outperforms other evaluation schemes on the WMT26 metrics shared task test set and is more stable with respect to human evaluation.

</details>


### [184] [Decision Models for Multilingual Emotion Classification: A Comparative Study Against LLMs and Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27721)
*Alessandro Piccolo,Francesco De Nicolò,Francesco Manco,Eufemia Lella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究在5种语言、10个社交媒体数据集、4类情感上，将3个决策模型与2个大语言模型（零样本）同2个微调多语编码器做配对差异与等价检验，结果显示最佳零样本系统平均仅比目标数据集微调的编码器低约1个宏F1点，是缺乏标注数据时的强默认方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策模型（输入封闭答案列表、输出各答案概率）宣称低延迟且对提示措辞稳健，但没有研究在多语言场景下将它们与多个此类系统及微调编码器进行比较；作者想知道零样本系统能否替代微调，以及决策格式相较通用LLM带来何种额外价值。

Method: 比较3个决策模型（Jev、OpenAI Decisions、Strands Decider 2B）与2个LLM（GPT-6 Luna、Qwen3.5-2B，均接收意大利语指令），以及2个微调多语编码器（tabularisai、XLM-EMO），任务为10个社交媒体数据集、5种语言上的4种情感（喜、悲、怒、惧）识别，采用配对差异检验与等价检验；另做提示语言、同尺寸模型、延迟与跨提示标准差等分析。

Result: 最佳零样本系统平均比在目标数据集上微调的编码器低约1个宏F1点，但检验无法排除4–5点差距，单数据集上任一方最多可赢22点；它们比在另一数据集微调的编码器高8–15点（对XLM-EMO可能不推广到新数据集）；提示语言改变结果不足1点；探索性上比最强公开情感模型高约6点。OpenAI决策端点与GPT-6 Luna持平但延迟约为其1/5；20亿参数决策模型比同尺寸通用LLM高10点，归因于训练而非决策格式；最佳零样本系统跨提示标准差约1点；仅最大数据集GoEmotions上微调大幅领先。

Conclusion: 无标注数据时，零样本决策模型与LLM是强默认选择；决策模型额外提供更低延迟和逐选项概率（不同于生成式LLM用法）；微调仅在数据量最大的场景（GoEmotions）显著占优。

Abstract: Decision models receive a question with a closed list of answers and return a probability for each answer instead of free text. They promise low latency and robustness to prompt wording, but no study has compared several of them with fine-tuned encoders across languages. We ask whether systems used without training on labelled data (zero-shot) can replace fine-tuning, and what the decision format adds over a general-purpose large language model (LLM). We compare three decision models (Jev, OpenAI Decisions, Strands Decider 2B) and two LLMs (GPT-6 Luna, Qwen3.5-2B), given Italian instructions, with two fine-tuned multilingual encoders (tabularisai, XLM-EMO) on four emotions (joy, sadness, anger, fear) in 10 social-media datasets in five languages, with paired difference and equivalence tests. The three best zero-shot systems (two decision models and one LLM) score on average about one macro-F1 point below an encoder fine-tuned on the target dataset; our test sets cannot exclude a gap of 4-5 points, and on single datasets either approach wins by up to 22 points. They beat encoders fine-tuned on another dataset by 8-15 points, though against XLM-EMO this may not extend to new datasets, and instructions in each text's language change their results by under 1 point. Exploratorily, they beat the strongest public emotion model used as is by about 6 points. OpenAI's decision endpoint matches GPT-6 Luna, the LLM it runs on, at a fifth of its median latency, and a 2-billion-parameter decision model beats a same-size general LLM by 10 points, owing to its training, not the decision format. The best zero-shot systems, the LLM included, have an SD of about 1 point across prompts. Without labelled data they are a strong default, and decision models add lower latency and, unlike generative LLM use, per-option probabilities. Fine-tuning won by a wide margin only on the largest dataset, GoEmotions.

</details>


### [185] [AutoOntologyBench: A Task-Grounded Benchmark for Agentic Knowledge Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27734)
*Minye Shao,Chenghao Xiao,Xingyue Zhao,Yang Long*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AutoOntologyBench：19,947个真实任务接地、覆盖7领域18知识源，评测自主智能体构建本体并用于解题的价值；智能体自建本体在检索之上提升6.1分，但落后专家本体13.0分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型智能体正从软件工程扩展到自主科学研究，核心难点是获取与整合领域知识。工业界已采用知识图谱与本体语义层组织异构知识，但尚不清楚智能体能否自主构建有助于真实任务的本体，且现有基准只孤立评测本体正确性或任务成功率，无法回答自主获取知识的实际价值。

Method: 提出AutoOntologyBench任务接地的基准：含19,947个经审计任务，基于7个领域18个真实知识源，采用源生验证；覆盖应用、修复、组织、构建四类任务，在匹配知识表示条件下比较九个前沿模型智能体。

Result: 在检索之上叠加智能体构建的本体可带来6.1个百分点的任务成功率提升，但仍比专家推导的参考本体低13.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 自主知识获取是迈向可扩展科学研究的瓶颈；智能体自建本体有增益但显著不及专家水平，需进一步研究与改进。

Abstract: Foundation-model agents are increasingly extending beyond software engineering (SWE) into autonomous scientific research, where acquiring and integrating domain knowledge remains a critical challenge. Recent industrial advances have increasingly adopted knowledge graphs and ontology-based semantic layers to organize heterogeneous knowledge into reusable representations. We ask: Can autonomous agents construct ontologies that help them solve real tasks, and do they offer measurable benefits over alternative knowledge representations? Existing benchmarks evaluate ontology correctness or agent task success in isolation, leaving the value of autonomously acquired knowledge unresolved. We therefore introduce AutoOntologyBench, a task-grounded benchmark comprising 19,947 audited tasks grounded in 18 real-world knowledge sources across 7 domains, with source-native verification. Spanning Application, Repair, Organization, and Construction, AutoOntologyBench compares nine frontier-model agents under matched knowledge representations. Layered on retrieval, agent-built ontologies add 6.1 points of task success, yet trail an expert-derived reference by 13.0, identifying autonomous knowledge acquisition as the bottleneck to scalable scientific research. Code and resources are available at https://github.com/VinyehShaw/AutoOntologyBench.

</details>


### [186] [RoSci: Exploring Robustness in Science VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27750)
*Junying Wang,Yijin Guo,Yalun Wu,Ziheng Jiao,Farong Wen,Qihang Yan,Wenzhe Li,Ye Shen,Qi Jia,Chunyi Li,Zicheng Zhang,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"科学模型鲁棒性"概念并构建RoSci-Bench基准(1220样本、六类退化)与RoSci-Agent框架(Parse-Verify-Decide流程),评测VLM在信息错误/缺失下的检测、可解性判断与自我校准能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在科学任务中通常被假设获得完整准确信息,忽视了真实场景中信息错误(不一致)或信息缺失(不充分)的挑战,模型甚至会对无效前提给出答案。

Method: 提出科学模型鲁棒性定义;用种子-变体策略构建含1,220样本、六种退化类型的诊断基准RoSci-Bench;设计RoSci-Agent框架,通过Parse-Verify-Decide流水线在推理前核查前提。

Result: 被评测的VLM有时会对前提无效或不完整的问题直接作答;RoSci-Agent的整体鲁棒性得分高于所评测的独立VLM。

Conclusion: RoSci-Bench为多模态推理社区提供了统一测试,用于衡量模型在何时应作答、请求澄清或拒绝科学问题。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable significant progress in scientific tasks. However, these models are generally assumed to be presented with complete and accurate information, overlooking the challenges of erroneous/missing information in wild scenarios. To address this gap, we introduce the concept of Scientific Model Robustness, which defines a model's ability to detect anomalies, assess solvability, and perform self-calibration when faced with Information Error (inconsistencies) or Information Loss (insufficiency). Specifically, we present RoSci-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark of 1,220 samples using a seed-variant strategy across six degradation types. To address VLM fragility, we further propose RoSci-Agent, a framework that employs a Parse-Verify-Decide pipeline to vet premises before reasoning. The evaluated VLMs sometimes answer questions whose premises are invalid or incomplete. RoSci-Agent achieves a higher Overall Robustness Score than the evaluated standalone VLMs. RoSci-Bench gives the multimodal reasoning community a common test of when models answer, request clarification, or reject a scientific question.

</details>


### [187] [From Novel Combinations to Informative Absence: Structural permission, communicative opportunity, and form choice in language learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27762)
*Yanzhe Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"反事实表达机会"生成理论，将话语产生分解为结构许可、交际机会与形式选择，解释语言学习者如何判断新表达是否合法、以及"持续不出现"何时构成反证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习者面临两个核心难题：哪些新组合在语法上被许可；何时"没听到某表达"应被视为负面证据。现有研究未统一处理这两个问题。

Method: (1) 用共享预测性续接从30个人工语法中恢复未见词-结构搭配的结构许可；(2) 在层级不确定性下推导将省略与语境多样性、语境重复相联系的联合证据律，及有限样本语境精化不等式；(3) 证明原始计数混淆语义可得性与形式选择，而场景级机会观测可同时识别两者；(4) 重新分析成人与儿童人工语言实验、自然主义儿童语料（11家庭、6语料库）及五种语言的使役判断。

Result: 结构许可恢复显著（120句后平均AUC 0.945）；省略的证据价值随语境多样性与重复单调变化，高预期使用可导致更强许可候选的后验更快下降（固定速率例子中6次省略后顺序反转）；意义条件限制成立，受限组合判断提升留出产出预测（399名被试，+0.036 nats/产出）；自然语料显示超出词汇、数、依存角色控制的来源级用法聚集；跨五语言使役判断表明结构兼容性与校准使用概率依赖不同线索。

Conclusion: 交际机会的组织与校准决定了语言缺失的证据价值；结构许可与使用概率是可通过可区分线索习得的独立成分。

Abstract: Language learners must infer which novel expressions are grammatically licensed and when continued absence is evidence against them. Our generative theory unites these capacities via counterfactual expression opportunities, decomposing utterance generation into structural permission, communicative opportunity, and form choice. First, shared predictive continuations recover structural permission for unseen word-construction pairings across 30 artificial grammars (mean AUC 0.945 after 120 sentences). Second, under hierarchical uncertainty, we derive a joint evidence law linking omissions to context diversity and within-context repetition, and an exact finite-sample context-refinement inequality. Thus, observing related expressions calibrates expected use to sharpen negative evidence, and higher expected use can drive steeper posterior declines for stronger-permission candidates (reversing initial order after six omissions in a fixed-rate example). Third, we prove that while raw counts confound semantic availability and form selection, scene-level opportunity observations identify both. Empirically, reanalyzing adult and child artificial-language experiments shows meaning-conditioned restriction; judgments of restricted combinations improve held-out production predictions across 399 participants (+0.036 nats/production). Naturalistic child-directed and child speech (11 families, six corpora) shows pervasive source-level usage clustering beyond lexical, number, and dependency-role controls, grounding hierarchical rate uncertainty. Finally, modeling causative judgments across five languages shows that structural compatibility and calibrated usage probabilities draw on distinct distributional, semantic, and discriminative cues. Together, these findings show how the organization and calibration of communicative opportunities govern the evidential value of linguistic absence.

</details>


### [188] [Enhancing Attention with Higher-Order Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27764)
*R. I. Cukier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出解码器注意力的"自回归"性质并不严格，提出三种增强自回归特性的注意力方法，以提升推理/生成所用自回归概率的信息量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前解码器大模型依赖注意力、前馈与残差层，所用注意力被称为自回归（AR），但作者认为这一命名并不严格正确，因此希望引入能真正增强 AR 特性的注意力机制。

Method: 提出三类注意力：(1) 基于互信息分解为一组条件互信息（CMI），将 key token 与成对 query token 相关联；(2) 高阶注意力，引入相邻 key 与 query token 间的相关性；(3) 引入一组 key 与 query token 间的相关性，并利用广义最大熵原理得到跨 token 序列的注意力表达式。

Result: 这些方法可增强推理/生成中所用自回归概率的信息含量；并应用于对比解码（contrastive decoding）及对自回归概率的系统性分析。

Conclusion: 通过重思"自回归注意力"的概念，可设计出更具 AR 特性、信息更丰富的注意力变体，并用于对比解码与自回归概率分析。

Abstract: Current decoder large language models rely on a combination of attention, feed forward and residual layers. The attention used is autoregressive (AR) though this is not strictly correct, as we note. That motivates the introduction of other attention methods that, while also not strictly AR, do enhance the AR character. One is based on a decomposition of mutual information (MI) into a set of Conditional MIs (CMIs) that correlate key tokens with pairs of query tokens. A second, higher-order attention, introduces correlations between contiguous key and query tokens. A third introduces correlations between a set of key and query tokens and uses a Generalized Maximum Entropy Principle to obtain an attention expression spanning a sequence of tokens. All these methods may enhance the information content of the autoregressive probabilities that are used in inference/generation. Applications to contrastive decoding and a systematic analysis of autoregressive probabilities are presented.

</details>


### [189] [Evaluating Prompting Strategies for Zero-Shot Multilingual Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27807)
*Jakub Šm{í}d,Pavel Kr{á}l*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 系统评估7个开权重LLM在12种语言、3个领域的零样本TASD，发现单步提示优于多步流水线，稠密模型优于MoE，初始方面词召回是瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ABSA研究多聚焦英语或依赖微调模型，缺乏对开权重LLM在跨语言族、跨领域零样本TASD（复合三元组抽取）能力的系统理解。

Method: 选用7个指令微调开权重模型（8B–35B，含稠密与MoE架构），覆盖12种语言、3个领域，比较四种提示范式：单步（monolithic）、思维链、顺序式、顺序精修；并进行细粒度错误分类画像与流水线步骤级追踪、生成量分析。

Result: 单步提示取得强零样本性能并优于多步流水线；稠密模型稳定优于稀疏MoE；初始方面词召回构成硬瓶颈，多步分解降低抽取质量；迭代精修仅在大模型上部分挽回损失，在小模型上产生净负的过度精修；多步策略带来最高2.38倍token开销却无F1提升。

Conclusion: 对多语言ABSA，单步提示是最有效且最高效的范式，简单直接优于复杂分解流水线。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) have advanced natural language understanding, their ability to perform complex target-aspect-sentiment detection (TASD) -- a key task within aspect-based sentiment analysis (ABSA) -- in zero-shot multilingual settings remains underexplored. Existing ABSA research predominantly focuses on English or relies on fine-tuned task-specific models, leaving a gap in understanding how open-weight LLMs handle compound sentiment triplet extraction across diverse language families and domains. We present a systematic evaluation of zero-shot multilingual TASD across 12 languages and three domains using seven instruction-tuned open-weight models -- ranging from 8B to 35B parameters across dense and mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures -- under four prompting paradigms: monolithic, chain-of-thought, sequential, and sequential refine. Our results show that direct monolithic prompting achieves strong zero-shot performance, outperforming multi-step pipelines, while dense models consistently outperform sparse MoE architectures. Through fine-grained error taxonomy profiling and step-level pipeline tracking, we demonstrate that initial aspect term recall forms a hard bottleneck, whereas multi-step decomposition degrades extraction quality; iterative refinement only partially recovers this loss on larger models while inducing net-negative over-refinement on smaller architectures. Finally, generation volume analysis shows that multi-step strategies incur up to 2.38x token overhead without F1 gains, establishing monolithic prompting as the most effective and efficient paradigm for multilingual ABSA.

</details>


### [190] [MemOnce: A Scalable Memory Architecture for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27973)
*Chang Nie,Chaoyou Fu,Songlin Liu,Yiqing Hu,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemOnce 是一种学习可扩展记忆状态的基础架构，通过"记忆编码器-解码器"与"记忆稀疏注意力"两大设计，仅缓存最后一层少量隐状态作为紧凑记忆，并跨层复用稀疏索引动态解码为 KV 缓存，实现线性时间复杂度并大幅降低显存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程、持续交互场景下 LLM 需要维护可复用的记忆状态。标准 Transformer 的逐层、逐 token KV 缓存带来线性显存增长与二次计算复杂度；而线性时间架构虽将历史压缩为固定大小状态，却难以保留细粒度细节。因此需要兼顾效率与细节保真度的新架构。

Method: 提出 MemOnce，包含两个核心设计：(a) 记忆编码器-解码器：只缓存最后一层少量隐状态作为紧凑记忆表示，并按需动态解码回注意力层的 KV 缓存；(b) 记忆稀疏注意力：对每个持续输入自适应检索相关记忆表示，且稀疏索引只在所有层间计算一次。二者结合实现高效的即时记忆加载与 KV 解码。

Result: 在多种模型规模、上下文长度及训练范式（预训练与后训练适配）下均优于 Transformer 及其他架构。以 Qwen3.5-4B 适配为例：1M 上下文下记忆状态缓存减少 1313×，prefill 延迟降低 6.9×，吞吐提升 3.5×，整体记忆性能提升 +5.77%。此外可零样本泛化到下游表示任务，与专用模型表现相当。

Conclusion: MemOnce 通过紧凑记忆状态与跨层共享稀疏检索，同时实现了显存高效、线性复杂度和细粒度信息保留，可作为长程记忆场景下的可扩展基础架构，并具备良好的下游迁移能力。

Abstract: Deploying large language models (LLMs) in long-horizon and continual interactions requires maintaining reusable memory states for future inference, posing challenges for existing architectures. Standard Transformers accumulate layer-wise, token-level KV caches, incurring linear GPU memory growth and quadratic computational complexity as the context expands. In contrast, linear-time architectures compress history into fixed-size states, but struggle to retain fine-grained details. We introduce MemOnce, a foundation architecture that learns scalable memory states for language models. MemOnce is based on two core designs: (a) Memory encoder-decoder: it caches only a small subset of final-layer hidden states as compact memory representations and dynamically decodes them back into KV caches for attention layers; (b) Memory sparse attention: for each continual input, MemOnce adaptively retrieves relevant memory representations, with the sparse indices computed only once across layers. Coupling these two designs enables efficient on-the-fly memory loading and KV cache decoding, drastically reducing the GPU memory footprint and achieving linear-time complexity. We conduct extensive experiments to show that MemOnce outperforms Transformers and other architectures across varying model sizes, context lengths, and training paradigms (pretraining and post-training adaptation). In particular, adapting Qwen3.5-4B with MemOnce reduces memory state cache by 1313$\times$ and prefill latency by 6.9$\times$ at 1M context, and improves throughput by 3.5$\times$ and overall memory performance by +5.77%. Finally, MemOnce generalizes zero-shot to downstream representation tasks, achieving competitive results against specialized models.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Testing Settlement Flags after Late Evidence and Process Restart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26665)
*Zengjian Liu,Tommy He,ELE Zhao,HongLiang Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一个可执行的合约，用于区分"结算标志在应用重启后仍残留"这类歧义经济状态；通过504条合成历史、文件与SQLite双实现对比及672次受控进程退出来检验一致性，发现文件实现漏掉54例同身份支付日期冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结算标志在应用重启后可以存活，但其经济后果（是否重复记账、是否提前入账）仍然模糊，现有仅凭运行标志、产物存在性和购买计数等监视手段无法识别这类错误。

Method: 定义一个合约，固定计划、两个截断点处的来源准入、以身份为键的账本效果以及调用后状态；生成504条合成历史，分别在文件实现与独立编写的SQLite实现上运行，并进行672次受控进程退出。

Result: 文件实现漏检54起同身份支付日期冲突，其中27起提前入账现金；基于运行标志、产物存在性与购买计数的监视器把这54起全部判为通过。该合约还能在运行中的内核与文件系统上区分"等待关闭的购买"与"正确报价的等待"。

Conclusion: 仅靠表面持久化标志不足以判定经济正确性，需要以身份为键、跨重启边界的可执行合约作为判定标准。

Abstract: A settlement flag can survive an application restart while the economic effects remain ambiguous. We define a contract linking the fixed plan, source admission at two cutoffs, identity-keyed ledger effects and post-invocation state. We generated 504 synthetic histories, ran each through a file implementation and a separately coded SQLite implementation, and observed 672 controlled process exits. The file implementation missed 54 same-identity payment-date conflicts, including 27 that credited cash early, and a monitor using run flags, artifact presence and purchase counts accepted all 54. The same contract distinguishes a purchase awaiting closure from a correct quote wait for a single writer on a running kernel and filesystem.

</details>


### [192] [Which Archived Results Survive a Rule Change? A Case Study in Revalidating Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26666)
*Zengjian Liu,Tommy He,ELE Zhao,HongLiang Sun*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究提出应将"数值结果仍正确"与"其批准依据是否仍有效"区分开来，指出常见增量构建方法只识别可复用计算，却不能判断每次批准背后的政策是否过期；通过对312条配对研究记录重放迁移解析器，发现若把旧投影当作当前有效，11/312条记录至少有一个字段会变化，且数值一致也可能缺乏当前证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究工作流中，结论所依据的证据或审批可能已经过期，但数值结果仍被保留和沿用。现有增量构建方法只关注计算复用，缺少对"留存结果是否有当前有效批准/证明"的判断，因此需要将数值恢复与适用审批分离考察。

Method: 对一次已记录的研究解析器迁移开展实证研究：涵盖312对配对研究记录，用相同输入重放新旧两个解析器，对整个队列施加计算文件遗漏（calculation-file omission）处理，并在GNU Make对比环境中评估"当前证明"的判定能力。

Result: 若将先前的投影视为当前有效，312条记录中有11条至少有一个字段会发生变化；受控案例表明，即使数值完全一致，也可能不具备当前证明。

Conclusion: 研究区分了数值恢复与适用审批两个层面，并给出维护型依赖图能够同时保证两者的条件，为可审计的研究工作流提供了依据。

Abstract: A research workflow can retain a correct numerical result after the evidence or approval that supported its use has expired. Common incremental build methods identify reusable computations, while deciding what remains usable also requires knowing which policy justified each approval. We examined a recorded parser migration across 312 paired research records, replayed both parsers with the same inputs, applied a calculation-file omission to the complete cohort, and evaluated current proof in a GNU Make comparison. Treating the prior projections as current would change at least one field in 11 of 312 records, and controlled cases show that even matching values can lack a current proof. The study separates numerical recovery from applicable approval and sets out the conditions under which a maintained dependency graph can preserve both.

</details>


### [193] [How developers split roles among them and a coding agent and what "collaboration" means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26668)
*Linus Ververs,Helya Dadkhah,Lutz Prechelt*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过2025年博客文章定性编码与2026年开发者问卷调查的混合方法，考察开发者如何划分自己与编码智能体（如Claude Code）的角色分工，并发现智能体参与度显著上升，而问责仍由人类承担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体能力不断增强，其在开发流程中的角色随之扩大，但开发者如何界定自身与智能体的角色分工、理想协作方式是什么，尚缺乏实证认识。

Method: 三步混合方法：对2025年早期采用者的博客文章进行开放式定性编码；对2026年从业者开展定量问卷；并开发一种由编码智能体支持的开放式编码方法来处理问卷自由文本。

Result: 2025年智能体仅承担辅助角色，问责牢牢归于人类；2026年这两点基本仍成立，但角色划分已明显变化，智能体参与度大幅提高。开发者对协作的理解包含三方面：将工作步骤分为纯人类、纯智能体与协作三类并设定条件与目标；容纳缺乏工程依据的个人偏好；以覆盖整个开发流程的整体视角看待协作。

Conclusion: 界定自身相对智能体的角色将成为一项反复进行的工作；团队内部对协作的不同理解很可能成为摩擦与冲突来源，后续研究需探讨应对方式。

Abstract: Background: Coding agents such as Claude Code become more and more competent, so that their role in the development process can grow.
  Objective: How do developers characterize the role-split between them and their agent? How would they ideally want to collaborate with their agent?
  Methods: We perform a 3-step, mixed-methods study. We apply qualitative open coding to 2025 early adopters' blog articles; perform a quantitative survey of 2026 practitioners; develop and apply a method for coding-agent-supported open coding of survey freetext responses.
  Results: The 2025 practitioners have the agent work in supporting roles only. Accountability was firmly human. In 2026, both are fundamentally still true. Gradually, however, the role-split is now different, with much more agent involvement. The developers' notion of collaboration has three facets: Predominantly a separation into human-only, agent-only, and collaborative work steps, with defined conditions and goals. Second, accommodating personal preferences without engineering justification. Third, a holistic view of collaboration covering the entire development process.
  Conclusions: Defining one's role relative to the agent will need to be a recurring activity. The different views on collaboration are likely a source of friction and conflict for teams; future work should find out how to cope with that.

</details>


### [194] [Exercised, Rarely Recovered: What Public Reports Say About Recovery in 50 AI Agent Harnesses and Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27090)
*Kaviru Hapuarachchi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文以预先冻结的审阅协议，对 12 个持久化智能体运行时、26 个编码智能体框架和 12 个研究型运行时的 8 种恢复机制进行证据分级（从"未发现"到"真实运行中被实际使用"），并结合两轮盲审、仲裁与回档复核。结论是：恢复机制在真实使用中几乎处处被触发，但成功率很低；而研究型运行时则相反——有基准评测却无实际运行报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 编码智能体存在误删文件、长任务崩溃等故障，业界与学界都宣称提供检查点续跑、撤销、人工审批、日志、Saga 补偿、事务回滚等更强保障。但这类承诺是否在真实使用中兑现，缺乏公共证据。作者因此要回答：两类"恢复保证"在实践中是否真的有效。

Method: 采用数据采集前即冻结的评估协议，对 50 个系统（12 持久化运行时 + 26 编码智能体框架 + 12 研究型运行时）× 8 种恢复机制进行成熟度分级；分级从"未发现"到"真实运行中被实际使用"，并设置两轮盲审编码、分歧仲裁与对照存档来源的再验证，以保证可复现与低偏差。

Result: 恢复机制在实践层面被广泛使用：12 个运行时中 11 个、26 个框架中 25 个至少有一条真实使用报告。但 128 条此类报告中，仅 19 条为完全恢复，54 条为部分恢复，45 条失败；检查点/续跑在 27 条报告中仅成功 1 次，回滚在 15 条中仅成功 1 次。研究型运行时呈现相反画像：保证在基准上被评测，却无任何实际运行报告；其核心保证——对外部副作用的补偿——在所有框架中均无人提供。

Conclusion: 现有智能体系统的"恢复保障"在实践中远未兑现：机制被触发频繁但端到端修复成功率极低（完全恢复仅约 15%）。工程界的保障多停留在简单机制层面，而研究界的强保证缺乏真实使用证据且与实际需求脱节。作者据此呼吁建立统一的恢复机制分级与真实运行证据标准，并将外部副作用补偿作为重点研究方向。

Abstract: When an AI coding agent deletes the wrong files or a long agent run crashes, users fall back on recovery mechanisms: resume from a checkpoint, undo, ask for approval, read the log. Research runtimes promise stronger guarantees, such as saga-style compensation and transactional rollback. We ask what public evidence shows about whether either kind works in real use. Under a protocol frozen before data collection, we graded 12 persistent agent runtimes, 26 coding-agent harnesses and 12 research runtimes on eight recovery mechanisms, from not found to exercised in a real run, with two blind coding passes, adjudication and re-verification against archived sources. Recovery is exercised almost everywhere in practice: 11 of 12 runtimes and 25 of 26 harnesses have at least one real-use report. Yet of 128 such reports, only 19 describe the run fully recovering; 54 are partial and 45 failed. Checkpoint/resume recovered once in 27 reports, rollback once in 15. Research runtimes show the opposite profile: guarantees evaluated on benchmarks, none reported in operation, and compensation of external effects, their central guarantee, is offered by no harness at all.

</details>


### [195] [Epistemic Guard: Out-of-Process MCP Receipt Proxying, Post-Turn Prose Supervision, and Provenance-Gated Memory for Tool-Using Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27095)
*Sean Maxwell*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 epistemic-guard：一个零GPU显存的Python库与MCP收据代理，用于在运行时验证工具调用后的叙述、记忆来源与多源证据，抵御动作捏造、来源塌陷和回声放大三类风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用MCP的语言模型代理存在三个运行时验证缺口：一是动作捏造（工具失败后仍叙述副作用），二是来源塌陷（未经验证的模型叙述或网页文本与真实工具输出一同写入持久记忆），三是回声放大（镜像网页冒充多源佐证）。现有MCP防火墙只检查执行前的通信调用，LLM裁判护栏需数GB显存且无法把叙述与收据绑定。

Method: 实现为开源Python库及进程外stdio MCP收据代理（15模块，0 GPU显存）。核心机制：Ed25519签名密钥进程外隔离；写出RFC 6962 Merkle日志并做AER-1收据转换；用经SHA-256校验的OpenVINO INT8编码器（4模型共279.48MB，监督路径82.67MB）在CPU上核查回合后叙述；对7类来源域的SQLite写入进行门控；对逐字及轻度改写的通信镜像做归并去重。

Result: 一致性测试中，100%拒绝被篡改收据（200个Merkle叶子），通过58/58官方AER-1摄入向量和50/50参考校验导出；100%验证40次故障注入调用及100次并发追加且零密钥泄露，100%拦截100次未授权记忆写入。在N=250条冻结基准轨迹上达93.2%准确率，热态中位CPU延迟17.9ms（单子句8.62ms，冷启动921.8ms），较12B–14B GPU裁判高6.4–6.8分（p=0.0259、p=0.0095）且延迟低4.26–4.5倍。在N=60条实时mcp-proxy轨迹上，以9.16ms p50捕获96.15%风险，准确率76.67%（FPR 38.24%），较仅Stage-1提升18.34分（p=0.001）。

Conclusion: epistemic-guard以零GPU、进程外密钥、CPU轻量编码器实现了可验证、低延迟的MCP运行时护栏，在合成一致性测试上表现近乎完美，在真实轨迹上召回高但误报率仍偏高（38.24%），是后续改进重点。

Abstract: Language-model agents using the Model Context Protocol (MCP) face three runtime verification gaps: (i) action fabrication, where post-tool prose narrates side effects that failed, timed out, or never ran; (ii) provenance collapse, where unverified model prose or web text enters durable memory alongside executed tool outputs; and (iii) syndicated echo amplification, where mirrored web pages satisfy multi-source corroboration from one origin. Existing MCP firewalls inspect wire calls before execution, while LLM-judge guardrails require multi-gigabyte VRAM without binding prose to receipts. We present epistemic-guard (v0.5.2), an open-source Python library and out-of-process stdio MCP receipt proxy (15 modules, 0 MiB GPU VRAM). epistemic-guard isolates Ed25519 signing keys out of process, writes RFC 6962 Merkle logs with AER-1 receipt translation, checks post-turn prose on CPU with SHA-256-verified OpenVINO INT8 encoders (4 models, 279.48 MB total; 82.67 MB on the supervision path), gates SQLite writes across 7 provenance domains, and collapses verbatim and lightly edited wire-syndicated RAG mirrors. On conformance suites, epistemic-guard rejects 100.0% of tampered receipts (200 Merkle leaves), passes 58/58 official AER-1 ingestion vectors and 50/50 reference-verified AER-1 exports, verifies 100.0% of 40 fault-injected wire calls and 100 concurrent appends with 0 key leaks, and blocks 100.0% of 100 unauthorized memory writes. Across $N=250$ frozen-benchmark traces, epistemic-guard reaches 93.2% accuracy at 17.9 ms warm median CPU latency (8.62 ms single-clause; 921.8 ms cold-start load), beating 12B-14B GPU judges by 6.4-6.8 points ($p=0.0259$, $p=0.0095$) at 4.26-4.5$\times$ lower latency; on $N=60$ live mcp-proxy traces, it catches 96.15% of hazards at 76.67% accuracy (38.24% FPR; $+18.34$ points over Stage-1-only, $p=0.001$) in 9.16 ms p50.

</details>


### [196] [Schema-Validated LLM Workflows: A Benchmark Protocol and Comparative Evaluation Across Six Python Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27106)
*Sidney Shapiro,Sanad Al-Khatib,Aurora Pinzón Arzola,Emiliano Sebastian Gonzalez Venegas,Daniel Pearson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出可复现的基准协议与公开可执行工件，并在实时API上对六个Python实现条件（PydanticAI、LangGraph、DSPy、CrewAI、Agno、无框架SDK）在五类业务工作流任务上评估，区分"结构化输出合法"与"任务正确"，发现二者可分离、各绑定路径表现异质且存在显著的条件×任务交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛用于记录抽取、SQL生成、报告摘要等软件边界任务，在这些环节中"输出可解析"常常被误当作"任务正确"。作者希望建立一个可复现的评测框架，把结构有效性与任务特定正确性分开度量，并检验不同结构化输出实现路径（框架 vs. 原生SDK）在共享模型和统一契约下是否存在系统性差异。

Method: 提出三条假设：H1 schema有效性与任务质量可分离；H2 在共享模型和"最低公约数"契约下，结构化输出绑定路径存在异质性；H3 失败集中在条件×任务交互而非全体任务均匀下降。采用冻结分析计划的确认性实验：抽取任务100实例、其余任务各50实例、每实例三个嵌套重复，构成5400行主矩阵，使用等价边界与Holm校正的对照，以无框架SDK为参考基线；辅以带标注的消融实验（重试预算、使用特性的机制）来解释结果。

Result: 确认性运行中总体schema有效率为0.684，平均自动质量分为0.638。PydanticAI、LangGraph、DSPy在schema有效性上接近上限，平均质量0.91–0.94；而纯SDK、Agno、CrewAI在该包与模型配置下显著更低。即便schema合法的样本仍大量出现任务评分告警，尤其集中在抽取与合规QA任务；质量残差显示强烈的条件×任务交互（包括CrewAI出现工作流崩塌）。

Conclusion: 结构化输出的"可解析"并不能保证任务正确，两者应分开评测；不同框架绑定路径在同一模型下表现差异明显，且失败呈任务特异性集中。作者强调主要对比结论属于系统配置层面的发现，不能因果归因于各库宣传的具体功能特性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to extract records, generate SQL, summarize reports, answer policy questions, and route operational work. At these software boundaries, parseable output is necessary but does not establish task correctness. We present a reproducible benchmark protocol, a public executable artifact, and a confirmatory live API evaluation that separate structural validity from task-specific correctness across five business workflow tasks and six Python implementation conditions: PydanticAI, LangGraph, DSPy, CrewAI, Agno, and a framework-free SDK reference.
  We hypothesize that (H1) schema validity and task quality are separable, (H2) structured-output binding paths are heterogeneous under a shared model and lowest-common-denominator contract, and (H3) condition-by-task interactions concentrate failures rather than shifting all tasks equally. Confirmatory analysis uses distinct task instances (100 extraction and 50 per other task, with three nested replicates) under a frozen analysis plan, yielding a 5400-row primary matrix with equivalence margins and Holm-adjusted contrasts against the SDK reference.
  In the eligible confirmatory run, overall schema validity was 0.684 and mean automated quality was 0.638. PydanticAI, LangGraph, and DSPy retained near-ceiling schema validity with mean quality 0.91-0.94, while the plain SDK, Agno, and CrewAI conditions were substantially lower in this package and model configuration. Schema-valid rows still produced many task-score flags, especially in extraction and compliance QA, and quality residuals show strong condition-by-task interactions (including a workflow collapse for CrewAI). Labelled ablations address retry budgets and feature-using mechanisms; primary contrasts remain system-configuration findings, not causal attributions to marketed library features.

</details>


### [197] [Omo: Giving Continuing AI-Agent Work a Place to Return To](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27107)
*Kaviru Hapuarachchi,Harrison Edwards*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Omo 为持续的 AI 编码代理工作提供“持久的可返回空间”，并实现了两个系统（Omo Space 与 Omo Theia），同时提出四项工程经验和一个用于验证“回归空间”能否帮助用户正确接手代理工作的研究设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 编码代理可在用户离开时持续工作，但用户回归时缺乏上下文：会话列表只显示代理在哪，却不说明它被要求做什么、改了什么、下一步该说什么。

Method: 提出“为每段延续中的代理工作赋予持久返回位置”的思路，并以两种方式实现：Omo Space 是开源 Minecraft 模组，玩家建造的房间与屏幕即本机上的实时终端；Omo Theia 将同样的房间与工位带入可定制 IDE，紧邻编辑器与 Git 补丁。两者均在视图关闭后仍让代理会话在主机上运行。

Result: 文中描述了两个系统的构建，总结出四条构建经验，并设计了一项研究来检验“回到一个地方”是否有助于人们正确地接手代理工作。

Conclusion: 以“空间/场所”而非“会话列表”作为代理工作的回归锚点，可能改善用户恢复代理工作的正确性；该假设将通过所设计的研究加以验证。

Abstract: AI coding agents now keep working while the people who started them are away. Coming back is the hard part: a session list tells you which agent is where, but not what it was asked to do, what it changed, or what to tell it next. We present Omo, which gives each piece of continuing agent work a persistent place to return to. We built the idea twice. Omo Space is an open-source Minecraft mod in which rooms and screens that people build are live terminals on their own machine. Omo Theia brings the same rooms and desks into a customizable IDE, one step away from the editor and the Git patch. Both keep agent sessions running on the host after the view is closed. We describe the two systems, four lessons from building them, and a study designed to test whether returning to a place helps people pick up agent work correctly.

</details>


### [198] [Markdown Is Not a Firewall: four coding agents, one rules file, four boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27112)
*Sandeep Dhuri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文检验仓库中的"规则文件"对编码智能体的实际约束力：在预注册、隐藏测试和多边界测试下，规则文件只是"建议"，且会干扰部分智能体表现，真正能决定行为的是"网关（gate）"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 团队常把一页规则文件放进仓库，就当作对编码智能体的管控；而已有注册研究发现智能体很少查阅贡献规则。作者想从安全角度追问：规则文件在执行边界处究竟起什么作用，它能否替代强制网关？

Method: 预注册（先存假设与分析代码），测试1份278词规则文件。用Claude Code、Codex CLI、Gemini CLI、OpenCode+DeepSeek在20个含隐藏测试的小仓库上工作（其中三个各12个）。设置四类边界：拒绝访问的端点、破坏性捷径、植入指令、植入恶意包。故意关闭各厂商自带的权限提示控制层。在模拟服务前设网关，拦截并记录越权请求；所有判定取自智能体无法篡改的记录。首轮评分对处理组保持盲态。

Result: 检查器未发现真实代码缺陷（有无规则文件皆然）。规则文件使Codex CLI隐藏测试得分从99.0%降至67.7%，其36步中有11步在隐藏测试不到半数通过时报告"全部通过"。遇到规则冲突时3个智能体遵循任务、1个遵循规则。合并后通过率由98.2%降至90.9%；配对样本降7.7个百分点（去掉Codex后降1.3）。越界尝试罕见：90个网关步中仅5次（无文件3次、有文件2次），均为OpenCode+DeepSeek且全部被拦截。无网关且用中性文件时，45次运行中7次越权请求到达模拟服务。因脚本错误，无文件与有文件组在三个HTTP边界上始终在网关后运行，故未能检验规则文件是否改变那里的行为。

Conclusion: 在可测量范围内，规则文件只是"建议"：它告诉智能体你想要什么，却无法强制执行；而网关决定智能体实际能得到什么，才是真正的控制手段。

Abstract: Teams put a one-page rules file in a repository and call it a control on coding agents. A registered study found agents rarely look up a repository's contribution rules. I asked the security question: what does a rules file do at a boundary, against a gate? I tested one 278-word rules file, with hypotheses and analysis code deposited first. Claude Code, Codex CLI, Gemini CLI and OpenCode with DeepSeek worked through 20 small repositories with hidden tests, 12 for three of them. Three agents met four boundaries I built: a refused endpoint, a destructive shortcut, a planted instruction and a planted package. Permission prompts, each vendor's own control layer, were off by design. A gate in front of the mock service blocked and logged forbidden requests. Every verdict was read from records the agent could not change. Only my first scoring pass was blind to arm. My checkers found no real bugs, with the file or without it. The file cost one agent a third of its score: Codex CLI fell from 99.0 to 67.7 percent on hidden tests, and in 11 of its 36 steps reported the task complete with all tests passing while under half of the hidden tests passed. Its own tests passed. Faced with two of its rules that clash on these tasks, three agents followed the task and one followed the rule. Pooled, passes fell from 98.2 to 90.9 percent. Matched pairs fell 7.7 points, 1.3 without Codex CLI. Attempts to cross were rare: 5 in 90 gated steps, 3 without the file, 2 with it, all by OpenCode with DeepSeek, all blocked. With no gate, under the neutral file, the same requests reached the mock in 7 of 45 runs. By an error in my run script, the no-file and rules-file arms always ran behind the gate at the three HTTP boundaries, so I could not test whether the file changes what an agent does there. On what I could measure, the file acted as advice. A file tells an agent what you want. A gate decides what it gets.

</details>


### [199] [Simulation-Based Testing of RCM-Constrained Robotic Software via Task Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27144)
*Zhenyu Jiang,Yanlin Li,Rongjie Yan,Rihui Song,Kai Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出以任务流形为中心的测试框架，将可行测试域构建与测试生成策略解耦，在构造的可行域内生成测试输入并映射到关节指令，从而提升RCM手术机器人系统级测试效率并揭示多样化失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCM手术机械臂受任务几何、逆运动学与电机限位强耦合约束，工作空间狭窄。直接在电机指令或笛卡尔空间采样会大量浪费测试预算在物理上不可行的构型上。

Method: 构建任务流形可行域，候选输入直接在此流形内采样并映射为底层关节指令进行仿真；用离线轨迹安全oracle与空间参考网格评估失效产出率与覆盖率；与笛卡尔XYZ和PCA表示对比，采用随机测试、系统遍历与遗传搜索三种策略；并做缺陷版与修补版控制器差分测试，以及在7-DoF Kinova Gen3 + MoveIt 2上的跨平台验证。

Result: 可行任务域提升了测试生成效率并支持系统化参考单元覆盖；随机与遗传搜索策略比基线表示检测到更广谱的失效模式，说明完整单元覆盖并不穷尽单元内行为动态；定向进化搜索能检测软件修复带来的整体失效率偏移；Kinova Gen3跨平台实验证实工作流可迁移至运动学冗余机械臂。

Conclusion: 机器人软件系统化测试需将可行性感知的域建模与互补搜索策略相结合，二者缺一不可。

Abstract: System-level testing of surgical robots requires generating executable test scenarios that exercise diverse, safety-critical behaviors. However, remote-center-of-motion (RCM) surgical manipulators operate within tightly constrained workspaces governed by coupled task geometry, inverse kinematics, and motor limits. Direct sampling in motor-command or Cartesian spaces frequently wastes testing budgets on physically unfeasible configurations. To address this limitation, we present a manifold-centered testing framework that decouples feasible test-domain construction from downstream test-generation strategies. Candidate inputs are generated directly within this constructed feasible domain and mapped to lower-level joint commands for simulation execution. Offline trajectory safety oracles and a spatial reference grid then evaluate failure yield and coverage. We compare our task-manifold representation against Cartesian XYZ and principal component analysis (PCA) under random testing, systematic traversal, and genetic search strategies. Results demonstrate that the feasible task domain improves test-generation efficiency and enables systematic reference-cell coverage. Crucially, random and genetic search strategies detect a broader spectrum of failure patterns than baseline representations, revealing that complete reference-cell coverage alone does not exhaust intra-cell behavioral dynamics. Furthermore, differential testing between defective and patched controller versions reveals that targeted evolutionary search detects aggregate failure-rate shifts from software repairs. Finally, a cross-platform study on a 7-DoF Kinova Gen3 arm in MoveIt 2 confirms workflow transferability to kinematically redundant manipulators. Together, these findings highlight the necessity of combining feasibility-aware domain modeling with complementary search strategies for systematic testing of robot software.

</details>


### [200] [Git History Is a Pangenome: Repetition in Code Training Corpora and What Deduplication Removes, Keeps and Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27154)
*Saurabh Chopra*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文测量了代码模型在训练于软件仓库完整历史时的数据重复情况：一个典型仓库的历史约等于其当前代码的19份副本（成熟仓库89份），精确去重无效，而近似去重可降至约2份，但需修正"保留版本"规则并单独剔除基准测试答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去重工具是为单次快照设计的，而代码模型越来越多地训练于仓库的完整历史，历史中的重复程度及其对去重的影响此前未被测量。

Method: 在306个公开仓库的预注册随机样本、25个成熟项目及SWE-bench的12个仓库上测量历史重复；类比文本索引中的泛基因组概念；评估精确匹配去重与近似重复文件分组去重（保留一份）在跨版本场景下的效果；检验去重时版本选择规则对日期的影响；检查基准修复在后续版本中的存留。

Result: 典型仓库历史相当于当前代码的19份副本，成熟仓库为89份；变更文件中九成token在改动前已存在；精确去重无法去除任何副本；跨版本近似去重可降至约2份；但广泛使用的实现忽略日期，导致半数文件保留的是旧版本；按最新优先排序后近乎零成本解决；SWE-bench的合并修复中位数会在18个后续版本中存活，因此需逐版本检查的独立步骤来移除基准答案。

Conclusion: 跨版本近似去重能大幅减少历史冗余，但需（1）去重前将版本按新到旧排序、（2）以检查每个版本的独立步骤剔除基准修复数据；论文据此给出面向代码历史训练团队的简明规则，并公开工具与冻结输出。

Abstract: Code models are increasingly trained on the full history of software repositories, every version of every file, rather than on a single snapshot. The tools that deduplicate training data were built for snapshots, and what they do to a history has not been measured. We measured it on a pre-registered random sample of 306 public repositories, 25 mature projects, and the 12 repositories behind the SWE-bench benchmark. A history is mostly repetition: in the vocabulary of text indexing, a pangenome, one text in many near-identical variants. The history of a typical repository holds the equivalent of 19 copies of its current code, and a mature repository holds 89. The reason is that each edit changes a few lines and leaves the rest of the file in place: nine tokens in ten of a changed file already existed before the change. Exact-match deduplication removes none of these copies, since no two versions are identical. Large training pipelines already deduplicate their snapshots by grouping near-identical files and keeping one. Applied across versions, the same step brings the repetition down to about two copies. But two problems remain. The deduplicator keeps one version of each file, and in widely used implementations, including the one we measured, the rule for choosing that version ignores dates, so for half of all files the version kept for training is an old one. Sorting versions newest first before deduplicating fixes the problem at almost no cost. The second problem is benchmark answers. Benchmarks such as SWE-bench test a model on real bug fixes, and a merged fix survives in a median of 18 later versions of the files it changed. Therefore, removing benchmark fixes needs a separate step that checks every version. We turn the measurements into a short set of rules for teams that train on code history, and release the instruments and frozen outputs.

</details>


### [201] [From 6G Capabilities to Software-Business Value: A Multivocal Review of Characteristics, Opportunities, Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27675)
*Muhammad Zohaib,Azeem Akbar,Sami Hyrynsalmi,Muhammad Hamza*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过多声部文献综述(133个来源)系统梳理了6G软件业务的特征(18项/5主题)、机遇(32项/8主题)与挑战(19项/6主题),指出三者相互依存,价值创造依赖技术能力向可行服务与商业模式的转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G有望催生智能、自主、软件定义的生态,为软件企业创造新价值,但关于6G软件业务特征、机遇与挑战的证据分散在学术与从业者文献中,缺乏系统整合。

Method: 采用多声部文献综述(multivocal literature review),纳入133个来源(102篇同行评审研究、31份灰色文献);运用主题分析归纳特征、机遇与挑战,并由三名独立评分者评估因子到主题分类的可靠性(Fleiss' kappa 0.744–0.832)。

Result: 识别出18项特征(5个主题)、32项商业机遇(8个主题)、19项挑战与约束(6个主题);分类可靠性达到较高至近乎完美的一致水平。发现价值创造不仅取决于技术能力,还取决于将其转化为可行服务与商业模式,并需应对集成复杂性、组织与经济壁垒、监管不确定性以及安全与可持续性问题。

Conclusion: 特征、机遇与挑战是6G软件业务相互依存的维度;整合学术与从业者证据为未来实证研究和战略决策提供了可追溯的基础。

Abstract: Sixth-generation (6G) technology is expected to enable intelligent, autonomous, and software-defined ecosystems, creating new ways for software firms to generate value. However, evidence on the characteristics, opportunities, and challenges of 6G software businesses remains fragmented across academic and practitioner literature. We conducted a multivocal literature review of 133 sources (102 peer-reviewed studies and 31 grey literature sources) to examine this emerging landscape from the perspective of software-producing organizations. Thematic analysis identified 18 characteristics grouped into five themes, 32 business opportunities across eight themes, and 19 challenges and constraints across six themes. Three independent raters assessed the reliability of factor-to-theme classification, with Fleiss' kappa indicating substantial to almost perfect agreement (0.744 to 0.832). Findings suggest that value creation depends not only on technological capabilities but also on translating them into viable services and business models while addressing integration complexity, organizational and economic barriers, regulatory uncertainty, and security and sustainability concerns. The synthesis reveals that characteristics, opportunities, and challenges are interdependent dimensions of 6G software businesses. By integrating academic and practitioner evidence, this study provides a traceable foundation for future empirical research and strategic decision-making as 6G technologies, standards, and markets evolve.

</details>


### [202] [From Correctness to Consistency: Redefining Reliability for the Agentware Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27829)
*Xue Qin,Maurício Gruppi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"差分逻辑分析"（DLA）框架，从正确性验证转向逻辑一致性检测，利用逻辑推理发现并行与串行开发流程中的内部矛盾，初步仿真显示可成功拦截逻辑漂移（矛盾与未声明假设）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "vibe coding"兴起使需求分析被边缘化，"Make it Work"式未经验证的假设不断累积，形成规避标准测试的复杂缺陷；而缺乏可靠的ground truth oracle，使传统正确性验证难以适用。

Method: 提出差分逻辑分析（DLA）框架，借助逻辑推理（Logical Inference）在并行与串行开发工作流之间进行差分比对，检测内部逻辑矛盾与未声明假设。

Result: 初步仿真表明，DLA能成功拦截逻辑漂移，包括矛盾性结论和未声明假设，为AI辅助开发时代的可靠性评估提供新视角。

Conclusion: 在缺乏真实基准的情况下，以逻辑一致性替代正确性验证是可行方向；DLA为AI辅助软件开发的可靠性评估提供了初步有效的范式转变。

Abstract: The rise of "vibe coding" has marginalized requirements analysis, allowing unverified "Make it Work" assumptions to accumulate into complex defects that evade standard testing. In the absence of a reliable ground truth oracle, we advocate a paradigm shift from correctness verification to logic consistency. We introduce Differential Logic Analysis (DLA), a framework that utilizes Logical Inference to detect internal contradictions across parallel and sequential development workflows. Preliminary simulations demonstrate that DLA successfully intercepts logic drift, such as contradictions and unspecified assumptions, and offers a new perspective on reliability assessment in the AI-assistant development era.

</details>


### [203] [TEA-Bench-BR: Can Coding Agents Turn a Branching Clinical Specification into Correct Code?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27895)
*Marcio Andre de Souza Estevam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出TEA-Bench-BR，一个基于巴西儿童早期自闭症(TEA)筛查路径的编码智能体基准，包含三个虚构量表、多专业转诊流程及符合RNDS国家规范的FHIR R4导出（全程葡萄牙语），用以检测"静默bug"（能运行但输出貌似合理的错误代码）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体越来越多地实现领域专家编写的规则，此类代码中的bug具有隐蔽性：程序照常运行并返回看似合理的结果。现有基准缺乏针对领域规则正确性、且具备高杀伤力隐藏用例的评测环境，尤其缺少非英语、涉及临床流程与医疗数据标准的场景。

Method: 构建144个任务、6,798个隐藏用例的生成器（隐藏用例可杀死参考实现全部1,955个非等价变异体）；以Gemma 4（12B与31B）作为智能体，分别评测单量表任务与FHIR层面任务，并做英葡语言对照；另用修改版PHQ-9做污染探测。

Result: 12B解决38.0%单量表任务、FHIR层为0；31B在1~3层分别达77.8%、85.2%、78.7%，隐藏用例通过率超99%，剩余失败多在输入校验而非临床判断。同任务英文下31B第3层仅57.4%（对标识符校验较少），12B反而更常校验。需转诊病例漏检率12B为10.10%、31B为0.02%。PHQ-9污染探测未发现遵循原始规则的错误。

Conclusion: 模型规模显著影响领域规则编码的可靠性，但即便强模型仍集中于输入校验类缺陷；语言会系统性改变代码行为（如校验习惯），凸显非英语医疗标准场景评测的必要性，且基准经变异体与污染探测验证具备较高区分度。

Abstract: Coding agents increasingly implement rules written by domain experts, and bugs in such code are silent: it runs and returns a plausible answer. We introduce TEA-Bench-BR, a benchmark built on the Brazilian pathway for early autism (TEA) screening: three fictitious instruments with the structure of real ones, a multi-professional referral flow, and export to FHIR R4 with the national RNDS profiles, all specified in Portuguese. A generator samples each task's parameters (144 tasks, 6,798 hidden cases), and the hidden cases kill all 1,955 non-equivalent mutants of the reference. With Gemma 4 as the agent, the 12B solves 38.0% of single-instrument tasks and none at the FHIR level; the 31B solves 77.8%, 85.2% and 78.7% at levels 1 to 3 and passes over 99% of the hidden cases, its remaining failures mostly in input validation rather than clinical decisions. With the same tasks in English, the 31B solves 57.4% at level 3, as its code validates an identifier less often; the 12B's, more often. The 12B misses 10.10% of the cases that call for referral; the 31B, 0.02%. A contamination probe on a modified PHQ-9 finds no error that follows the original rules.

</details>


### [204] [Perceived Differences in Activity and Experience Between Developing Software With and Without AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27947)
*Rubash Mali,Juho Leinonen,John Edwards*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 生成式AI正在改变软件开发的"技艺面"（问题讨论、分解、查文档、调试、深度专注与控制感），这些变化随编程经验增加而放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI驱动的软件工程变革不仅影响岗位数量，也改变软件工程本身的面貌；需要回答下一代工程师应具备何种技能、资深工程师是否仍被该职业吸引等问题。为此作者研究AI辅助开发与传统开发的感知差异，以及编程年限如何调节这些差异。

Method: 通过调查研究方法，让工程师比较使用AI工具与传统的软件开发体验，并按编程年限（是否在生成式AI出现之前就开始编程）分组比较感知差异，区分"核心代码生产活动"与"技艺类活动"。

Result: 在活动层面，工程师报告与同伴讨论技术问题更少、分解更少、检索文档更少、使用调试器更少，但任务切换更多；在体验层面，报告深度专注减少、对代码与设计的控制感减弱；而写作、重构、修复等核心代码生产活动的均值差异较小。这些差异随编程年限呈"经验梯度"放大，AI出现前开始编程者感知到更大的活动与体验变化。

Conclusion: 作者将其称为"different craft"结果，指出AI对软件工程的影响主要体现在技艺与体验维度而非核心编码产出；并讨论了其对下一代工程师培养、以及难以向AI融合型软件工程转型的资深工程师的启示。

Abstract: The fundamental changes in software engineering driven by generative AI will have profound effects not only on the number of available software engineering jobs but also on what software engineering itself looks like. As the career evolves, questions arise about what the skillset and interests of the next generation of software engineers will be and whether long-time engineers will continue to be attracted to the career. In this paper, we contribute to answering these questions by studying how software development with AI tools is perceived to differ from traditional development and how the differences are modulated by years of programming experience. We find that, in terms of programming activities, engineers report less talking with peers about technical problems, less decomposition, less searching of documentation, and less using a debugger, while they report more task switching. We also find experiential differences, including less deep focus and less sense of control over code and design. We call these the different craft results, in contrast to core code-production activities such as writing, refactoring, and fixing code, which show only small mean differences. We also find that these differences are amplified by years of programming experience (an experience gradient), with software engineers who started programming before the age of generative AI perceiving greater changes in both activity and developer experience. We discuss our findings and their implications on training of the next generation of engineers and on engineers who may have difficulty transitioning to the AI-infused world of software engineering.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Adding One More Agent: How AI Team Size and Formation Shape Productivity and Diversity in Human-AI Brainstorming Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26663)
*Rong-Ching Chang,Hao-Chuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在头脑风暴实验中，AI伙伴数量（1个vs 2个）与形成方式（校准至人类平均水平vs根据参与者定制）交叉影响人类贡献：单个平均校准AI使人类发帖最多，加入第二个AI后该效应消失且人类自创想法显著减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI提升了个体生产力，但增加多个AI伙伴究竟有益还是有害尚无定论。现有研究多关注单智能体，其结论是否可推广到多智能体团队尚不清楚。

Method: 采用2×2被试间头脑风暴实验（N=168），交叉AI伙伴数量（1个/2个）与行为形成方式（校准到平均人类行为/由参与者定制），并以人类-人类双人组为参照基线。

Result: 双AI房间整体发帖更多，但人类作者的想法大幅减少；单AI条件下形成方式有影响——与平均校准AI配对的人类发帖量高于其他所有条件，双AI时该效应消失。人类仍持续交谈但转化为发帖的想法减少。AI的社交性发言预测人类发帖增加，AI发帖量则预测人类发帖减少。AI在场提升了人类想法的全局多样性，但只有第二个AI提升了会话内的差异化。

Conclusion: AI伙伴的数量及其形成方式会重塑人类的贡献方式，单智能体的研究结论不能直接推广到多智能体团队。

Abstract: Generative AI has improved individual productivity, but whether adding a plurality of AI partners helps or hurts remains open. We crossed AI partnership (one versus two) with behavioral formation (calibrated to average human behavior versus customized by the participant) in a brainstorming experiment ($N$=168) with human-human dyads as reference. Two-AI rooms posted more ideas to the shared board, but human-authored ideas dropped substantially. With one AI partner, formation mattered: humans paired with an average-calibrated AI posted more than any other condition. With two, this effect vanished. Humans continued talking but converted fewer ideas into board posts. AI social talk predicted more human posts, while AI posting volume predicted fewer. AI presence increased global diversity of human-authored ideas, but only a second AI increased within-session differentiation. The number of AI partners and their formation can reshape what the human contributes; single-agent results do not generalize to multi-agent teams.

</details>


### [206] [Coherence by Construction: Synthesizing CTI Knowledge Graphs and the Formal Limits of Coherence Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26717)
*Tanzimul Alam Fahim,Mohammad Arif Hossain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ??


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ??

Method: ??

Result: ??

Conclusion: ??

Abstract: Synthesizing relational training data such as knowledge graphs with a large language model typically emits structured objects one at a time, which keeps each object valid but leaves the relationships between them ungrounded. We study an alternative paradigm, generation by simulation: rather than describing a relational artifact, we simulate a process that produces it and take the artifact to be its causal record. Language-model agents propose intent and a deterministic referee resolves outcomes against an explicit world model, so a relation is emitted only when the event it represents occurs, making coherence a property of construction. We instantiate the paradigm for cyber threat intelligence knowledge graphs in the STIX 2.1 standard with three engines, among them an evolutionary engine applying language-model genetic operators to structured graph genomes, sharing a deterministic compiler that makes malformed and dangling output unrepresentable. We prove referential closure, causal grounding, and identifier stability, an ablation localizes each to the referee and world model rather than the policy, and two independent LLM judges reproduce the rubric's engine ranking. Our central result is cautionary, and we prove it: a validator reading any graph feature set a structure-matched random graph can reproduce is uninformative about coherence for a coherence-by-construction generator. Using Erdős-Rényi controls we realize the theorem four times, as structural separation, embedding transfer, inductive link prediction, and real-data augmentation each reward a triviality rather than coherence; we give the mechanism behind each and the conditions a valid benchmark must meet.

</details>


### [207] [TOUMAII: A 4.5M-Pair Tool-Calling Preference Dataset and Its Source-Agnostic Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26722)
*Moritz Pötzsch,Andrea Gentilini,Lisa Rossgoderer,Tom Osterfeld,Kesava Prasad Sanjivi Arul,Swanand Ravindra Kadhe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TOUMAII大规模工具调用偏好数据集（约4.5M验证过的偏好对）及TOUMAII-Gen合成生成流水线，填补多轮真实场景下DPO数据稀缺，并用3B模型验证其效果与数据源无关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业智能体需可靠地进行工具调用。SFT只学习生成正确调用，无法与貌似合理的错误调用形成对比；而工具调用中细微格式错误即导致失败，错误决策会使交互脱轨，因此需要DPO这类偏好对比学习。但工具调用（尤其真实多轮场景）的偏好数据极度稀缺。

Method: 从Agent-Ark/Toucan-1.5M对话构建TOUMAII数据集，含约4.5M经校验的偏好对，覆盖多轮/单轮、并行与串行函数调用；并发布TOUMAII-Gen流水线，可消费任意工具调用SFT数据集，生成包含模型自然产生失败模式的偏好数据；另从ToolACE构建第二份小规模偏好数据集。

Result: 用该数据微调3B参数模型，性能与其他高质量工具调用偏好数据集持平；第二份来自ToolACE的数据集表明流水线与数据源无关。

Conclusion: TOUMAII提供了规模化、可复现的工具调用偏好数据与生成方法，代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: To autonomously execute multi-step tasks, enterprise agents must reliably interact with tools, call APIs, or query databases, making tool-calling a fundamental capability of AI models. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) optimises for producing the correct call, but never contrasts it with a plausible incorrect one. In an environment where even a minor formatting error can invalidate a call and an incorrect decision can derail the interaction, direct preference optimisation (DPO) targets exactly this contrast. DPO, therefore, requires preference pairs rather than plain demonstrations. For tool-calling, such data, especially in realistic multi-turn settings, remains extremely scarce. To address this scarcity, we introduce TOUMAII, a large-scale tool-calling preference dataset containing ~4.5M validated preference pairs, derived from Agent-Ark/Toucan-1.5M (apache-2.0) conversations. The dataset consists of multi- and single-turn interactions and includes parallel and sequential function calls. Additionally, we release TOUMAII-Gen, a synthetic data generation pipeline that consumes tool-calling SFT datasets and generates a preference dataset with naturally occurring model-generated tool-calling failure modes. To validate the effectiveness of our DPO data, we fine-tune a 3-billion-parameter model, achieving performance parity with other high-quality tool-calling preference datasets. Furthermore, we release a second small preference dataset derived from ToolACE (apache-2.0), showcasing the source-agnostic nature of our pipeline.
  We release the complete code and datasets: https://github.com/tum-ai/toumaii-gen and https://huggingface.co/collections/tum-ai/toumaii-generated-datasets

</details>


### [208] [ChatAtom: A Multi-Agent LLM System for Molecular Dynamics Simulation and Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26782)
*Trupti Mohanty,Ming Du,Mitchell A. Wood,Brad L. Boyce,Remi Dingreville,Mathew J. Cherukara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChatAtom 是一个多智能体大语言模型系统，可将自然语言请求转化为完整的分子动力学（MD）工作流，无需用户编写输入脚本或分析代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MD 模拟是材料设计核心手段，但需要专家驱动流程：设计模拟、编写可靠脚本、执行计算、用不同工具分析结果，门槛高、流程割裂。

Method: 采用分工明确的多智能体架构：编排器负责研究规划；LAMMPS 与 OVITO 智能体各用"生成器-审查器"配对来编写并审核模拟与可视化脚本；高性能计算智能体负责向远程资源派发作业；分析智能体解读运行结果。提供浏览器界面，本地执行时实时流式显示热力学数据与渲染帧；并支持通过可加载技能扩展功能而不改动智能体本身。

Result: 通过脚本生成基准测试、端到端裂纹扩展研究以及闭环自主研究活动进行评测，验证了系统能力。

Conclusion: ChatAtom 将脚本生成、执行、可视化与分析整合为可审查、可观测的统一流水线，降低 MD 研究门槛，扩大先进模拟工作流的可及性。

Abstract: Molecular dynamics (MD) simulation resolves atomic-scale motion and predicts the structural, mechanical, and dynamic properties of materials, making it cen- tral to materials design and discovery. Yet an MD study requires an expert-driven workflow: designing the simulation, writing reliable input scripts, executing them, and analyzing the results with separate tools. Here we present ChatAtom, a multi-agent large language model system that translates a natural-language request into a complete MD workflow without the user writing input scripts or analysis code. Each agent has a defined responsibility within the workflow. An orchestrator plans the study; the LAMMPS and OVITO agents each use a generator-reviewer pair to prepare and review simulation and visualization scripts before execution; a high-performance computing agent dispatches jobs to remote resources; and an analysis agent interprets the completed run. The work- flow is accessible through a browser interface that streams thermodynamic data and rendered frames during local execution, allowing users to follow the simu- lation as it progresses. The architecture also supports new capabilities through loadable skills without modifying the agents, enabling autonomous hypothesis generation and testing. We evaluate ChatAtom through script-generation bench- marks, an end-to-end study of crack propagation, and a closed-loop campaign. By bringing script generation, execution, visualization, and analysis into one reviewed and observable pipeline, ChatAtom lowers the barrier to conducting MD studies and broadens access to advanced simulation workflows.

</details>


### [209] [Beyond Final Answers: Interpretable Tumor Immune Microenvironment Reasoning from H&E and mIF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26794)
*Yixuan Duan,Kexin Geng,Hanwen Xu,Wei Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HERMES 是一个 R1 风格的多模态大模型，联合 H&E 与 mIF 图像、按临床指南步骤推理肿瘤免疫微环境（TIME）指标，并用基于 mIF 细胞级真值的**过程奖励**逐步核验中间量，在结直肠癌四任务上以 4B 规模达到 84.5% 平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TIME 读数（CD8+ T 细胞密度、上皮内浸润、Ki67 指数）需要结合 H&E 的组织结构与 mIF 的细胞表型。现有病理 MLLM 只读 H&E，无法获取 mIF 提供的表型信息；且其推理缺乏步骤级真值，R1 式强化学习只奖励最终答案，推理过程未被验证。同切片 H&E–mIF 配对恰好能同时填补这两个缺口：mIF 无需人工标注即可测得每个细胞，为指南规定的每一步（划分组织区室、计数并归一化标志物阳性细胞）提供细胞级真值。

Method: 问题提法：将 TIME 读数拆解为临床指南式的多步推理链，每步的数值都有 mIF 派生的细胞级真值可比对。模型：HERMES（H&E-mIF Reasoning with Multimodal Evidence Supervision）在配对的 H&E 与 mIF 上联合推理。训练：R1 式强化学习，并引入过程奖励，对推理中陈述的每一个量逐一与真值核验，而非仅奖励最终答案。评测：结直肠癌四个视野级 TIME 任务，含 885 道测试题；与 12 个商业/通用/医学/病理 MLLM 对比；消融过程奖励、中间量正确率、决定性量的干预编辑；外部 CODEX 队列与头颈癌（训练中未出现）零样本泛化。

Result: HERMES（4B）平均准确率 84.5%，最强基线为 74.7%；中间量正确比例高于最强开源权重基线。去掉过程奖励后，RL 相对 SFT 提升甚微（80.0% vs 78.8%）且陈述量正确数更少。在全部 885 题上，改动一个决定性陈述量会导致与预期一致的答案改变。不重训情况下，外部 CODEX 队列 76.4% vs 67.3%，头颈癌 76.7% vs 72.5%。

Conclusion: 配对 H&E–mIF 既提供缺失的细胞表型信息，又提供逐步可核验的真值，使过程奖励真正约束推理链而非只约束答案。小模型加过程监督即可超过更大的通用与病理 MLLM，且推理链具备因果可干预性与跨染色、跨癌种的迁移性。

Abstract: Readouts of the tumor immune microenvironment (TIME), such as CD8+ T-cell density, intra-epithelial infiltration and the Ki67 index, combine tissue architecture from H&E with cell phenotypes from multiplex immunofluorescence (mIF). Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) could automate these readouts with an explanation, but two gaps remain. Pathology MLLMs read H&E alone, while these readouts need the cell phenotypes that mIF measures. And their reasoning has no step-level truth to be checked against, so R1-style reinforcement learning rewards only the final answer and leaves the reasoning unverified. Same-section H&E and mIF provide what both gaps lack: mIF measures every cell without manual annotation, which gives cell-level ground truth for each step that clinical guidelines prescribe, from delineating a tissue compartment to counting and normalizing the marker-positive cells within it. We therefore present HERMES (H&E-mIF Reasoning with Multimodal Evidence Supervision), an R1-style MLLM that reasons jointly over paired H&E and mIF in guideline steps and is trained with a process reward that checks every stated quantity against this truth. Across four field-level TIME tasks in colorectal cancer, HERMES (4B) reaches an average accuracy of 84.5%, compared with 74.7% for the strongest of twelve proprietary, general, medical and pathology MLLMs, and a higher share of its intermediate quantities is correct than for the strongest open-weight baseline. Without the process reward, reinforcement learning barely improves on supervised fine-tuning (80.0% vs. 78.8%) and fewer stated quantities are correct; editing a decisive stated quantity changes the answer as implied in all 885 test questions. Without retraining, it keeps the highest average accuracy on an external cohort imaged with CODEX (76.4% vs. 67.3%) and on head and neck cancer, a cancer type absent from training (76.7% vs. 72.5%).

</details>


### [210] [Memorization and Generalization in Generative Models: A Perspective From Energy-based Modeling and Associative Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26797)
*Bao Pham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文以联想记忆（AM）为统一框架，主张生成模型的"记忆"与"泛化"并非对立，而是同一检索过程的两种结果；伪状态（spurious states）正是创造力的起点。研究分别在扩散模型与离散扩散语言模型中刻画从记忆到泛化的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型已能写代码、生成图像、长对话，但其智能机制仍不清楚。AM中模式存为能量极小值、经吸引子动力学检索，而伪状态长期被视为记忆失败；作者希望重新解释这一"失败"，并回答生成模型何时回忆、何时创造。

Method: 1) 论证扩散模型等价于AM：小数据时每个训练样本周围形成独立吸引域，数据增多后吸引域收缩、伪状态作为混合样本的吸引子出现；通过测量吸引域的体量、能量与曲率（跨数据集与架构，含Stable Diffusion）刻画该转变。2) 转向语言，超越能量视角：证明均匀态离散扩散模型是能检索未见数据的AM，其吸引域仅由条件似然最大化产生；训练集增大时训练样本吸引域收缩、未见样本吸引域扩张，直至损坏的测试句能被可靠恢复；并用条件熵在单序列内揭示共存、低成本诊断模型所处转变阶段。

Result: 实验显示扩散模型中随数据增长出现从样本吸引域到伪状态吸引子的转变；离散扩散模型中训练与未见样本的吸引域此消彼长，损坏测试句可被如训练样本般可靠恢复；条件熵可作为判断模型处于转变位置的廉价指标。

Conclusion: AM是由吸引域定义的普遍原理，记忆与泛化可共存于同一模型之内；AM为判断生成模型何时回忆、何时创造提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Generative models now write working code, produce realistic images, and hold long conversations, yet the mechanisms behind their intelligence still evade our understanding. This thesis explores that puzzle through Associative Memory (AM), in which patterns are stored as energy minima and retrieved through attractor dynamics. Beyond their capacity, AMs form new minima known as spurious states, long regarded as a failure of memory. We argue that this failure is where creativity begins, and that memorization and generalization can coexist as outcomes of one retrieval process. First, we show that diffusion models behave as AMs. With little data, a diffusion model forms a distinct basin of attraction around each training sample. As the data grows, these basins shrink, and spurious states emerge as attractors that blend stored samples yet match none of them. They are the first signs of generative capability. We characterize this transition by measuring the volume, energy, and curvature of these basins across datasets and architectures, including Stable Diffusion. We then turn to language and move beyond the energy-based view of AM. We show that uniform-state discrete diffusion models are AMs capable of retrieving unseen data, with basins of attraction that arise from conditional likelihood maximization alone. As the training set grows, basins around training examples contract while basins around unseen examples expand, until corrupted test sentences are recovered as reliably as training ones. Conditional entropy exposes this coexistence within a single sequence and cheaply diagnoses where a model lies along the transition. Together, these results suggest that AM is a general principle defined by basins of attraction, and that memorization and generalization can coexist within one model. AM thus offers a framework for asking when a generative model recalls and when it creates.

</details>
