In practical deployment, this paradigm introduces two fundamental limitations: the game loop must wait for model inference, making real-time interaction impractical, and repeated VLM calls incur substantial cost. Therefore, we propose PlayScriptBench, a script-based game benchmark that evaluates whether VLMs can synthesize an executable game-playing program from game states. This setting jointly evaluates visual grounding, understanding of game dynamics, long-horizon strategic reasoning, and executable program synthesis, providing a more comprehensive assessment of game-playing agents. To support more tractable evaluation for current general-purpose VLMs, we further introduce a four-stage pipeline --- Explore, Perception, API Construction, and Strategy Composition --- that decomposes controller synthesis into game exploration, state perception, interface construction, and strategic control, enabling agents to progressively construct an executable program. Experiments on 16 games spanning 4 genres show that even strong VLMs struggle on our benchmark, revealing a substantial gap between passive visual game understanding and executable closed-loop game control. These results establish script-based controller synthesis as a complementary direction for benchmarking VLM-based game-playing agents.

</details>


### [280] [QuASAR: Toward an LLM-Assisted Framework for Screening, Prototyping, and Assessing Quantum-Advantage Opportunities in Scientific Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27778)
*Mark L. Carroll,Jordan A. Caraballo-Vega,Brian P. Powell,John L. Schnase*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QuASAR 是一个两智能体 LLM 流水线：一个智能体从科学文献中筛选可能受益于量子算法的计算内核并提出候选，另一个智能体独立核验引用并重建算法、构建可运行量子原型、与经典基线对比。在十个天体物理计算候选上，九个跑通原型，三个详述案例（组合优化、随机模拟、贝叶斯推断）均未在测试规模实现量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别哪些科学计算能从量子算法中获益，同时需要领域知识与量子算法设计能力，而这两种专长很少集中在一个人身上。作者希望用 LLM 流水线弥合这一鸿沟，帮助领域科学家把文献中的想法转成可检验的量子原型。

Method: 采用实验性两智能体 LLM 驱动流水线。智能体一扫描文献，找出有量子算法对应物的计算内核并提出带引用的候选；智能体二独立核验引用，对通过者重建源算法的计算结构、给出量子映射、构建并执行原型、与经典基线做基准测试。

Result: 应用于十个天体物理文献候选计算，九个完成小规模基准原型。详细报告三个代表案例，覆盖组合优化、随机模拟与贝叶斯推断，重构了已有源算法的部分、对无算法者给出原创且公开披露的映射。三个案例在测试规模上均未取得量子优势，其天体物理框架也尚未经专家验证。

Conclusion: 实验表明该流水线能把已发表的设想转化为可运行原型，供科学家检查、与经典方法对比并决定是否深入。QuASAR 是探索性框架，供社区评估、批评与改进，该方法有望扩展量子计算在科研中的使用，并推广到更一般的计算改进场景。

Abstract: Identifying scientific computations that could benefit from quantum algorithms requires both domain knowledge and fluency in quantum algorithm design, expertise that rarely resides in one person. We report results from QuASAR, an experimental, two-agent, LLM-driven pipeline intended to bridge this gap. One agent screens scientific literature for computational kernels with a plausible quantum-algorithmic analogue and proposes candidates, each backed by citations. A second agent independently verifies those citations. For candidates that survive verification, it reconstructs the source's computational structure, formulates a quantum mapping, builds and executes a prototype, and benchmarks it against classical baselines. We applied this pipeline to ten candidate computations drawn from the astrophysics literature. Nine candidates were carried through to a small-scale, benchmarked prototype. We report three in detail, chosen as the clearest examples with the least redundancy across the set. They span combinatorial optimization, stochastic simulation, and Bayesian inference. The pipeline attempted to supply quantum-design expertise a domain scientist might lack, reconstructing source algorithms where one existed and building original, disclosed mappings where none did. None of the three case studies achieved quantum advantage at the tested scales, and their astrophysical framing has not yet undergone expert validation. What the experiment does show is that the pipeline can turn a published idea into a runnable prototype, which a scientist can then inspect, test against classical methods, and decide whether to pursue. QuASAR is an exploratory framework, offered to the community for evaluation, critique, and further development, with an approach that could broaden the use of quantum computing in scientific research and extend to computational improvements more generally.

</details>


### [281] [A Benchmark Protocol and Fairness Analysis Towards Open-Set EEG Seizure Type Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27783)
*Targol Bakhtiarvand,Terrance E. Boult,Jugal Kalita,Adham Atyabi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文首次为EEG发作类型分类引入开集识别协议，基于TUSZ v2.0.3按临床分类体系定义已知/未知类别，评测七种后处理开集评分器，结果显示开集EEG发作类型识别仍是难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG发作类型自动分类系统都基于闭集假设训练评估，即部署时遇到的发作类型都在训练中出现过。但临床中患者会出现罕见或非典型发作类型，超出训练标签空间，此时强制给出错误标签比弃权更糟。

Method: 在Temple University Hospital Seizure Corpus (TUSZ v2.0.3)上构建开集识别协议：以至少有12名受试者代表的发作类型作为已知类，罕见类型作为未知类。对部分微调的EEG基础模型(EFM)应用七种后处理开集评分器(MSP、MaxLogit、energy、Mahalanobis、kNN、GHOST、COSTARR)，采用受试者不重叠的留一类出(LOCO)协议，三个随机种子，检验方法能否检测出未参与微调的发作类型。

Result: 在真实未知类上，最佳评分器Mahalanobis平均AUROC达0.66，且顶级评分器间差异在种子波动范围内。来自未见受试者的留出已知发作类型检测仅略高于随机(AUROC 0.54–0.58)。各评分器的逐受试者拒识率极不均衡，GHOST相比energy评分未显著提升逐受试者公平性(双侧Wilcoxon符号秩检验，p ≥ 0.25)。

Conclusion: 作者公开了协议、受试者划分与代码作为初始基线；结果表明开集EEG发作类型识别仍是困难且未解决的问题。

Abstract: Automated EEG seizure type classification systems are typically trained and evaluated under the closed-set assumption, where all seizure types encountered at deployment are seen during training. In clinical practice, this assumption fails: patients present with rare or atypical seizure types that fall outside the training label space, and a system that forces a wrong label is worse than one that abstains. We introduce the first open-set recognition protocol for EEG seizure type classification on the Temple University Hospital Seizure Corpus (TUSZ v2.0.3), defining known and unknown classes using the clinical taxonomy itself: seizure types with sufficient subject representation (at least 12 subjects) form the known set, and rare types form the unknown set. We evaluate seven post-hoc open-set scorers (MSP, MaxLogit, energy, Mahalanobis, kNN, GHOST, and COSTARR) applied to a partially fine-tuned EEG Foundation Model (EFM), using a subject-disjoint leave-one-class-out (LOCO) protocol over three seeds that tests whether methods can detect seizure types excluded from fine-tuning. On true unknown classes, the best scorer (Mahalanobis) reaches a mean AUROC of 0.66, and differences among the top scorers are within seed-to-seed variation. Held-out known seizure types from unseen subjects are detected only slightly above chance (AUROC 0.54-0.58 for all scorers). Per-subject rejection rates are highly uneven for all scorers, and GHOST does not significantly improve per-subject fairness over energy scoring (two-sided Wilcoxon signed-rank, p >= 0.25). We release the protocol, subject splits, and code as an initial baseline; our results indicate that open-set seizure type recognition remains a hard, open problem.

</details>


### [282] [ChildAI-Gov: An Evidence-Linked AI System for Assessing EdTech Policies Against Children's Digital Rights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27799)
*Naveen Sigalapalli,Huizhi Liang,Clara Crivellaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出可解释AI系统ChildAI-Gov，基于CRIS五维指标（安全、隐私、透明、自主、非歧视）自动评估EdTech政策对儿童数字权利的尊重程度，结合LLM判断、规则检查与RAG证据溯源，并构建57份真实政策的AIPolicy-EdTech数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI进入儿童教育的速度快于治理能力，但人工核查EdTech工具是否符合儿童权利成本高、依赖专家、耗时长，缺少可追溯证据的自动化评估手段。

Method: 定义五维度儿童权利影响分数（CRIS）；每个维度由gpt-4o-mini的LLM判断与确定性规则检查组合打分；引入RAG，将评分锚定到欧盟AI法案、UNICEF指南等权利框架条文，使审计者可追溯依据；并构建含57份真实EdTech政策的AIPolicy-EdTech数据集。

Result: 五维度构成单一测量（Cronbach's α=0.79）；加入证据锚定后评分更严格但排序保持一致；无锚定基线宽松得多，放行近半数政策，而ChildAI-Gov一份也未通过。

Conclusion: 系统能对政策书面承诺提供证据可追溯的决策支持，但不观察真实行为，也不构成法律合规判定。

Abstract: We present ChildAI-Gov, an explainable AI system for assessing whether education-technology (EdTech) policies respect children's digital rights. Generative AI is reaching children's education faster than its governance can keep pace, yet checking a tool against children's rights remains manual, expert-dependent, and slow. We propose the Child Rights Impact Score (CRIS), an evidence-linked measure across five dimensions: safety, privacy, transparency, autonomy, and non-discrimination. ChildAI-Gov reads an EdTech tool's privacy or AI policy and scores each dimension by combining a large-language-model judgement (gpt-4o-mini) with a deterministic rule check. A retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) component grounds every score in the governing provisions, retrieved from a curated knowledge base of children's rights frameworks (the EU AI Act, UNICEF guidance, and others), so each score is tied to the evidence behind it and an auditor can trace which provision it rests on. We also construct AIPolicy-EdTech, a dataset of 57 real EdTech policies. On it, the five dimensions cohered as a single measure (Cronbach's alpha = 0.79), and grounding made scoring stricter while preserving rankings; an ungrounded baseline was far more lenient, passing nearly half the policies where ChildAI-Gov passed none. It offers evidence-linked decision support on documented commitments, not observation of real-world behaviour or determinations of legal compliance.

</details>


### [283] [SLP-Bench: Evaluating and Improving Acoustic Claims of Reasoning Audio Language Models on Clinical Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27810)
*Côme Hosxe,Rachid Riad,Quentin Spinat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SLP-Bench，对推理型音频语言模型思维链中每条声学论断与临床医生感知评分进行逐句核验，覆盖5个数据集25个感知维度；十个开源模型零样本下论断平衡准确率仅0.52–0.58，且一致性高的模型未必更准确；用GRPO微调7B模型可将准确率从0.54提升至0.60、平均kappa从0.39提升至0.61，同时保持答题准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床语音评估仅看最终答案，忽视了模型思维链中关于说话人声音特性的中间论断是否正确，缺乏对“论断是否与临床医生感知一致”的细粒度检验。

Method: 构建SLP-Bench基准：以临床医生对同一录音的感知评分为金标准，逐条评判思维链中的声学论断，评估事实性、重采样下的稳定性、以及改写问题与不同问题间的一致性；并设计基于“论断与临床一致”奖励的GRPO微调方案。

Result: 十个开源推理音频语言模型零样本下论断平衡准确率0.52–0.58，论断在重采样和改写下发生变化，最一致的模型并非最事实准确；GRPO微调7B模型后论断平衡准确率0.54→0.60，平均kappa 0.39→0.61，且每维度问题的回答准确率未下降。

Conclusion: 对思维链中声学论断进行过程级评估与奖励优化，能显著提升临床语音推理的可信度与一致性，SLP-Bench为临床音频模型的可靠性评估提供新范式（数据集需审批后获取）。

Abstract: Audio language models (ALMs) can answer clinical questions from speech, outperforming baselines built on handcrafted acoustic features. Reasoning ALMs generate a chain of thought that refers to properties of the speaker's voice before predicting. Each statement the model makes about the voice needs to be checked against clinicians' perceptual ratings of the same recording. On clinical speech, existing evaluations score only the final answer. We introduce Speech Language Pathologist Bench (SLP-Bench), a benchmark grading each acoustic claim in the chain of thought against clinicians' perceptual ratings of the same recording. It covers five clinician-rated datasets and 25 perceptual dimensions, and assesses claims for factuality, stability under resampling, and consistency across reworded and different questions. Across ten open-weight reasoning ALMs evaluated zero-shot, claim balanced accuracy ranges from 0.52 to 0.58, claims change under resampling and rewording, and the most consistent models are not the most factual. Fine-tuning a 7B ALM with GRPO on a reward for claims that agree with clinicians raises its claim balanced accuracy from 0.54 to 0.60 and its mean kappa from 0.39 to 0.61, while preserving answer accuracy on per-dimension questions. SLP-Bench is available upon request, subject to approval by the providers of each dataset.

</details>


### [284] [Auditable Financial Knowledge Graphs: Schema-Driven Construction and Intrinsic Quality Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27835)
*Samarth Sarin,Lovepreet Singh,Dhagash Mehta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种可审计、schema驱动的时序财务知识图谱构建方法：用通用文档智能服务解析分页财务文档，通过分阶段LLM抽取结构实体、量化观测与领域关系，经schema校验、多策略实体消歧、时序合并约束与细粒度溯源生成证据可追溯的知识图谱，并配套七维度内在质量评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 财务文档同时包含叙述性披露、会计指标、公司结构、管理层评论与时间相关事实，传统基于文本块的RAG难以有效表达这些异构、结构化且依赖报告期的信息，因此需要构建时序财务知识图谱。

Method: 1) 用通用文档智能服务解析分页文档，采用重叠文档窗口；2) 分阶段LLM抽取结构实体、定量观测和领域特定关系；3) 用预定义schema校验候选图元素；4) 通过scope-aware的精确、规范化、模糊、嵌入和LLM辅助实体消歧进行整合；5) 使用时序合并约束、确定性边去重、冲突感知属性管理和细粒度溯源，保证发行体身份、报告期有效性与证据可追溯性。

Result: 构建了来自异构财务文档的证据支撑型时序知识图谱，并提出包含七个加权维度、两个硬性校验门和结构诊断的内在评估框架；该框架将图谱质量评估与下游检索和答案生成分离，可系统诊断并迭代优化图谱。

Conclusion: 将图谱质量评估从下游检索/生成中解耦，可实现系统的质量诊断与迭代改进；主要贡献包括构建方法论、证据支撑的时序财务知识图谱，以及内在质量评估框架。

Abstract: Financial documents combine narrative disclosures, accounting metrics, corporate structures, management commentary, and time-dependent facts, making them difficult to represent using conventional chunk-based retrieval-augmented generation systems. This paper presents an auditable, schema-driven methodology for constructing temporal financial knowledge graphs from page-delimited documents parsed using a general purpose document intelligence service. Overlapping document windows undergo phased large language model extraction of structural entities, quantitative observations, and domain-specific relationships. Candidate graph elements are validated against predefined schemas and consolidated through scope-aware exact, normalized, fuzzy, embedding-based, and LLM-assisted entity resolution. Temporal merge constraints, deterministic edge deduplication, conflict-aware property management, and fine-grained provenance preserve issuer identity, reporting-period validity, and traceability to source evidence. The contributions of this work comprise the proposed methodology for financial knowledge graph construction, the creation of an evidence-grounded temporal knowledge graph from heterogeneous financial documents, and an intrinsic framework for evaluating the quality of the resulting graph. The evaluation framework assesses seven weighted dimensions, two hard validation gates, and structural diagnostics. By separating graph-quality assessment from downstream retrieval and answer generation, the framework supports systematic diagnosis and iterative refinement of the constructed graph.

</details>


### [285] [Beyond Self-Critique: Epistemic Convergence Control for Multi-Agent Research Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27839)
*Chrisantus Eze*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多智能体科研验证框架，用非对称角色、Belief-Delta收敛准则和NLI证据锚定，缓解自我批判偏见与过迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同源基模型实例化的辩论智能体共享潜在表征，导致结构性自我批判偏见；固定迭代协议要么过早停止，要么在收敛后浪费算力。

Method: ①非对称角色架构（Advocate/Skeptic/Arbitrator，对立认识论指令）；②Belief-Delta(DB)证据锚定的收敛停止准则；③每条支持性主张锚定到逐字文献片段，并由独立NLI分类器审计。

Result: 角色嵌入相似度0.852 vs 对称MAD 0.918（p=5.82e-8）；争议主张更多（22.0% vs 15.4%）；过迭代0.0% vs Fixed-3的62.5%，LLM调用减少2.4倍；幻觉率21.7% vs Self-Refine 25.9%（p=0.61）。200次运行、40个假设、DeepSeek-R1-32B。

Conclusion: \sys{}在计算效率与严格认识论验证之间取得平衡，并提供可审计的证据溯源。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems for scientific claim verification increasingly rely on role-structured LLM debate to pressure-test hypotheses. However, when debating agents are instantiated from the same underlying base model, they share identical latent representations, making self-critique bias structurally inevitable. Static fixed-iteration protocols compound this by either halting prematurely before evidence stabilizes or wasting inference compute after convergence. We present \sys{}, a multi-agent research verification framework that addresses both failure modes. First, we introduce an asymmetric role architecture comprising an Advocate, Skeptic, and Arbitrator with opposing epistemic directives, producing significantly lower role-embedding similarity (0.852 vs.\ 0.918 for symmetric Multi-Agent Debate [MAD], $p = 5.82 \times 10^{-8}$) and surfacing more contested claims (22.0\% vs.\ 15.4\%). Second, we propose the Belief-Delta ($\DB$) convergence criterion, an evidence-grounded stopping rule that eliminates over-iteration (0.0\% vs.\ 62.5\% for Fixed-$3$) while cutting LLM calls by 2.4$\times$. Third, each supported claim is anchored to a verbatim literature snippet audited by an independent NLI classifier, achieving a 21.7\% hallucination rate (comparable to 25.9\% for a Self-Refine critic, $p = 0.61$) while establishing auditable evidence grounding. Across 200 runs on 40 research hypotheses evaluated with DeepSeek-R1-32B, \sys{} balances compute efficiency with rigorous epistemic verification.

</details>


### [286] [SLICR: Strategy Logic-Guided Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27865)
*Arshia Rafieioskouie,Matthew Lucas,Tzu-Han Hsu,Borzoo Bonakdarpour*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SLICR is a specification-guided RL framework using Strategy Logic (SL) plus LTL that for the first time targets adversarial, partially observable settings.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing specification-guided RL translates formal specs into shaped rewards, but does not handle adversarial multi-agent settings under partial observability, nor reason about how agents should respond to others' strategies.

Method: Use Strategy Logic (SL) to explicitly quantify over agents' strategies, combined with LTL to express objectives and constraints, and derive shaped rewards for RL agents.

Result: Evaluated on Predator-Prey, Google Research Football, and a home-grown SMAC-HARD extension, showing effectiveness, scalability, and adaptability to diverse adversarial strategies.

Conclusion: SLICR advances specification-guided RL to adversarial, partially observable multi-agent domains, enabling reasoning over strategic responses to other agents.

Abstract: Specification-guided reinforcement learning (RL) translates formal specifications of desired behavior of agents into shaped rewards. This paper introduces SLICR, a significant paradigm shift that advances the state of the art to specification-guided RL in adversarial settings under partial observability. We employ Strategy Logic (SL), which allows explicit quantification over agents' strategies and uses the linear temporal logic (LTL) to specify the agents' objectives and constraints. This enables reasoning not only about how agents should act to satisfy a specification, but also about how they should respond to the strategies and behaviors of other agents. We evaluate SLICR on three popular benchmarks: Predator-Prey, Google Research Football, and our home-grown extension of SMAC-HARD, demonstrating its effectiveness, scalability, and ability to adapt to diverse adversarial strategies.

</details>


### [287] [Integrating Generative Artificial Intelligence with Learning Style Models: Opportunities, Challenges, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27868)
*Malithi Nawarathne,Prasanna Haddela,Nuwan Kodagoda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇系统综述（PRISMA 2020，检索Scopus、IEEE Xplore、Google Scholar，2015–2026，纳入41篇研究），探讨如何将生成式AI与VARK、Kolb、Felder-Silverman、Honey & Mumford等学习风格模型结合，以提升大规模在线课堂中的学生参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模在线课堂中个性化与参与度不足，生成式AI具备灵活、多模态、实时适配潜力，但缺乏其与成熟学习风格模型系统性整合的梳理。

Method: 遵循PRISMA 2020指南进行系统文献综述：跨三个数据库检索并筛选出41篇符合条件的研究，将生成式AI能力映射到学习风格偏好，综合现有实现方式，归纳收益与挑战，并提出未来研究与实践路径。

Result: 构建了生成式AI能力与学习风格偏好的映射，梳理了已有实现案例，识别出收益与挑战（同时指出学习风格本身实证效度仍存争议）。

Conclusion: 尽管学习风格理论存在争议，生成式AI仍可实现灵活、多模态、实时的学习适配；研究为教育者与开发者构建更有效、以学习者为中心的系统提供了实践启示。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (Generative AI) has emerged as a powerful tool for transforming learning. This review examines how Generative AI can be integrated with established learning style models (VARK, Kolb, Felder-Silverman, and Honey & Mumford) to support student engagement in large online classes. Following PRISMA 2020 guidelines, we searched Scopus, IEEE Xplore, and Google Scholar (2015 to 2026) and included 41 studies that met the eligibility criteria. The review maps Generative AI capabilities to learning style preferences, synthesizes existing implementations, highlights benefits and challenges, and proposes pathways for future research and practice. Despite ongoing debates regarding the empirical validity of learning styles, Generative AI enables flexible, multimodal, and real-time adaptation. This study offers practical insights for educators and developers seeking to build more effective, learner centered learning systems

</details>


### [288] [Speculative Decoding Is Less Speculative Than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27891)
*Guanning Zeng,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DICE（Draw-Informed Candidate Selection）利用草稿阶段可预先固定采样随机数这一观察，将验证阶段的随机性与草稿同步，从而把有限草稿计算集中到更可能被采用的续写上，提升投机解码吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码的效率取决于有限的草稿计算能否准确预判目标模型实际会走的路径。该路径的不确定性有两个来源：目标模型预测（需前向才能获知）和采样随机性（可在草稿前固定）。现有方法只利用前者，忽略了后者的可利用性。

Method: 在草稿与验证之间预采样并同步验证随机数（draws），把已知随机数与现有drafter预测结合，用于草稿树构建中的节点选择；该原则进一步扩展到异步预取，预测下一轮应准备的草稿。无需额外drafter训练，也不改变目标采样分布。

Result: 在多种设置下达到state-of-the-art解码吞吐，相对强基线最高提升24.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 将采样随机性前移并纳入草稿决策是一种低成本、无训练、保持分布不变的通用提速手段，对同步树构建与异步预取均有效。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates language-model inference by preparing candidate continuations before the target model generates them. Its efficiency depends on how well limited drafting computation anticipates the path the target will actually follow. Anticipating this path involves two sources of uncertainty: the target model's predictions and the randomness of sampling. Yet the former requires a target-model forward pass, while the latter can be fixed before drafting. Building on this observation, we introduce DICE (Draw-Informed Candidate Selection), which pre-samples and synchronizes verification randomness across drafting and verification. By combining these known draws with predictions from an existing drafter, DICE allocates computation toward continuations more likely to be used in the current sampling run. This principle guides node selection throughout draft-tree construction and extends to asynchronous prefetching, where DICE predicts which next-round drafts to prepare. Without additional drafter training or changes to the target sampling distribution, DICE achieves state-of-the-art decoding throughput across diverse settings, with speedups of up to 24.6pp over strong baselines.

</details>


### [289] [The Dual-Tier Sentiment Framework: Scalable Multimodal UX Attribution in Web Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27899)
*Yi Ding,Gaurav Kumar,Rui Min,Isabella Ye,Bhavika Goyal,Emilio Antunez,Huihui Chen,Ke Xu,Xiaowei Sun,Xin Xu,Huijie Feng,Pradyumna Narayana,Golnaz Farhadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"双层级情感框架"(Dual-Tier Sentiment Framework)：Tier 1 用微调的编码器-解码器 LLM 做大规模用户情感回归检测，Tier 2 用多模态 LLM 诊断视觉-空间布局问题，实现可扩展的 UX 归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模网络搜索的 UX 质量评估需要可扩展方案。传统问卷调研慢，制约快速 A/B 测试；自动化指标又缺乏细粒度，无法诊断视觉-空间类 UX 异常。

Method: 两层架构：Tier 1（宏观系统）基于 Gemini XS 微调编码器-解码器 LLM，输入遥测特征，采用多任务正则化与上下文特征工程做用户情感回归检测；Tier 2（微观系统）用多模态 LLM（Gemini 3.0 Pro）原生评估搜索结果块的视觉渲染与空间布局，以"User Toil"量化用户达成满意结果所需努力，并结合 RL 自动 prompt 优化与 AI judge 判别胜负模式。

Result: Tier 1 在 50 万+ 留存记录上 PR-AUC 提升 9.8%，12 个留存实验中召回率持平基线、精确率相对提升约 20%；生产环境将测量延迟从数周降至数小时，调研开销降低 40%+。Tier 2 的 MLLM 在 RL prompt 优化与 AI judge 加持下 F1 提升 6.5%+。系统每日处理 O(100k) 实时实验查询。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了可扩展、准确且具备诊断能力的 UX 归因，可在工业级搜索场景中支撑大规模 A/B 测试与细粒度布局摩擦分析。

Abstract: Sustaining user experience (UX) quality in large-scale web search requires scalable evaluation. Traditional surveys are slow and bottleneck rapid A/B testing, while automated metrics often lack the granularity to diagnose visual-spatial UX anomalies. To address this, we introduce the Dual-Tier Sentiment Framework, a novel industrial architecture for automated UX attribution. Tier 1 (Macro System) acts as a scalable user sentiment regression detector, utilizing a fine-tuned encoder-decoder LLM (based on Gemini XS) on telemetry features. Through multi-task regularization and contextual feature engineering, this tier achieves a +9.8% PR-AUC gain on over 500,000 held-out records, matching baseline recall on all validated regressions with $\sim$20% relative precision gain among the 12 held-out experiments. In production, it reduces measurement latency from weeks to hours and cuts survey overhead by over 40%. Tier 2 (Micro System) provides granular diagnostics by employing a Multimodal LLM (Gemini 3.0 Pro) to natively assess visual renderings and spatial layout of web result blocks, quantifying "User Toil" as the estimated effort required to reach a satisfactory result. Enhanced by RL-based automated prompt optimization and an AI judge for win/loss patterns, the MLLM yields a $>$6.5% F1 score improvement, offering deep insights into content quality and layout friction. Ultimately, this framework enables scalable, accurate, and diagnostic UX attribution, processing O(100k) live experiment queries daily.

</details>


### [290] [Functional Depth Is Learned: Isolating the Computation That Layers Produce Only Together](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27911)
*JaeSeong Kim,KyungTae Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出精确的容斥分解来分离 Transformer 中"多层协同计算"（连通分量），并在 Pythia、OLMo、Amber 的公开检查点上追踪其随预训练的演化。结果表明跨层协同随预训练增强、对行为有实质因果影响，且与表征相似性呈负相关但不可互换，说明架构深度提供顺序计算的机会，而预训练逐步组织出跨层依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层数只代表"顺序计算的机会"，但各层可能只是近乎独立的逐步精修，彼此几乎不依赖。因此需要量化 Transformer 在预训练中如何发展出真正的"功能深度"，即层间协同计算的程度，而现有指标不足以隔离这种多层共同作用才出现的计算。

Method: 提出精确的容斥（inclusion-exclusion）分解，在"单位消融"（identity ablation）下隔离指定层集合共同作用时才出现的计算，即连接分量（connected component）；在 Pythia、OLMo、Amber 的公开检查点上测量各阶交互，并结合直接干预（删除该分量 vs. 删除同范数随机方向）以及成对表征相似性进行对比分析。

Result: 三点发现：(1) 写相对（write-relative）的连通协同在各交互阶上均从初始化/最早检查点基线起随预训练增强；(2) 行为上具实质影响——在全部 35 个"模型×阶"单元中，删除该连通分量对困惑度的损害均大于删除同位置同范数随机方向；(3) 成对表征相似性与成对连通协同在 7 个模型中持续负相关，但二者不可互换：基于相似性与基于协同的模块删除判据在 Pythia 上常一致，在 Amber 上则出现分歧。

Conclusion: 架构深度只提供顺序计算的可能性，是预训练逐步在深度内组织出跨层依赖，才形成功能深度；跨层协同是行为相关的独立信号，不能用表征相似性替代。

Abstract: Transformer performance scales with depth, but layer count merely counts the opportunities for sequential computation: layers could still behave as nearly independent refinements that barely depend on one another. We study how transformers develop functional depth during pretraining, introducing an exact inclusion-exclusion decomposition that isolates the connected component, i.e., the computation that appears only when a specified set of layers acts together under identity ablation. Tracking this quantity across public checkpoints of Pythia, OLMo, and Amber yields three findings. First, write-relative connected coordination strengthens from its initialization or earliest-checkpoint baseline as pretraining proceeds, across all measured interaction orders. Second, these connected components are behaviorally consequential under direct intervention: deleting them damages perplexity more than deleting a norm-matched random direction at the same site in all 35 measured model-order cells. Third, pairwise representation similarity is consistently negatively correlated with pairwise connected coordination across seven models, yet the two signals are not interchangeable: similarity- and coordination-based block-removal criteria often agree in Pythia but diverge on Amber. Together, these results suggest that architectural depth provides the opportunity for sequential computation, while pretraining progressively organizes cross-layer dependencies within that depth.

</details>


### [291] [BioEmu2: Scalable Emulation of Biomolecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27924)
*Michael Gastegger,Adam E. Foster,José Jiménez-Luna,Tim Hempel,Sarah Lewis,Yu Xie,Lixin Sun,Iryna Zaporozhets,J. Thorben Frank,Yaoyi Chen,Michael Plainer,Ludwig Winkler,Pedro B. P. S. Reis,Sven H. Giese,Roberto Sordillo,Victor García Satorras,Eray Inanc,Kacper Kapuśniak,Soojung Yang,Rishwanth Raghu,Rachel Kim,Gabriel Dernbach,Deniz Gunceler,Hannes Schulz,Bonnie Kruft,Marwin Segler,Christopher M. Bishop,Cecilia Clementi,Ryota Tomioka,Frank Noé*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BioEmu2，一个统一的生成模型，可同时对生物分子进行静态结构预测、平衡态采样与时间演化，速度比分子动力学快数个数量级，并支持含蛋白质、核酸、修饰残基、配体与离子的多组分体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构预测虽已取得重大进展，但大规模建模驱动生物分子功能的运动与相互作用仍是核心挑战；传统分子动力学（MD）计算代价过高，难以规模化。

Method: 构建生成式模型，训练数据融合实验结构、合成结构与大量MD数据；支持多组分体系；采用上下文并行模式以处理超大复合物；可通过残基接触条件化或用户自定义偏置势进行引导；并结合增强采样。

Result: 结构预测精度媲美顶级开源折叠模型；预测规模可达兆道尔顿级复合物；平衡分布接近MD参考，自由能误差约1 kcal/mol；可预测长时程动力学轨迹及结合与构象变化的动力学；增强采样可计算涉及稀有态的自由能。

Conclusion: BioEmu2将静态预测、平衡采样与动力学统一于单一生成框架，使生物分子系综与动力学研究具备可扩展性。

Abstract: Despite major advances in structure prediction, modelling the motions and interactions that drive biomolecular function at scale remains a central challenge. We introduce BioEmu2, a generative model for atomically resolved biomolecular structures combining static structure prediction, equilibrium sampling and time evolution, that samples orders of magnitude faster than molecular dynamics (MD). Trained on experimental and synthetic structures and extensive MD data, it supports multi-component systems containing proteins, nucleic acids, modified residues, ligands and ions. Structure-prediction accuracy is comparable to top open-weight biomolecular folding models, and a context parallel mode enables prediction of megadalton complexes. Equilibrium distributions approximate MD references with free-energy errors near 1 kcal/mol across tested proteins and complexes. Time propagation predicts long-timescale dynamical trajectories and kinetics of binding and conformational changes. Generated structures can be controlled by conditioning on residue contacts or steering with user-defined bias potentials. Enhanced sampling enables free energy calculation involving rare states. BioEmu2 makes the study of biomolecular ensembles and dynamics scalable.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [292] [Learning under Bounded Manipulation for Quadratic Incentive Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26736)
*Arda Güçlü,Subhonmesh Bose,Tamer Başar*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 研究重复二次激励设计博弈中，跟随者可通过偏离短视最优反应来操纵领导者的观测与类型估计，领导者在此操纵下的最小最大遗憾率为 max{log T, min{C_T, T}}，并给出达到下界的简单学习策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领导者需从跟随者动作的噪声观测中学习其私有类型，而跟随者知道学习策略并有动机操纵观测以影响未来估计；因此需要量化这种操纵能力如何影响领导者的性能。

Method: 在操纵预算 C_T（约束跟随者动作偏离短视最优反应的期望累积平方偏差）下，建立信息论下界，该下界适用于一般类的因果估计与操纵策略；并构造一个简单的学习策略来匹配该下界。

Result: 对近似理性的跟随者，最小最大遗憾率为 max{log T, min{C_T, T}} 阶；所提简单学习策略可达到该下界；且在固定学习规则下，增大跟随者操纵预算反而可能降低领导者遗憾，呈反直觉现象。

Conclusion: 操纵预算与时间共同决定领导者可达性能的下界与上界，二者在 max{log T, min{C_T,T}} 阶处吻合；同时遗憾对操纵预算并非单调，说明设计学习规则时需谨慎考虑跟随者的策略性操纵。

Abstract: We study a repeated quadratic incentive design game in which a leader learns a follower's private type from noisy observations of the follower's actions and uses her estimates in an affine incentive rule. The follower knows the leader's learning policy, which incorporates past observations to produce estimates of the follower's type, and can deviate from his myopic best response to influence the leader's future estimates. We ask how the follower's ability to manipulate these observations affects the leader's performance. Under a manipulation budget $C_T$ constraining the expected cumulative squared deviation of the follower's actions from his myopic best response, we establish a minimax regret rate of order $\max\{\log T, \min\{C_T,T\}\}$ for approximately rational followers. Our information-theoretic lower bound applies to a general class of causal estimation and manipulation policies for the leader and the follower. We provide a simple learning policy that achieves the lower bound. Finally, we show that under a fixed learning rule, increasing the manipulation budget of the follower can counterintuitively reduce the leader's regret.

</details>


### [293] [Incentive Compatible Mechanisms for Sequential Fair Resource Allocation with Private Demands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26800)
*Ramtin Salamat,Sean R. Sinclair,Seyed M. R. Iravani*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 研究在线公平分配的策略性版本：代理人私下知道自身需求并可能谎报，无货币转移。作者刻画了激励相容（IC）分配规则的结构，给出最优机制呈简单“上限（cap）”结构的条件，并通过将离散动态规划归约为DAG上的最长路径问题来近似最优IC机制；实验表明忽略策略性谎报的代价远高于施加激励相容约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典在线公平分配假设需求真实，但在需求自报且无货币转移的非营利场景中，代理人会策略性虚报需求，因此需要研究带策略性报告的在线公平分配机制设计。

Method: 建立序贯分配模型：每阶段规划者公布分配规则，代理人报告需求，按规则与报告分配可分资源。先刻画IC分配规则的结构，据此推导最优机制具有简单cap结构的条件；再将离散化动态规划归约为有向无环图上的最长路径问题，得到最优IC机制的近似算法。

Result: 得到了IC规则的结构刻画与最优机制为cap结构的充分条件；提出基于DAG最长路径的近似方法；数值实验显示忽略策略性报告所导致的损失显著大于为满足激励相容所付出的代价。

Conclusion: 在自报需求的在线公平分配中，策略性谎报不可忽视；IC机制可在较小效率代价下实现，且其最优解可通过结构刻画与最长路径归约有效近似。

Abstract: We study a strategic variant of the canonical online fair allocation problem, where a planner must sequentially allocate a divisible resource to agents with privately known demands. At each stage, the planner announces an allocation rule, the agent reports their demand, and the planner allocates resources according to the rule and the reported demand. Unlike the standard model, we allow agents to strategically misreport their needs, as can arise in nonprofit settings where demand is self-reported and monetary transfers are unavailable. We characterize the structure of incentive-compatible (IC) allocation rules in this model, and use this to identify conditions under which the optimal mechanism has a simple cap structure. We then develop an approximation method for the optimal IC mechanism through a careful reduction of the discretized dynamic program to a longest-path problem on a directed acyclic graph. Numerical experiments show that ignoring strategic reporting can be substantially more costly than imposing incentive compatibility.

</details>


### [294] [Bottlenecked: The Capital Cost of Protected DeFi Lending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26948)
*Tarun Chitra,Manvir Schneider,Nagu Thogiti*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文用三方图模型刻画DeFi借贷中用户资产、被覆盖负债与可赔付的次级资本的匹配关系，提出"对抗性违约溢价(ADP)"度量可组合金融中保险的超额成本，并证明现有Aave/Morpho式设计的保险成本为Θ(N)，而优化图结构可降至Θ(√N)，实测资本节省最高达34.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeFi锁仓数百亿美元，单靠加密抵押无法吸收全部损失，需要经济资本(次级资本)兜底；而可组合性使"保护的数量与位置"都变得关键——2026年4月Kelp约3亿美元跨链桥攻击显示攻击者可跨借贷市场以被盗资产为抵押借款，损失因此扩散。作者希望回答：应如何配置在用户受损前先吸收损失的次级资本。

Method: 构建记录用户资产、被覆盖负债与允许赔付的次级资本的三方图(tripartite graph)；用图割(cuts)识别缺失的保护，用图上的静态安全博弈定义保护的合理价格，即对抗性违约溢价ADP；进而研究"贷款融资+债权人保险"的联合成本，对比N个借款人与M个贷款人的图结构。

Result: 证明Aave、Morpho等现有系统的保险成本为Θ(N)；在温和假设下，最优图的保险成本为Θ(√N)且常数很小。将模型应用于多起2026年DeFi网络攻击进行ADP估计与优化图性能评估，相对类Aave/Morpho设计可实现最高34.5%的实际资本成本节省。

Conclusion: 把次级资本作为协议设计中的一阶约束(first-class constraint)，能显著提升借贷协议的资本效率；ADP为可组合金融系统中的保险定价提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Securing hundreds of billions of dollars in decentralized finance (DeFi) requires both cryptographically secured collateral and economic capital to absorb losses that collateral alone cannot cover. Composability makes both the amount and location of this protection matter: Kelp's nearly \$300 million April 2026 bridge exploit showed how an attacker could borrow against stolen assets across lending markets. We study how to allocate junior capital that absorbs losses before users incur losses in lending protocols. The main object we study is a tripartite graph that records user assets, their covered liabilities, and the junior capital permitted to pay them. Cuts in this graph determine what protection is missing, while a static security game on the graph defines how much such protection should cost. We call this cost the adversarial default premium (ADP), which can be viewed as the excess cost of insurance in composable financial systems. We then study the joint cost of funding loans and protecting their creditors via this insurance. In a tripartite graph with $N$ borrowers and $M$ lenders, we show that existing systems such as Aave and Morpho have an insurance cost that is $Θ(N)$. Under mild assumptions, we show that the optimal graph has an insurance cost that is $Θ(\sqrt{N})$ with small constants. We apply our model to numerous 2026 DeFi cyber attacks and estimate ADP and the performance of optimized graphs. We find practical capital cost savings of up to 34.5% for our optimized design versus designs that resemble Aave and Morpho. Our results suggest that new lending protocols can improve capital efficiency by treating junior capital as a first-class constraint in protocol design.

</details>


### [295] [The Complexity of Fair and Stable Data Exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27438)
*Hannaneh Akrami,Bhaskar Ray Chaudhury*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 该论文解决了互惠且核心稳定数据交换的高效计算问题，在子模情形给出FPTAS，在超模情形证明PPAD-hard，形成计算分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web应用中跨参与者交换数据可提升推荐、搜索、欺诈检测等效果，但需保证贡献公平回报（互惠）并防止群体脱离（核心稳定）。Akrami等证明此类交换在宽泛假设下存在，却未解决高效计算问题。

Method: 针对交叉单调份额（含单调子模效用下的Shapley份额），提出循环（circulation）算法，在可高效访问近似份额时以poly(n,1/ε,L)时间计算精确核心稳定、ε-互惠的交换，得到L多项式时有界时的FPTAS。对单调格超模效用，通过归约证明ε-互惠、ε-核心稳定交换的求解是PPAD-hard。

Result: 子模效用下存在FPTAS；超模效用下即使精度为反多项式、L为常数、Shapley份额可精确多项式时间求值，问题仍为PPAD-hard，从而在Shapley份额下建立计算分离。

Conclusion: 重叠信息允许高效、公平且稳定的数据交换，而互补性即便贡献评估容易也会造成计算壁垒。

Abstract: Data exchange is a fundamental challenge for Web applications such as recommender systems, personalized search, and fraud detection, where combining data across participants can improve predictions. Sustaining such collaboration requires fair returns for contributions and protection against groups leaving for a more beneficial exchange. These requirements are captured by reciprocity, which equates each participant's received utility with its credited contribution to others, and core stability, which rules out any coalition exchanging its own data to make every member strictly better off. Akrami et al. prove that such exchanges exist under broad assumptions, but leave efficient computation open. Two fundamental cases are submodular utilities, capturing diminishing returns from overlapping datasets, and supermodular utilities, capturing complementarities. We settle the approximate computation of reciprocal and core-stable exchanges in both cases, establishing a computational separation under Shapley sharing. For cross-monotone shares, which include Shapley shares under monotone submodular utilities, we give a circulation algorithm that computes an exactly core-stable, $ε$-reciprocal exchange in time poly$(n,1/ε,L)$ given efficient access to approximate shares, where $n$ is the number of participants and $L$ bounds how fast each donor's credit changes with its own contribution. This yields an FPTAS when $L$ is polynomially bounded. In contrast, finding an $ε$-reciprocal, $ε$-core-stable exchange is PPAD-hard for monotone lattice-supermodular utilities, even at inverse-polynomial accuracy, with constant $L$ and exact polynomial-time evaluation of Shapley shares. Thus overlapping information permits efficient fair and stable exchange, while complementarities create a computational barrier even when contribution credits are easy to evaluate.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [296] [Windchime: an Audiovisual Installation with Audio-Language Model implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26759)
*Trent Eriksen,Celeste Betancur Gutierrez*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: Windchime 是一个视听装置，用语音检索艺术家预设的音乐与交互动画，通过本地ASR、音频语言模型嵌入检索和 Three.js 场景，实现人、模型与艺术家三方分权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望探索声音、语言与视觉的多模态交互，并重新思考控制权在艺术家、AI模型与观众之间的分配（agency distribution）。

Method: 本地ASR模型转写提示，音频语言模型(ALM)将其嵌入并在406个音频stem上检索，返回声景并叠加实时编码的Strudel片段；同时检索Three.js交互动画场景，通过monome grid与arc硬件提供触觉控制。

Result: 构建出可运行的视听装置，语音输入能同时驱动音乐检索与交互视觉动画，并支持实体硬件操作。

Conclusion: Windchime 通过三方分权（艺术家、ALM、访客）实现了多模态具身交互，展示了生成式AI在艺术装置中人机共创的可能性。

Abstract: Windchime is an audiovisual installation in which spoken language retrieves music and interactive animations from an artist authored corpus. A local Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) model transcribes each prompt while an audio-language model (ALM) embeds it and retrieval over 406 audio stems returns a soundscape with live coded Strudel fragments that layer into audio output. Simultaneously each spoken input results in retrieval of interactive Three.js animation scenes each containing unique tactile control via monome grid and arc hardware. Windchime distributes agency in three distinct directions; across the artist who authored the possible outputs, the ALM that influences what areas of the corpus can be retrieved, and the visitor whose spoken inputs result in multimodal embodied interaction.

</details>


### [297] [Fooling the Mind Twice: Human Detection of Audiovisual Deepfakes Under Repeated Exposure and Corrective Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27080)
*Ammarah Hashmi,Sahibzada Adil Shahzad,Yu Tsao,Hsin-Min Wang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 重复评估结合纠正性反馈能适度提升人类的深伪检测准确率（+4.68pp 与 +8.64pp），但判断不稳定性和过度自信依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类深伪检测能力仍不可靠，而"重复评估+纠正性反馈"能否改善后续判断尚不清楚，因此需要考察初次与重复评估之间的差异。

Method: 110 名被试对来自 FakeAVCeleb 和 DFDC 两个数据集的各 40 段音视频进行两阶段评估：PhaseI 初次判断，PhaseII 在同一批视频上结合重复呈现与纠正性反馈重新判断；分析采用配对 t 检验，以及以被试和视频为聚类稳健标准误的判断层面逻辑回归。

Result: FakeAVCeleb 准确率由 63.30% 升至 67.98%（+4.68pp，t(109)=5.61，p<.001，d_z=0.54；β=0.208，OR=1.23）；DFDC 由 59.52% 升至 68.16%（+8.64pp，t(109)=7.37，p<.001，d_z=0.70；β=0.375，OR=1.46）。提升主要源于假阴性减少，假阳性率不变；但判断反转率达 27.55%（FakeAVCeleb）和 32.91%（DFDC），部分原本正确的判断变为错误。两阶段中被试自评信心均系统性高于实际准确率约 11.99pp 和 12.82pp。

Conclusion: 重复呈现加纠正性反馈可中等程度提升深伪检测并主要通过减少漏检起效，但无法消除判断不稳定性与残余错误，过度自信也持续存在，说明人类判断的固有局限未被完全克服。

Abstract: Human deepfake detection remains unreliable, and whether reassessment with corrective feedback improves subsequent judgments remains unclear. This study examined differences between initial and repeated deepfake assessments among 110 participants using audiovisual stimuli from the FakeAVCeleb and Deepfake Detection Challenge (DFDC) datasets. Participants evaluated 40 videos from each dataset in phase 1 (PhaseI) and reassessed the same videos in phase 2 (PhaseII) under a procedure combining repeated presentation and corrective feedback. For the FakeAVCeleb dataset, the participants' mean accuracy increased by 4.68 percentage points from 63.30 in PhaseI to 67.98 in PhaseII (t(109)=5.61, p<.001, d_z=0.54). A judgment-level logistic regression with standard errors clustered by participant and video produced a consistent result: beta=0.208, SE=0.040, z=5.23, p<.001, {OR}=1.23. For the DFDC dataset, the participants' mean accuracy increased by 8.64 percentage points from 59.52% in PhaseI to 68.16% in PhaseII (t(109)=7.37, p<.001, d_z=0.70); the logistic model yielded: beta=0.375, SE=0.065, z=5.78, p<.001, {OR}=1.46. Improvements primarily came from fewer false negatives, while false-positive rates remained unchanged. However, judgment reversals occurred in 27.55% of FakeAVCeleb and 32.91% of DFDC assessments, with some initially correct judgments becoming incorrect upon reassessment. Despite the accuracy gains, repeated assessment did not eliminate judgment instability or residual error. Participants also exhibited consistent overconfidence, with mean self-rated confidence exceeding actual accuracy by 11.99 percentage points for FakeAVCeleb and 12.82 percentage points for DFDC across both phases. These findings indicate that repetition with corrective feedback moderately improves deepfake detection but does not fully resolve the limitations of human judgment.

</details>


### [298] [Show Me the Answer: Visual Grounding on Dashboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27355)
*Yuefei Zhang,Songyin Tang,Yile Cai,Yuqi Bu,Liuwu Li,Yanna Lin,Zikun Deng,Yi Cai*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出仪表盘视觉定位任务（VGD），并给出两阶段框架VGD-Net与基准VGD-Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言已成为访问复杂可视化系统的重要交互方式，但视觉定位研究多集中于自然图像，仪表盘因多图表布局复杂、元素细粒度而研究不足，且需要将查询定位到具体视觉元素以提供证据。

Method: 提出VGD任务：给定仪表盘截图与自然语言查询，输出对应视觉元素的边界框。模型VGD-Net采用两阶段框架，模拟用户定位信息的流程：先做查询感知的图表检索，再做图表内细粒度定位。同时构建VGD-Bench基准，覆盖图表检索、图内定位与整体VGD三种互补设置。

Result: 在VGD任务上，VGD-Net优于强多模态基线，平均IoU达57.06，成功率为66.82；在图表检索与图内定位两个阶段也均超过现有方法。

Conclusion: 阶段分解有助于实现可靠且可解释的VGD，并有望支持查询驱动的可视化分析。

Abstract: Natural language interaction has become an increasingly important paradigm for accessing complex visual information systems. In visual data analytics environments, such as dashboards, a key capability is to ground user queries to specific visual elements, enabling systems to identify the visual evidence relevant to the query. However, existing visual grounding research has primarily focused on natural images, while grounding in dashboards remains underexplored due to their complex multi-chart layouts and fine-grained visual elements. In this paper, we study the task of Visual Grounding on Dashboards (VGD): Given a dashboard screenshot and a natural language query, the goal is to localize the visual element corresponding to the query using a bounding box. We propose VGD-Net, a two-stage grounding framework that reflects how users typically locate information in dashboards, by first performing query-aware chart retrieval and then fine-grained in-chart grounding. To systematically evaluate VGD-Net's performance on this task, we construct VGD-Bench, a benchmark specifically designed for VGD, covering three complementary settings: chart retrieval, in-chart grounding, and overall VGD. Experimental results show that our two-stage VGD-Net outperforms strong multimodal baselines on the VGD task, achieving a mean IoU of 57.06 and a success rate of 66.82, while also delivering superior performance over existing methods in both stages of chart retrieval and in-chart grounding. These results highlight the value of stage decomposition for reliable and interpretable VGD, and its potential to support query-driven visual analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [299] [ERA: Embed, Route, Adapt for Cold-Start Cross-Model Latent Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26919)
*Venkata Chivukula,Zifeng Wang,Jinsung Yoon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ERA（Embed, Route, Adapt）是一个面向冷启动跨模型潜在检索的系统，无需重嵌全库或查询日志，通过锚点采样+轻量适配器+确定性路由器实现新编码器高效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产检索系统与索引语料的嵌入模型绑定，换编码器通常需重嵌全语料；冷启动时无查询日志，且新编码器嵌入每篇文档都有API或计算成本。

Method: 1）用小型锚点预算，通过Cosine Farthest Point Sampling从现有目标索引中挑选最具信息量的文档；2）用Manifold Alignment Loss（方向对齐+正交正则化防表示坍塌）训练轻量适配器；3）搭配确定性路由器，仅对少量难查询升级处理。

Result: 在五个BEIR数据集上匹配或超越逐点L2与嵌入转换基线，Arguana上nDCG@10相对提升达35%；聚类语料上锚点选择在固定预算下也改进既有方法；适配器+路由器仅增1.2ms开销，20%升级预算下将端到端查询延迟从约160ms降至38-86ms，并集中于投影失败最多的查询。

Conclusion: ERA证明冷启动跨模型检索可低成本实现：少量锚点+轻量适配+选择性升级即可在保持质量的同时显著降延迟，为生产检索系统的编码器迁移提供实用方案。

Abstract: Production retrieval systems are tied to the embedding model that indexed their corpus. Moving queries to a different encoder normally requires re-embedding the whole corpus, and at cold start there are no query logs while every document embedded by the new encoder incurs an API or compute cost. We present ERA (Embed, Route, Adapt), a system for cold-start cross-model latent retrieval. ERA spends a small anchor budget on maximally informative documents, selected from the existing target index via Cosine Farthest Point Sampling, trains a lightweight adapter with a Manifold Alignment Loss that couples directional alignment with orthogonal regularization to prevent representation collapse, and pairs the adapter with a deterministic router that escalates the rare, hard queries that define perceived retrieval quality. Across five BEIR datasets, ERA matches or outperforms point-wise L2 and embedding-conversion baselines, with relative gains of up to 35% nDCG@10 on Arguana, and on clustered corpora its anchor selection also improves prior methods under a fixed anchor budget. With 1.2 ms of adapter and router overhead on top of a ~20 ms local encoder, ERA at a 20% nominal escalation budget reduces end-to-end query latency from roughly 160 ms to 38-86 ms while concentrating escalations on the queries where the projection fails most.

</details>


### [300] [Leader Finding: A New Variation of Expert Finding in Community Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26921)
*Sepideh Entezari Maleki,Mahmood Neshati*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出"领导者发现"问题：给定项目与团队，按领导力素质对候选领导者排序，并构建融合技术契合度、管理知识与团队沟通能力三要素的概率模型，在Stack Overflow两个大规模数据集上NDCG@10超过最强基线两倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统专家发现仅按话题专长排序人员，但许多职位（尤其团队领导者）需要更多元的素质：领导者应了解项目技术领域但不能过度专精、具备较强管理知识、且能与团队良好沟通。单一话题专长无法刻画这些要求。

Method: 将领导者发现问题形式化：查询为项目与团队，输出候选领导者排序列表。在概率框架下将技术契合度、管理知识、与团队的沟通能力三者结合建模，并为每种素质分别提出一个评价指标。

Result: 在基于Stack Overflow构建的两个大规模数据集上，模型最佳配置的NDCG@10达到最强基线的两倍以上。

Conclusion: 领导者的排序必须平衡多种素质，这些素质是仅凭话题专长无法捕捉的；领导者发现可作为专家发现的重要扩展方向。

Abstract: Expert finding ranks people by their knowledge of a given topic. Many job positions need more than topical expertise, and a team leader is a clear case. A suitable leader knows the technical areas of the project without being overqualified, has strong managerial knowledge, and communicates well with the team. We study the leader finding problem, where the query is a project and a team and the output is a ranked list of candidate leaders. We model the problem in a probabilistic framework that combines technical fit, managerial knowledge, and communication with the team, and we propose one evaluation metric for each of these qualities. On two large collections built from Stack Overflow, the best configuration of our model more than doubles the NDCG@10 of the strongest baseline. These results show that a ranking of leaders must balance several qualities that topical expertise alone does not capture.

</details>


### [301] [Deep Cross-Division Network for Product-and-Ratio Feature Interactions in Industrial Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27514)
*Mikhail Trapeznikov,Sofia Gulevskaia,Maksim Utushkin,Aleksandr Poslavsky,Alexander D'yakonov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DCDN在DCNv2交叉层中加入除法分支，可联合学习乘积、比值及混合交互，理论上等价于Laurent多项式，并在短视频生产推荐系统中通过A/B测试显著提升互动指标，同时去除人工比值特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DCN/DCNv2的交叉层只用输入特征的非负整数幂构造交互，最高阶受网络深度限制，无法直接表示比值、比率、归一化计数所需的倒数项，除非人工以输入形式提供。这类人工比值特征带来维护与泛化负担。

Method: 提出DCDN（Deep Cross-Division Network）：在DCNv2风格交叉层基础上增加除法分支，联合学习乘积、比值和乘积-比值混合交互。将可表示交互刻画为多元Laurent多项式，并分析K层DCDN可生成ℓ1阶至多K+1、带符号次数在[-K, K+1]范围内的Laurent单项式。

Result: 与基于DCNv2并人工构造比值特征的生产级神经排序基线对比，用DCDN替换DCNv2块后可完全移除人工比值特征，输入特征数减少40.1%。线上A/B测试：点赞+3.25%、分享+16.26%、总观看时长TVT+0.33%、点击全屏+0.38%（均统计显著），展示视频数略降0.24%。

Conclusion: DCDN能以端到端方式建模比值类交互，在提升用户互动的同时摆脱对人工比值特征的依赖，已部署于大型短视频服务生产排序栈，代码开源。

Abstract: Learning effective feature interactions is essential for industrial recommender systems. DCN and DCNv2 efficiently automate polynomial feature crossing. However, their cross layers construct interactions using only non-negative integer powers of the input features, with the maximum degree controlled by network depth. Consequently, they cannot directly represent the reciprocal terms required for ratios, rates, and normalized counts unless such features are explicitly provided as inputs. We introduce DCDN, a Deep Cross-Division Network that augments a DCNv2-style cross layer with a division branch. DCDN jointly learns product, ratio, and mixed product-ratio interactions. We characterize the representable interactions as multivariate Laurent polynomials and show that a $K$-layer DCDN generates Laurent monomials of $\ell_1$-order up to $K+1$, with signed degree between $-K$ and $K+1$. We evaluate DCDN against a production-optimized neural-ranking baseline built around DCNv2 and supplied with manually engineered ratio features. Replacing the DCNv2 block with DCDN allowed us to eliminate the entire class of manually engineered ratio features from the model input, reducing the number of input features by $40.1\%$. DCDN is deployed in the production ranking stack of a major short-video service. In a live A/B test on this service, DCDN increased likes by $3.25\%$, shares by $16.26\%$, total view time (TVT) by $0.33\%$, and clicks to open fullscreen by $0.38\%$; all changes were statistically significant. The number of shown videos decreased slightly ($-0.24\%$). These results demonstrate that DCDN can improve user engagement while eliminating reliance on manually engineered ratio features. Our code is publicly released at https://github.com/rw404/DCDN.

</details>


### [302] [Decision Columns as an Index: Interpretable Embeddings for Explainable Entity Matching and Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27910)
*Aadharsh Kannan,Robert Bruckner,Philip A. Bernstein*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出"决策列"可解释嵌入：每列由决策模型对自然语言问题给出 yes/no/not stated 的校准答案，记录向量即问题答案序列，相似度可精确分解到每个问题，新增问题只加坐标不改旧坐标。在公开产品基准上，其检索效果与稠密嵌入统计相当，同时为匹配提供精确解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业务数据系统需要判断记录是否指向同一实体、并检索满足"隐含关键属性"的请求，且因代理会依据记录行动，两任务都需可审计的理由。稠密嵌入虽效果好但坐标不透明、无法按语义过滤、新增属性需重编码整个语料。

Method: 以决策列为维度的可解释嵌入：每维存储决策模型对某自然语言问题的校准回答（yes/no/not stated）。记录嵌入为有序问题集上的答案序列，相似度精确分解为逐问题贡献，可无破坏性追加新问题。另用小型神经网络从现有稠密嵌入预测完整可解释向量，实现语料回填；在检索-验证式实体匹配器中与稠密候选生成对比，并将检索失败转化为新问题。

Result: 产品基准上，问题空间检索与稠密候选生成统计上无显著差异，且对接受匹配给出精确解释；由检索失败派生的问题使 MRR 提升三倍且无需重编码；搜索中推断"合适结果的属性"优于仅编码字面请求，问题空间与稠密分数融合有提升但未达预设置信阈值；单个小网络以约 1/200 成本从数千标注行回填语料。

Conclusion: 可解释嵌入可作为一种互补、可读的检索通道，用于解释匹配、按属性过滤，并随失败暴露缺失事实而扩展索引；其检索性能接近稠密方法，成本低且支持持续演进。

Abstract: Business data systems must determine whether records refer to the same entity and retrieve records satisfying requests whose decisive properties are implicit. As agents act on these records, both tasks require auditable reasons. Dense embeddings perform well but offer opaque coordinates, cannot be filtered by meaning, and require corpus re-encoding to add new properties. We propose interpretable embeddings built from decision columns: each column stores a decision model's calibrated answer to a natural-language question, with outcomes yes, no, or not stated. A record's embedding is its answers across an ordered set of questions. Similarity decomposes exactly into per-question contributions, and new questions add coordinates without altering existing ones.
  On public product benchmarks, question-space retrieval matches dense candidate generation statistically in a retrieve-then-verify entity matcher, while providing exact explanations for accepted matches. Questions derived from retrieval failures triple mean reciprocal rank without re-encoding prior answers. For search, inferring the attributes of a suitable result outperforms encoding only the literal request, and combining question-space and dense scores improves retrieval, though not at our prespecified confidence threshold. A single small neural network predicts the full interpretable vector from an existing dense embedding, enabling corpus backfilling from a few thousand labeled rows at roughly 1/200 the cost. We position interpretable embeddings as a complementary, readable retrieval channel for explaining matches, filtering on attributes, and extending indexes as failures expose missing facts.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [303] [On the loss landscape of the linear neural network: beyond critical points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26671)
*Antoine Lejay,Lionel Lenotre,Sa{ï}d Toubra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出"临界谷线"(critical thalweg)这一中间尺度结构，统一刻画线性神经网络损失景观中的全部临界点，借助更弱的假设几何地重证Baldi & Hornik的结论，并可二维可视化；实验表明梯度下降被吸引至该谷线，沿其下降至全局最小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性网络损失景观的临界点自Baldi & Hornik以来已被充分研究，但其中间/全局尺度的结构描述仍缺失；而大量文献表明梯度下降能绕过临界点，因此需要一种能同时涵盖临界点与全局走向的尺度刻画。

Method: 提出名为critical thalweg的中间结构，包含所有临界点并具备关键几何性质；在比既有文献更弱的假设下给出Baldi & Hornik结果的几何证明；构造可在二维平面上可视化的全局景观视图，并结合实验观察梯度下降的动态轨迹。

Result: 几何地重证了Baldi & Hornik的临界点刻画结论且假设更弱；获得可二维可视化的全局景观描述；实证显示梯度下降被吸引向临界谷线，并沿该结构持续下降直至全局最小。

Conclusion: 临界谷线为线性网络损失景观提供了中间尺度的统一几何框架，既说明临界点并不阻碍优化，也解释了梯度下降绕过临界点、最终收敛到全局最小的行为。

Abstract: While the critical points of the loss landscape in linear neural networks have been extensively studied since the seminal work of Baldi \& Hornik in the late 1980s, a description of this landscape at an intermediate or global scale is still lacking. Such a characterization is crucial, particularly as a substantial body of literature indicates that gradient descent circumvents critical points. The present article introduces an intermediate structure, termed the critical thalweg, which encompasses all critical points and exhibits key geometric properties. Beyond providing a geometric proof of Baldi \& Hornik's results using simpler assumptions than the existing literature, this framework offers a global view of the loss landscape that can be easily visualized in a two-dimensional plane. Empirical observations suggest that gradient descent is attracted toward the critical thalweg, along which the loss continues to decrease to its global minimum.

</details>


### [304] [Recurrent Amplification and Exact Nonexpansiveness Criteria for Query-Driven Erasure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27109)
*Haozhe Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QED 线性注意力更新（rank-one 更新 + 与 key 正交的 query 分量）在逐元素衰减下可保持单步转移特征值不变，但周期乘积谱半径仍可能大于 1，导致迭代不稳定；作者给出周期构造、随机采样下均方增长充分条件及 O(d) 单步非扩张谱范数判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QED 的"擦除方向"修正在单步谱性质上看似安全（特征值不变），但线性注意力的稳定性由多步转移的有序乘积决定，单步谱半径小于 1 并不能保证长序列稳定，需要系统刻画这种"单步安全、多步爆炸"的风险并给出可高效验证的判据。

Method: 1）在标量衰减下分析 rank-one 擦除转移，证明修正不改变各同质状态转移的特征值，扰动传播由有序乘积决定；2）构造固定 key/query 投影的周期 token 序列，使每步谱半径 <1 而周期乘积谱半径 >1；3）将该构造推广为独立均匀 token 采样下的均方增长充分条件；4）对 channel-wise 衰减推出 O(d) 时间的必要充分谱范数非扩张检验，并在 1,800 个算子上与 SVD 对比。

Result: 提出可显式构造的不稳定反例，说明单步谱判据不足；O(d) 判据与直接 SVD 在全部 1,800 个算子上结果一致；在 Qwen3.5-0.8B-Base 选定头的反事实回放中（特征与 checkpoint gate 轨迹固定），4,096 token 重复文本序列的终端增益由 0.99938 升至 1.3977，证实该机制在真实模型中可触发增益放大。

Conclusion: QED 的 rank-one 擦除修正在单步层面是谱保守的，但多步有序乘积可导致不稳定乃至均方增长；实际模型头中已出现显著增益放大，说明需以多步/乘积级判据评估稳定性。所给 O(d) 判据为高效实用的替代检测手段。

Abstract: The Query-derived Erase Direction (QED) augments a rank-one linear attention update with a key-orthogonal query component. Under scalar decay, this correction leaves the eigenvalues of each homogeneous state transition unchanged, while the propagation of an initial perturbation depends on the ordered product of these transitions. We construct a periodic token sequence with fixed key and query projections for which every one-step spectral radius is below one but the spectral radius of the period product exceeds one. The same construction yields a sufficient condition for mean-square growth under independent uniform token sampling. For rank-one erasure with channel-wise decay, we derive a necessary and sufficient spectral-norm test for one-step nonexpansiveness that can be evaluated in $O(d)$ time. The test agrees with direct singular value decomposition on all 1,800 operators evaluated. In a counterfactual replay of a selected head of Qwen3.5-0.8B-Base, the terminal gain on a 4,096-token repeated-text sequence increased from 0.99938 to 1.3977 with the feature and checkpoint gate trajectories held fixed.

</details>


### [305] [Parasite Features: Causal Abstraction Through Spurious Pathways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26711)
*Denis Sutter,Qing Yao,Sasha Boguraev,Julian Minder,Antonio Di Maio,Thomas Hofmann,Kyle Mahowald,Tiago Pimentel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 因果抽象（Causal Abstraction）中可存在一类"寄生特征"（parasite features）：模型表征空间中一个方向编码输入特征、另一个方向驱动输出，二者组合的路径可被线性对齐方法（如DAS）错误地当作模型实现了该特征，从而通过等价性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果抽象被广泛用作神经网络与算法之间等价性的判据，是机制可解释性的重要框架，但其等价性检验是否可靠尚缺乏批判性考察。

Method: 作者从理论上构造并论证一类"寄生特征"：表征空间中方向(i)按某特征区分输入、方向(ii)将模型推向特定输出，经这些方向的组合路径即可被因果抽象方法仿效，造成模型实现了该特征的假象；实验上用 Distributed Alignment Search（DAS）配合线性对齐映射，在句法一致性和两位数加法两个任务上检验该现象。

Result: 实验表明 DAS 在这两个任务中确实发现了寄生特征，即所判定的"等价"可由上述虚假路径解释，而非模型真正实现了对应算法。

Conclusion: 因果抽象类方法的等价性检验易受寄生特征干扰，论文提出这一重要考量，为机制可解释性的最佳实践讨论提供警示。

Abstract: Causal Abstraction defines a notion of \emph{equivalence} between a neural network and an algorithm, thus serving as a powerful framework for mechanistic interpretability. In this paper, however, we show the existence of a class of \emph{spurious features} which straightforwardly pass such equivalence tests. Suppose there are two directions in a model's representation space where: (i) one discriminates the input based on some feature, and (ii) the other steers the model towards a certain output. Interpretability methods based on Causal Abstraction (e.g., Distributed Alignment Search) can \emph{spuriously} simulate these directions' combined behavior by leveraging pathways passing through them, giving the impression that the model implements this feature. In such cases, we say the method discovered a \defn{parasite feature}. Experimentally, we show that Distributed Alignment Search finds parasite features using linear alignment maps in two tasks: syntactic agreement and two-digit addition. Our paper highlights an important consideration for Causal Abstraction and contributes to the ongoing debate about best practices for mechanistic interpretability.

</details>


### [306] [Cross-Attack Robust Aggregation for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26731)
*Eyhab Al-Masri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedTether 通过对客户端更新相对坐标中位数的偏差施加带死区的鲁棒评分进行重加权，在多种数据集、攻击类型和最高40%拜占庭比例下均保持顶级性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许IoT/边缘设备在不交换原始数据的情况下训练共享模型，但被攻陷或故障设备会污染全局模型。现有鲁棒聚合器各有失效情形：FedAvg在高幅攻击下崩溃，Krum/中位数/截断均值在非IID数据上损失精度且易受隐蔽攻击。

Method: 提出FedTether：以客户端更新相对坐标中位数的偏差为基础，设计鲁棒且带死区（dead-zone）的评分函数，并将该重加权作为服务器端优化器的一个步骤来聚合客户端更新。

Result: 在三个数据集、三种攻击类型、拜占庭比例最高40%的设置下，FedTether是唯一在所有情形均达顶级的方方法：隐蔽攻击下与FedAvg持平且无鲁棒性代价，比鲁棒基线最多高42个精度点，在高幅攻击使FedAvg近乎随机时仍保持准确；在50客户端部分参与、无共享数据或验证集条件下依然成立。

Conclusion: FedTether 在广泛攻击与非IID场景下统一了鲁棒性与精度，且具备良好的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) lets resource-constrained IoT and edge devices train a shared model without exchanging raw data, but compromised or faulty devices can poison the global model. Existing robust aggregators each fail in some regime: naive averaging (FedAvg) collapses under high-magnitude attacks, whereas distance- and statistics-based defenses (Krum, median, trimmed mean) sacrifice accuracy on non-IID data and remain vulnerable to stealthy attacks. We present FedTether, which reweights client updates by their deviation from a coordinatewise median through a robust, dead-zoned score applied as a server-side optimizer step. Across three datasets, three attack types, and Byzantine fractions up to 40%, FedTether is the only method top-tier in every regime: it matches FedAvg under stealthy attacks with no robustness tax, exceeds robust baselines by up to 42 accuracy points, and stays accurate where high-magnitude attacks reduce FedAvg to near-random. These properties persist at 50 clients under partial participation, with no shared data or validation set.

</details>


### [307] [Depth-Linked Residual Tokens for Multimodal Retrieval and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26752)
*Jiangfan Zhang,Wentao Bao,Xian Wu,Xiangjun Fan,Jianpeng Cheng,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出深度链接残差令牌DepThink：在单次预填充前向中追加K个可学习残差令牌，通过轻量残差递归耦合其隐藏状态，兼顾循环架构的序列精炼能力与并行令牌的高效推理，在多模态检索与生成上超越基线并大幅加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代式循环架构（如looped语言模型）参数效率与推理能力强，但顺序迭代带来巨大计算延迟；而并行可学习特殊令牌（如ViT中的register token）支持单次高效推理，却缺乏序列精炼且令牌表征冗余。作者希望兼得二者优势。

Method: DepThink在单次预填充前向传播中追加一串K个可学习残差令牌，各令牌的隐藏状态通过轻量、可学习的残差递归相互耦合，使每个残差令牌逐步捕获前序令牌遗漏的互补信息。理论上证明其相较标准可学习令牌具有严格扩展的几何方向可达性，并保证可证明的一步下降优势。

Result: 在多模态检索与生成任务上，DepThink持续优于可学习令牌基线，并在密集(2B、8B)与稀疏MoE(30B-A3B)骨干、LoRA与全参数微调两种设置下达到或超过循环架构；相比循环架构训练加速最高11.9倍、推理加速4.3倍。

Conclusion: DepThink通过残差递归耦合的并行令牌设计，在单次前向内实现序列化信息累积，兼顾效果与效率，为多模态检索与生成提供了可扩展的高效替代方案。

Abstract: Iterative architectures such as looped language models improve parameter efficiency and reasoning capacity of transformers, yet their sequential backbone iterations introduce substantial computational latency. Conversely, parallelizable learnable special tokens (such as the register tokens in Vision Transformer) enable efficient single-pass inference, but often suffer from the absence of sequential refinement and exhibit redundant representations across tokens. To address these challenges, we introduce Depth-Linked Residual Tokens (DepThink), an efficient framework designed for multimodal retrieval and generation. Operating within a single pre-filling forward pass, DepThink appends a sequence of K learnable residual tokens whose hidden states are coupled through a lightweight, learnable residual recurrence. This mechanism enables each residual token to progressively capture complementary information missed by preceding tokens. Theoretically, we prove that DepThink provides strictly expanded geometric directional reachability over standard learnable tokens and guarantees a provable one-step descent advantage. We evaluate DepThink across multimodal retrieval and generation tasks. DepThink consistently outperforms learnable-token baselines and matches or exceeds looped architectures across dense (2B, 8B) and sparse mixture-of-experts (30B-A3B) backbones under both LoRA and full-parameter fine-tuning, while accelerating training by up to 11.9 and inference by 4.3 over looped architectures.

</details>


### [308] [Who Should Verify? Reviewer Selection with Partial Information and Outcome Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26770)
*The Viet Bui,Tien Mai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究如何以最小代价证明所选AI审稿程序与最优程序的差距不超过指定值，给出精确停止规则、对数近似算法及紧确难度，并揭示"找证书"可能远贵于证书本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择AI审稿程序需要关于其读稿后行为的证据；在只能购买部分评审与答案键、未观测证据存在多种补全的情形下，需量化"认证所选程序至多落后最优程序若干正确答案"的成本，并区分证书成本与寻找证书的搜索成本。

Method: 固定批次items上在原始答案与采用某审稿者答案间抉择；在评审全可用、仅验证答案键花钱时给出事后最优证书的精确停止规则与整数规划；评审与键均有价格时，基于已完成记录的多项式时间算法，近似最小证书成本达对数因子；构造两程序家族分析精确minimax期望搜索成本；两二元程序在已知iid项目分布下用五类计数计算最优搜索策略；在MMLU、数值报告与新加坡电力预测报告上回放评审。

Result: 对数近似因子在前导阶紧确（除非P=NP）；搜索可能远贵于证书：n个未决项的两程序家族中事后一次购买即可，但精确minimax期望搜索成本为(n+1)/2；MMLU上定向审计比开发数据选定的固定顺序少用6.9%答案键，数值报告少1.2%；与按独立准确率选择相比，所选程序在留出MMLU上提升2.94个百分点、数值报告为0；电力报告上五次检查即认证48份全平局，而各程序仅正确八次。

Conclusion: 论文形式化了审稿程序选择的可认证性，区分证书代价与搜索代价，给出可计算的最优与近似策略，并在真实评审数据上表明定向审计与基于差距的选择能节省验证成本并提升实际表现。

Abstract: Selecting an AI reviewer requires evidence about what it does after reading a draft. On a fixed batch of items, a team can keep the original answers or adopt one reviewer's answers throughout. We ask how much it costs to certify that the chosen procedure falls at most a specified number of correct answers short of the best available procedure, for every completion of the unobserved evidence. When all reviews are available and only verified answer keys cost money, we give an exact stopping rule and an integer program for the cheapest certificate in hindsight. When reviews and keys both have prices, a polynomial-time algorithm using the completed record approximates the minimum certificate cost within a factor logarithmic in the number of procedures. This dependence is tight to leading order unless P equals NP. Finding a certificate can be much more expensive. We exhibit a two-procedure family with $n$ unresolved items where one purchase suffices in hindsight but the exact minimax expected search cost is $(n+1)/2$. For two binary procedures under a known independent and identically distributed item law, five counts suffice to compute an optimal search policy. We replay reviews on 2,000 MMLU questions, 896 numerical reports, and 48 Singapore electricity forecast reports. With reviews available, a targeted audit used 6.9% fewer keys on MMLU and 1.2% fewer on numerical reports than a fixed order selected on development data. Compared with selection by stand-alone accuracy, the chosen procedures gained 2.94 percentage points on held-out MMLU questions and zero on numerical reports. On electricity reports, five checks certified a tie across all 48, yet each procedure was correct only eight times.

</details>


### [309] [Don't Discard Your Rollouts: Reusing Teacher RL Traces for Student Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26803)
*Vashisth Tiwari,Emma Strubell,J. Zico Kolter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将GRPO的RL rollout复用视为数据选择问题：用prompt通过率排序，先用最简单prompt做SFT热启动，再用混合成功prompt做DPO，并以教师log-likelihood筛选与student兼容的completion，实现无额外推理成本、接近标准蒸馏的蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练（PPO/GRPO）采样开销大，每个prompt含多条completion及reward；标准蒸馏却丢弃这些轨迹、重新从最终teacher生成数据。作者希望复用已保存的teacher RL rollout来蒸馏student。朴素SFT效果差，朴素DPO虽有效但对弱student不稳定、易崩溃。

Method: 把rollout复用建模为数据选择问题，利用GRPO中已记录的元数据：按prompt pass rate排序数据，先以最简单prompt做SFT热启动，再用mixed-success prompt做DPO；对每个prompt用保存的teacher log-likelihood作为代理，挑选student兼容的completion。

Result: 在数学推理与Python编程任务、多个模型家族的student上，两阶段配方取得与标准蒸馏相当的平均性能，且数据集构建无额外推理成本。

Conclusion: 已保存的teacher RL rollout可经合理数据选择有效复用于蒸馏，显著缩小与标准蒸馏的差距，并避免不稳定问题，是一种低成本蒸馏方案。

Abstract: RL post-training methods such as PPO and GRPO are computationally expensive, with substantial resources devoted to sampling rollouts, which contain multiple completions per prompt along with their respective rewards. Canonical distillation, however, discards these traces and uses more compute to regenerate data from the final teacher. We ask if and how we can reuse the saved teacher RL rollouts to distil student models. Reusing rollouts is non-trivial: naive SFT on the rollouts underperforms canonical distillation, and naive DPO, while performant, is unstable, collapsing on weaker students. We show that treating rollout reuse as a data selection problem using metadata already recorded during GRPO substantially reduces this gap. We use prompt pass rates to order the data, using the easiest prompts for an SFT warm start prior to using mixed-success prompts for DPO. For each prompt, we use saved teacher log-likelihoods as a proxy for selecting student-compatible completions. Across mathematical reasoning and Python coding tasks, with students from multiple model families, our two-stage recipe achieves comparable average performance to canonical distillation, while incurring no additional inference cost for dataset construction.

</details>


### [310] [LEAN: Library-Based Adaptation for Asynchronous, Federated Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26805)
*Erdong Hu,Zhimin Ding,Yuxin Tang,Chris Jermaine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LEAN：用"basis library + 借还机制"取代LoRA适配器平均，实现完全异步、低通信的联邦微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨设备联邦微调中，各端数据异构、异步加入退出，本地训练的LoRA适配器无法在置换意义下识别，且对差异数据训练的适配器做平均往往无效或不稳定，因此需要放弃平均范式。

Method: 服务器维护一批秩1基对（rank-1 basis pairs）组成"库"；参与者随机借出子集，在本地作为LoRA适配器微调后归还。每个基对在站点间不断被打乱重训，因而覆盖多用户数据；无需任何聚合步骤，仅传输低秩参数，天然支持异步。

Result: 理论上证明同步设定下LEAN收敛至驻点邻域，且该"打乱"在一阶上抵消数据异构；图像与文本微调基准上，多数设置优于平均类基线且通信量显著更低；在平均方法无法达到非平凡精度的强异构场景，以及导致平均方法严重退化的强异步场景下仍然有效。

Conclusion: LEAN以库式参数共享替代聚合，为异构、异步的联邦微调提供了可证明且实证有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of foundation models with low-rank adapters (LoRA) is typically reconciled by averaging locally trained adapters. In cross-device settings, where participants hold heterogeneous data and join or leave asynchronously, averaging is poorly suited: locally trained adapters are not identifiable up to permutation, and averages of adapters trained on dissimilar data can be uninformative or unstable. We propose a simple idea called federated library-based adaptation (LEAN), which replaces averaging altogether. The server maintains a library of rank-1 basis pairs; participants check out random subsets, fine-tune them locally as a LoRA adapter, and check them back in, so that each pair is shuffled across sites and trained on many participants' data. Because no aggregation step is required, LEAN operates fully asynchronously and communicates only low-rank parameters. We prove that, in a synchronous setting, LEAN converges to a neighborhood of a stationary point, with the shuffle canceling data heterogeneity at first order. Across image and text fine-tuning benchmarks, LEAN outperforms averaging-based baselines in most settings with substantially less communication, and remains effective under heavy data heterogeneity, where averaging-based methods fail to reach nontrivial accuracy, and under heavy asynchrony, where they degrade substantially.

</details>


### [311] [NitroGen: Observations on the Action Dynamics, Visual Instruction Following, and Spatial Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26807)
*Shengyuan Lin,Zhiling Li,Haoyu Nie,Ozan K. Tonguz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究开源的视觉-动作基础模型NitroGen（基于在线游戏视频的行为克隆训练），以《空洞骑士》和《赛道狂热》为例，从动作适应、视觉指令跟随和空间探索三个维度评估其冻结控制器，发现模型能适应局部情境，但缺乏长时程游戏控制所需的规划、失误恢复与探索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对通用视觉-动作基础模型在真实、长时程游戏控制中能力边界缺乏系统刻画，尤其需要明确"冻结控制器"在动作适应、指令跟随与探索三方面的表现与短板。

Method: 在《空洞骑士》与《赛道狂热》两个游戏上，以冻结的NitroGen控制器为对象，沿三个维度（动作适应、视觉指令跟随、空间探索）进行评测分析。

Result: NitroGen能够根据局部情境调整动作，但在规划、从失误中恢复以及空间探索方面表现不足。

Conclusion: 行为克隆得到的视觉-动作基础模型具备良好的局部反应能力，但尚不足以支撑长时程游戏控制，亟需在规划、恢复与探索能力上改进。

Abstract: NitroGen is an open vision-action foundation model for game control. It is trained by behavior cloning on online gameplay videos. In this paper, we study the frozen controller in Hollow Knight and Trackmania as examples along three dimensions: action adaptation, visual instruction following, and spatial exploration. Our results indicate that NitroGen can adapt its actions to local context, but it lacks the planning, recovery, and exploration needed for longer-horizon game control.

</details>


### [312] [Realistic Robustness Evaluation of Tabular Data Driven Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26818)
*Miguel Silva,Eva Maia,João Vitorino,Isabel Praça*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AURORA是一个面向表格数据分类的模块化开源对抗鲁棒性评估工具，统一集成多种扰动方法与评估指标，并生成可视化鲁棒性分析报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像和文本领域的对抗样本研究已较充分，但表格数据（在现实系统中广泛使用）的对抗鲁棒性研究相对不足，缺乏系统化的评估工具。同时，现有评估易产生不切实际的对抗样本，影响结论可靠性。

Method: 提出AURORA开源框架，集成多种对抗扰动方法与评估指标；核心为评估报告，量化性能退化、比较不同扰动策略的影响、识别特征与输入区域层面的脆弱性，并配以可视化产物；引入距离调整机制，基于特征级约束对不合理对抗样本施加惩罚。

Result: 提供一个统一框架可系统评估模型在对抗条件下的行为，报告能揭示跨特征和输入区域的脆弱模式，并通过距离调整保证鲁棒性评估基于现实扰动、更具可靠性。

Conclusion: AURORA为表格数据分类提供了实用、可解释且更贴近现实的对抗鲁棒性评估方案，弥补了该领域工具缺失，有助于高风隖应用中ML模型的安全性分析。

Abstract: The increasing adoption of Machine Learning (ML) models in high-stakes applications has raised critical concerns regarding their robustness against adversarial attacks. While extensive research has focused on adversarial example generation in image and text domains, the study of such examples in tabular data remains comparatively limited, despite its widespread use in real-world systems. This work introduces AURORA, a modular, open-source tool for evaluating the robustness of ML models on tabular data classification. It integrates multiple adversarial perturbation methods and evaluation metrics within a unified framework to systematically assess model behavior under adversarial conditions. Central to AURORA is its evaluation report, which focuses on robustness analysis by quantifying performance degradation, comparing the impact of different perturbation strategies, and identifying model vulnerabilities across features and input regions. The report is complemented by visual artifacts that facilitate the interpretability of robustness patterns. Additionally, AURORA incorporates a distance adjustment mechanism that penalizes implausible adversarial samples based on feature-level constraints, ensuring that the robustness assessment remains grounded in realistic perturbations and is therefore more reliable.

</details>


### [313] [Graph-XGB: A Hybrid GNN Framework for Outdoor-to-Indoor 5G Signal Reconstruction via Aerial-Only UAV RF Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26828)
*David Hason Rudd,Eunkwang Shin,Nithiwat Pattrapong,Huan Huo,Xiangmeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Graph-XGB混合推断框架，用UAV外部测量反推室内3D信号状态，实现室外到室内的覆盖预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O2I衰减在5G中频、mmWave及6G sub-THz下高达60-80 dB，室内3D信号无法从受限的外部飞行路径直接采样，构成根本性反演难题；UAV虽提供可扩展外部RF感知，但缺少将外部边界测量映射到室内的方法。

Method: 将空间拓扑推理与非线性信号回归解耦：用Floor-Aware 3D Distance度量与Bridge Edge Guarantees构建拓扑感知图，改进SpiderGNN编码器将UAV边界测量传播为128维室内嵌入；PCA压缩至10维（保留96.6%方差），与原始空间特征融合，由独立的物理定制XGBoost回归器解码，避免平滑路径损耗与波动干扰指标间的优化冲突。

Result: 在单建筑10层、613个验证节点的真实5G NR数据集上，Graph-XGB为对比架构中精度最高，RSSI的R²=0.759、RSRP的R²=0.770，较SimpleGNN基线分别提升35.5%和64.9%；且不使用任何室内RF测量作为输入（室内观测仅作训练监督，在留出内部节点上评估）。RSRQ、SNR、CQI等干扰相关目标精度显著低于路径损耗类指标。

Conclusion: 拓扑推理与信号回归的解耦有效，纯空间推断可较好重建路径损耗类指标，但干扰依赖型指标受限于空间信息的可达边界。

Abstract: Delivering robust In-Building Coverage (IBC) is critically impeded by severe Outdoor-to-Indoor (O2I) attenuation, a constraint that intensifies as deployments move from 5G mid-band towards mmWave and prospective 6G sub-THz carriers, where losses of 60-80 dB have been reported across modern building envelopes. While Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) offer scalable exterior RF sensing platforms, a fundamental inversion problem persists: interior 3D signal states cannot be directly sampled from constrained exterior flight paths. This paper proposes Graph-XGB, a hybrid inference framework that decouples spatial topology reasoning from non-linear signal regression to resolve this challenge. A modified SpiderGNN encoder, operating on a topology-aware graph constructed with a physically grounded Floor-Aware 3D Distance metric and Bridge Edge Guarantees, propagates UAV boundary measurements into 128-dimensional indoor embeddings. Principal Component Analysis (PCA) compresses these into a 10-dimensional manifold retaining 96.6% of embedding variance, fused with raw spatial features and decoded by independent, physics-tailored XGBoost regressors. This decoupling mitigates the optimization conflict between smoothly decaying path-loss signals and volatile interference-dependent metrics. Evaluated on a real-world 5G NR dataset of 613 verified nodes spanning the 10 floors of a single building, Graph-XGB attains the strongest accuracy among the architectures compared, with $R^2$ of 0.759 (RSSI) and 0.770 (RSRP), improvements of 35.5% and 64.9% over the SimpleGNN baseline, using no interior RF measurement as a model input; indoor observations enter only as training supervision, with evaluation on held-out internal nodes. Interference-dependent targets (RSRQ, SNR and CQI) remain bounded well below the path-loss metrics, delimiting the reach of purely spatial inference.

</details>


### [314] [A Sparsity Analysis of Traffic Forecasting Architectures: How Much Missing Data is Too Much?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26847)
*Mohamed Hamad,Bilal Mutahir,Mohamed Mabrok,Nizar Zorba,Hamid Saeedi,Halim Yanikomeroglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评测了GNN、Transformer与选择性状态空间模型在多变量交通预测中随传感器数据缺失程度变化的鲁棒性，发现精度与鲁棒性存在明显权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通预测模型多在完整数据的理想条件下评估，无法反映现实中传感器故障与通信中断频发的情况，缺乏对推理阶段数据丢失下模型可靠性的系统认识。

Method: 在两个常用的多通道交通基准上，对图神经网络、注意力Transformer和选择性状态空间模型（如Mamba类）进行基准测试；模拟从零缺失到完全传感器丢失的多档稀疏度；采用归一化指标以保证不同交通变量间可比，并定义可用性阈值以判定预测何时不可靠。

Result: 结果呈现明显的精度—鲁棒性权衡：全数据下精度最高的模型在数据稀疏时退化最快，而精度较低的模型表现更稳定；据此提出模型选择应基于传感器网络的预期可靠性而非仅看峰值精度。

Conclusion: 论文提供了一个评估交通预测模型的实用框架，强调实际部署中鲁棒性的重要性，并建议结合传感器可靠性预期进行模型选型。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting models are typically evaluated under ideal conditions with complete data. This evaluation does not reflect the frequent sensor failures and communication outages common in real-world deployments. This paper presents a comprehensive analysis of multivariate traffic prediction models to evaluate their performance under varying levels of sensor data loss during inference. We benchmark several architectural paradigms, including graph neural networks, attention-based Transformers, and selective state-space models, across sparsity levels ranging from zero to complete sensor loss, using two widely used multi-channel traffic benchmarks. To ensure consistent comparison across traffic variables, we use normalized metrics and define a usability threshold to identify when predictions become unreliable. Our results show a clear trade-off between baseline accuracy and robustness: models with the highest accuracy under full-data conditions often degrade most sharply as data becomes sparse, while less accurate models maintain more stable performance. These findings show that model selection should be based on the expected reliability of the sensor network rather than peak accuracy alone. This work provides a practical framework for assessing traffic forecasting models and highlights the importance of robustness in real-world applications.

</details>


### [315] [Self-Supervised Learning with Recursive Quadrature Filters for Neural Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26856)
*Mirko Morello,Shivang Rawat,David J. Heeger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出对递归正交滤波(RQF)编码器的自监督预训练方法，以极少参数在多个EEG基准上接近SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物启发的复值时序滤波器(RQF，属对角状态空间模型)具备可解释的频率/带宽参数，但缺乏针对无标签时序的自监督训练方案，且现有EEG模型参数庞大。

Method: 设计基于预测误差的自监督目标：窗口模式下用当前窗口摘要预测未来窗口摘要；稠密模式下用当前状态预测未来对数功率。编码器先在无标签数据上预训练，再训练读出层(可冻结或微调)。多通道EEG/颅内记录采用共享编码器逐通道处理，结合电极位置做空间读出。
