Result: 四个神经基准上接近SOTA，参数量极小。110k参数系统在TUAB 100s段达0.9247±0.0022 AUROC，超过SAMBA(0.9054，参数多9倍)；10s段0.9040±0.0019，与3.2M–369M参数的EEG基础模型相当。

Conclusion: 生物启发的RQF结合自监督预训练，能以极低参数量取得与大型EEG基础模型相当的性能，凸显高效、可解释架构的潜力。

Abstract: We develop self-supervised methods for training Recursive Quadrature Filter (RQF) encoders on unlabeled time series. RQFs are biologically motivated, complex-valued temporal filters that form a special class of diagonal state-space models (SSMs). Each RQF acts as a band-pass filter whose selectivity is determined by two learnable parameters: its tuning frequency and bandwidth. We separately introduce a self-supervised learning objective based on prediction error; the encoder is trained so that its current state predicts its own future activity. In the windowed mode, the summary of the current window predicts the summary of a later window; in the dense mode, the state at the current timestep predicts future log-power. Each RQF encoder is pretrained on a dataset and then a readout (e.g., a task-specific classifier) is trained on the output of the RQF encoder, with the encoder either frozen or fully fine-tuned. For multichannel EEG and intracranial recordings, a shared encoder processes each channel separately, and a spatial readout combines the channel representations using electrode locations. Across four neural benchmarks using scalp or intracranial EEG, the pretrained RQF system performs close to the state of the art while using a small fraction of the parameters of the competing models. For example, a 110k-parameter RQF system achieves 0.9247 +/- 0.0022 test AUROC on 100-s TUAB segments, above SAMBA (0.9054) at the same context with 9 times as many parameters, and 0.9040 +/- 0.0019 on 10-s segments, comparable to EEG foundation models with 3.2M-369M parameters.

</details>


### [316] [Multi-Tier Edge Triage: Optimizing Latency for Safe Clinical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26914)
*Joshua Broyde,Sarita A. Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"多层边缘分诊"(Multi-Tier Edge Triage)架构：在临床Agent本地主机部署紧凑的Tier-1序列分类器，快速处理明确合规查询，仅将模糊查询升级至云端LLM裁判，在满足延迟约束下实现接近云LLM的多维安全合规筛查效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业临床工作流需在交互延迟约束下执行多维安全与合规策略（HIPAA PHI防护、临床执业范围、患者阅读水平、品牌完整性）；但同步调用云端LLM裁判会带来显著网络与解码开销，拖慢关键响应路径。

Method: 采用两层分级筛查：Tier-1为与Agent同机部署的紧凑序列分类器，在进程内解决清晰查询，模糊查询才升级至Tier-2云端LLM裁判。为排除长度与unigram捷径，基于MedSafetyBench、ASQ-PHI及合成的四类临床助手策略(Generic HealthCorp)构建四个长度匹配的对比孪生基准；并对本地编码器进行端到端表示微调（335M BGE-Large分类器）。

Result: 冻结本地编码器在结构化PHI检测上与云模型相当（≥98.3%准确率，≤23.5ms），但在语义安全和对称黑名单提示上明显退化。微调后BGE-Large达98.90%折外准确率（零样本云LLM为99.20%，McNemar p=0.648，零共享错误），召回97.8%–98.2%，过度拒答率0.0%–0.4%，耗时39.0ms（比云LLM评估快>14倍）。分策略误差分析：PHI与品牌召回100%，临床范围98.7%–99.3%，残余误差集中于阅读水平检查（93.3%–95.3%）。

Conclusion: 经表示微调的紧凑本地分类器可媲美云端LLM裁判，并大幅降低延迟；剩余误差集中在阅读水平判断，适合通过针对性的多层升级机制解决，从而验证了边缘分诊架构的可行性。

Abstract: Deploying generative AI agents in enterprise clinical workflows requires enforcing multi-dimensional safety and compliance policies--preventing HIPAA Protected Health Information (PHI) exposure, maintaining clinical scope-of-practice boundaries, ensuring patient-appropriate reading comprehension, and preserving brand integrity--under interactive latency constraints. Synchronous outbound calls to cloud Large Language Model (LLM) judges add substantial network and decoding overhead onto the critical response path. We present Multi-Tier Edge Triage, a safety screening architecture that co-locates a compact Tier-1 sequence classifier on the clinical agent's host server, resolving clear queries in-process without a network round-trip and escalating only ambiguous queries to a Tier-2 cloud LLM judge. To evaluate Tier-1 encoders without length or unigram shortcuts, we construct four length-matched contrastive twin benchmarks seeded from MedSafetyBench, ASQ-PHI, and a synthetic four-mandate clinical assistant policy (Generic HealthCorp). While frozen local encoders match cloud models on structured PHI detection (>= 98.3% accuracy in <= 23.5 ms), they degrade markedly on semantic safety and symmetric-blocklist prompts. However, end-to-end representation fine-tuning closes the multi-policy accuracy gap: a fine-tuned 335M-parameter BGE-Large classifier achieves 98.90% out-of-fold accuracy versus 99.20% for a zero-shot cloud LLM judge (exact McNemar p = 0.648, zero shared errors), 97.8%-98.2% recall, and a 0.0%-0.4% over-refusal rate in 39.0 ms (>14x faster than cloud LLM evaluation on our benchmark host). Per-policy error analysis reveals ceiling local recall on PHI (100.0%), brand (100.0%), and clinical scope (98.7%-99.3%) violations, isolating residual errors to reading-level checks (93.3%-95.3%) and motivating targeted multi-tier escalation.

</details>


### [317] [Large Reported CHB-MIT Adaptation Gains Disappear with Pretrained Encoders and Matched Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26929)
*Joseph Sankoorikal Johny,Antony J. Sankoorikal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文复现并审计Dani & Liebe (2026)关于"末层编码器SSL适配大幅提升CHB-MIT癫痫检测"的结论，发现原发布代码存在预训练权重未加载、探针含dropout等缺陷，修正后适配增益基本消失（BIOT -0.6点，CBraMod +0.01）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DL报告末层编码器SSL适配比线性探针提升36.2（BIOT）和29.5（CBraMod）个ROC-AUC点，幅度异常大，需验证其可复现性与稳健性，并顺带审计被至少14篇论文共用的CHB-MIT评测协议。

Method: 用DL开源代码与独立流水线复现；修正"仅当提供默认未设配置键时才加载预训练权重"及"CBraMod探针在dropout激活下读取重建头"两处缺陷，改为预训练初始化+评估模式下编码器输出探针；在DL切分与CHB-MIT、Siena的5个受试者轮换上各评测18种设置（30套超参、至多500轮、验证集早停），并做依赖校正推断。

Result: 修正后BIOT增益-0.6点（95%区间[-3.8,+2.6]），CBraMod +0.01（[-0.02,+0.04]）；18个比较平均效应-1.1至+5.7点，12/18在±3点等价，6项未定；轮换区间依赖校正后无一正效应区间排除零；两个约+21点的固定探针增益在同调参下大幅缩水。未找到能整体复现DL表1的配置。

Conclusion: DL报告的大幅适配增益主要由代码缺陷与探针调参不足造成，稳健证据不支持该收益；±3点为分析阈值非临床界值；结论限于所测配置，且编码器级探针等选择为事后决定；共享的CHB-MIT协议亦需审慎对待。

Abstract: Dani and Liebe (2026) (DL) report that adapting the final encoder block of BIOT and CBraMod with self-supervised learning (SSL) improves CHB-MIT seizure-detection ROC-AUC by 36.2 and 29.5 points over linear probing. We investigate these results with their released code and an independent pipeline. The release loads pretrained weights for adaptation only when an otherwise unset configuration key is supplied, and its CBraMod probe reads the reconstruction head with dropout active. With pretrained initialisation and a probe on the encoder output in evaluation mode, the fixed-recipe adaptation gain on DL's split is -0.6 points for BIOT (95% interval [-3.8, +2.6]) and +0.01 for CBraMod ([-0.02, +0.04]) across five seeds. Across DL's split and five subject rotations on CHB-MIT and Siena Scalp EEG, with equally tuned linear probes (30 configurations, up to 500 epochs, validation-based early stopping), mean adaptation effects range from -1.1 to +5.7 ROC-AUC points over 18 comparisons (related settings and feature variants). Twelve of 18 support equivalence within +/-3 points under dependence-adjusted inference (8 of 12 rotation and 4 of 6 fixed-split comparisons); six are unresolved. After correcting rotation intervals for dependence, no positive effect has a 95% interval excluding zero. The +/-3 margin is an analysis threshold, not a clinically validated one, and smaller margins support fewer equivalences. Two fixed-probe gains near +21 points shrink under the same tuning, with smaller benefits unresolved. We also audit a CHB-MIT protocol shared by at least 14 papers. No tested configuration reproduces DL Table 1 as a whole. These findings concern the tested configurations; the encoder-level probe and subsequent checks were chosen post hoc.

</details>


### [318] [Improving the Optimisation Landscape for Particle Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26931)
*Yan Lin,David T. Frazier,Paula Cordero Encinar,Chris J. Oates*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明混合预测似然不适合梯度优化（粒子可能被"饿死"无训练信号），提出个体风险与混合风险的凸组合正则化，在贝叶斯后验计算与LLM低秩适配器集成两个案例中验证其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计与机器学习中常用等权混合（粒子集成）表示预测分布，并以混合预测似然训练。但混合似然无法识别混合在各粒子间如何分解，导致与数据不匹配的粒子得不到梯度信号（训练信号"饥饿"），因此需要一种适合梯度优化的替代目标。

Method: 证明混合预测似然在梯度优化下的病态性；提出个体风险（各粒子平均对数损失）与混合风险的凸组合正则化风险；从粒子层面分析，该正则项等价于对粒子责任度施加 reverse-KL 障碍，保证任何粒子都不被饿死。案例一：用平均场朗之万动力学计算面向预测的后验；案例二：在4个骨干模型、8个多项选择推理基准上联合微调低秩适配器集成。

Result: 案例一中，仅在采用所提正则化时数值表现才稳定良好；案例二中，直接最小化混合风险会导致责任度饥饿，其严重程度随骨干模型能力提升而减轻，而正则化估计器在所有骨干模型上均取得更低的留出混合风险。

Conclusion: 混合预测似然不利于梯度优化，凸组合正则化通过 reverse-KL 障碍防止粒子训练信号饥饿，实践上更稳定且泛化更优，适用于贝叶斯后验近似与大规模模型集成微调。

Abstract: Several methods in statistics and machine learning represent a predictive distribution as an equally weighted mixture of models, or particles, trained via the mixture predictive likelihood. However, we prove that the mixture predictive likelihood is poorly suited to gradient-based optimisation, because it does not identify how the mixture is decomposed across particles, so that a poorly matched particle can be starved of training signal. As a remedy, we study a convex combination between the individual risk, i.e. the average log loss of the individual particles, and the mixture risk. At the particle level, this regularised risk imposes a reverse-KL barrier on the responsibilities of the particles, so that no particle can be starved of training signal. These phenomena are illustrated in two case studies: In the first, a predictively-oriented posterior is computed using mean-field Langevin dynamics, and the numerics are well-behaved only when our regularisation is employed. In the second, ensembles of low-rank adapters are jointly fine-tuned on large language models, across four backbones and eight multiple-choice reasoning benchmarks; direct minimisation of the mixture risk leads to responsibility starvation, whose severity decreases as the competence of the backbone increases, whereas our regularised estimator attains a lower held-out mixture risk on every backbone considered.

</details>


### [319] [CENTRY: A Probabilistic Latent-State Model for Continual AI Risk Estimation, Monitoring, and Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26940)
*Bonny Banerjee,Geetha Raju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出CENTRY概率框架，将AI风险建模为来自数据集、硬件和模型/算法三源的潜变量，通过快慢潜子空间分解实现部署后AI系统的持续风险估计、监控与调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI安全框架主要用于定义、分类或治理风险，缺乏可实现的模型与方法，无法在AI系统整个运行生命周期中持续估计、监控和调控风险。

Method: 构建结构化潜变量模型：潜表示分解为一个刻画AI-环境动态演化的快速交互子空间，以及分别编码数据集、硬件、模型三组件长期风险状态的三个慢速潜子空间。组件风险状态作为潜变量，通过最大化变分证据下界学习，无需每步真实风险标签，可选地借助有证据支持的行为-原因记录进行训练与持续精化。

Result: 在明确假设下给出安全、学习、推断与运行四方面的形式化保证，包括有限时域与渐近风险预算合规、自适应调控的遗憾与收敛界、组件风险评级的相合性与可辨识性，以及检测延迟、误报、遏制与计算复杂度刻画。

Conclusion: CENTRY将持续概率推断、在线风险估计与自适应风险调控统一于可实现架构中，为AI系统全生命周期的风险管理提供了理论与工程基础。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications, yet existing AI safety frameworks primarily define, categorize, or govern AI risk rather than proposing implementable models and methods for continually estimating, monitoring, and regulating risk throughout an AI system's operational lifetime. This paper proposes CENTRY, a probabilistic framework that enables continual risk estimation, monitoring, and regulation of a deployed AI system from its interactions with its environment. The framework models AI risk as arising from three intrinsic sources: datasets, hardware resources, and models/algorithms. A structured latent-variable model is developed in which the latent representation is decomposed into a fast interaction subspace that captures the evolving AI--environment dynamics and three slow latent subspaces that encode the long-term risk states associated with the dataset, hardware, and model components. Rather than requiring a ground-truth risk label at every time step, the component-risk states are treated as latent variables and are learned by maximizing a variational evidence lower bound, optionally informed during training and continual refinement by evidence-supported behavior--cause records. The resulting framework unifies continual probabilistic inference, online risk estimation, and adaptive risk regulation in an implementable architecture. Under the explicit assumptions stated for the corresponding results, we establish formal guarantees spanning safety, learning, inference, and operations, including finite-horizon and asymptotic risk-budget compliance, regret and convergence bounds for adaptive regulation, consistency and identifiability of component-risk ratings, and detection-delay, false-alarm, containment, and computational-complexity characterizations.

</details>


### [320] [Affine-Image Propagation for Tight and Scalable Neural Network Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26978)
*Hong-Ming Chiu,Haoyu Li,Huan Zhang,Richard Y. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 affine-image SDP-CROWN，用仿射像约束近似预激活域并联合优化线性界，通过缩放对角占优代替稠密半正定约束实现 GPU 加速，显著提升非 Lipschitz 正则网络的 ℓ2 验证紧致度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ℓ∞ 对抗的界传播方法有效，但 ℓ2 对抗的紧致可扩展验证仍是开放难题。现有方法在几何上（用逐元素区间或轴对齐椭球包裹预激活集合）和函数上（限制在这些域上构造的线性界）都有损失，导致紧致 ℓ2 验证只适用于这些近似准确的 Lipschitz 正则网络。

Method: 引入 affine-image SDP-CROWN：用仿射像约束更精确地刻画预激活域，并联合优化线性界使其自适应于所得到的域；为规避仿射像表示所需稠密半正定约束的过高计算代价，用缩放对角占优条件替代，从而支持 GPU 加速；方法保持界传播递归结构，可与分支定界结合。

Result: 在五个 MNIST 和 CIFAR-10 的非 Lipschitz 正则网络上，相比 SOTA 方法认证精度提升 17% 到 57.5%；结合分支定界后提升达 27% 到 63.5%。

Conclusion: 通过仿射像域表示与线性界联合优化，并以对角占优松弛替代稠密 SDP，可在保持界传播递归与分支定界兼容性的同时，大幅提升非 Lipschitz 正则网络的 ℓ2 认证紧致度与可扩展性。

Abstract: While bound propagation methods are highly effective for certifying neural networks against $\ell_{\infty}$ adversaries, tight and scalable verification for $\ell_{2}$ adversaries remains a significant open challenge. Existing methods incur geometric loss by enclosing preactivation sets in elementwise intervals or axis-aligned ellipsoids, and functional loss by restricting the linear bounds constructed over those domains. Consequently, tight $\ell_{2}$ verification is currently restricted to Lipschitz-regularized networks where these approximations are accurate. In this paper, we introduce affine-image SDP-CROWN, a novel bound propagation method that better approximates preactivation domains using affine-image constraints and jointly optimizes the linear bound to adapt to the resulting domain. To bypass the prohibitive computational cost of dense semidefinite constraints required by the affine-image representation, we replace them with scaled diagonal-dominance conditions, allowing a GPU-accelerated implementation. Our method preserves the bound propagation recursion and can be integrated with branch-and-bound. On five MNIST and CIFAR-10 non-Lipschitz-regularized networks, our method improves verified accuracy over state-of-the-art methods by 17% to 57.5%. With branch-and-bound, these improvements increase to between 27% and 63.5%.

</details>


### [321] [Distributional Algebro-Geometric Persistence (dAGP): Wasserstein-Stable Polynomial-Relation Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26986)
*Edisy Kin Wai Chan,Andersen Ang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"分布式代数几何持续性"(dAGP)：以矩矩阵谱特征刻画点云分布所满足的低阶代数关系，并给出稳定性理论与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆与四次超椭圆同调等价，但定义方程次数分别为2和4，同调无法区分这种代数差异。作者希望为点云分布建立一种能捕捉"最低阶代数关系方向数量随次数与容差变化"的代数不变量。

Method: 对概率测度μ与m次正交多项式特征映射φ_m，构造矩矩阵G_m(μ)=E[φ_mφ_mᵀ]。其小特征值对应在μ上近似消没的低次多项式方向；统计随(m,ε)变化的近关系方向数目，得到双参数整数签名。并证明μ↦G_m(μ)在1-Wasserstein距离下Lipschitz，给出支撑局部化Lipschitz常数与阈值计数在谱间隔条件下稳定的结论。

Result: 总体噪声底预测与有限样本均值误差0.1%内一致；在选定容差下，关系出现顺序能正确排序圆锥曲线、四次曲线与非代数闭曲线，计数剖面可与二维对照区分；100次重复敏感性实验表明计数网格与两个连续谱基线几乎饱和了四族判别任务。

Conclusion: dAGP为点云分布提供可计算、稳定且具有代数判别力的双参数签名，是点云AGP的总体对应物，并具备定量稳定性保证。

Abstract: A circle and a quartic superellipse have the same homology, but their defining equations have degrees two and four, respectively. We study this algebraic distinction for point-cloud distributions. For a probability measure $μ$ on $Ω=[-1,1]^d$ and an orthonormal polynomial feature map $φ_m$ of degree $m$, the moment matrix $G_m(μ)=\mathbb{E}_{X\simμ}[φ_m(X)φ_m(X)^\top]$ satisfies $c^\top G_m(μ)c=\mathbb{E}_μ[f_c(X)^2]$, so coefficient directions associated with its small eigenvalues represent low-degree polynomials that nearly vanish on $μ$. Counting these near-relation directions across degree $m$ and the tolerance $\varepsilon$ gives a two-parameter integer signature. We call this representation distributional algebro-geometric persistence (dAGP), the population counterpart of the point-cloud algebro-geometric persistence (AGP) construction. For empirical measures, dAGP is the sample-normalised counterpart of point-cloud AGP. We also show that $μ\mapsto G_m(μ)$ is Lipschitz in the $1$-Wasserstein distance, so its ordered eigenvalues vary Lipschitz-continuously under transport of mass, while its threshold counts remain unchanged under perturbations satisfying a spectral-margin condition. We give an explicit support-localised Lipschitz constant and identify the regime in which the resulting guarantee is informative. Experimentally, the population noise-floor prediction agrees with the finite-sample mean to $0.1\%$, within sampling error. At a selected tolerance, relation onset orders the conic, quartic, and non-algebraic closed curves, while their count profiles distinguish them from the two-dimensional control. Calibration identifies an empirical tolerance range. A $100$-repetition sensitivity study quantifies onset recovery and shows that the count grid and two continuous spectral baselines nearly saturate the four-family discrimination task.

</details>


### [322] [JevRisk: Zero-Shot Financial Risk Assessment with a Language Decision Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26999)
*Yaodong Yang,Li Huang,Junpeng Liang,Yi Ma,Hongyao Tang,Xingyu Fan,Jinpeng Li,Furui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JevRisk：用代码计算合规风险指标，再由语言决策模型结合客户画像做零样本金融风险评估，在反洗钱和信贷风险任务上优于规则/无监督方法，媲美千级标注样本的监督模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行需结合客户画像解读金融活动以监控洗钱、违约等风险。现有专家规则指标对所有人用同一阈值、无法个性化；监督模型依赖昂贵缓慢的专家标注或长期还款记录，难以跨客户、跨风险类型泛化。因此需要少依赖标签的零样本决策系统。

Method: 两步法：(1) 依据金融监管指南用代码计算精确的风险指标；(2) 语言决策模型（如 Jev）读取这些指标与客户画像、活动信息，利用广泛世界知识在语境中判断案例并输出高风险评分。

Result: 反洗钱任务用 Scotiabank 公开合成数据，信贷风险任务用 Home Credit 与 Lending Club 真实数据。两类任务上均优于规则与无监督方法，与在约一千名标注客户上训练的监督方法（如 GBDT、TabPFN）持平或更优。

Conclusion: 将可验证的监管指标计算与语言模型的语境推理相结合，可实现几乎无需标签、可跨客户与风险类型泛化的金融风险评估。

Abstract: Banks must monitor every customer's financial risk, such as money laundering and credit default, by interpreting financial activity in the context of the customer's profile. Existing risk monitoring systems of banks are mostly based on expert-designed risk indicators, which apply predefined thresholds to every customer and cannot adapt to their personal situations. Supervised models recognise risk patterns from labelled customers, but each label often needs an expert's review or months of repayment history, which is slow and costly. Financial risk assessment therefore calls for a zero-shot decision system that minimises the need for labels and generalises across customers and risk types. To this end, we present JevRisk, a system for zero-shot financial risk assessment with a language decision model. JevRisk works in two steps. First, following regulatory guidelines in the finance domain, JevRisk computes exact risk indicators with code. Second, the language decision model (e.g., \jev{}) reads these indicators together with the customer's profile and activity, judges the case in context with broad world knowledge, and returns the score of high risk. We evaluate JevRisk on the anti-money laundering task with public synthetic data from Scotiabank and on the credit risk task with real data from Home Credit and Lending Club. On both tasks, JevRisk outperforms rule-based and unsupervised methods, and is on par with or better than supervised methods (e.g., GBDT and TabPFN) trained on about a thousand labelled customers.

</details>


### [323] [Fair Property Tax: Reducing Regressivity in Machine-Learning Valuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27009)
*Nicolas Acevedo Villena,Haihao Lu,Michael Wagner,Timothy Sparer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出并评估两种训练方法（Direct惩罚项与Surrogate加权损失），在保持LightGBM价格尺度预测精度的同时，显著降低房产税评估中机器学习模型的累退性（regressivity），并已部署于库克县评估办公室。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进机器学习模型在房产税评估中预测精度高，但可能系统性地对低价房产给出更高的估值/成交价比率，即"累退性"（regressivity），造成税收不公平，需在现有评估工作流内加以缓解。

Method: 与库克县评估办公室（CCAO）合作，保留其使用的LightGBM模型类，提出两种训练方法：Direct法对对数估值误差与对数售价的协方差平方施加惩罚；Surrogate法采用观测加性构造，导出加权平方误差损失。使用7个时序验证折、留出测试集及2025年前进测试集评估，并以价格相关偏差（PRB）衡量累退性，另做八县基准测试。

Result: 在交叉验证选定的Surrogate设置下，留出销售集PRB降幅约49%，2025年销售集约39%；价格尺度R²基本不变（0.893→0.897，0.904→0.908），对数价格RMSE分别上升3.1%和1.7%；配对自助法90%区间宽度约两个百分点；八县基准中两种方法在所有县均降低累退性，代价是对数价格预测误差上升。

Conclusion: 研究证明可在现有评估机构工作流中实际降低可测量的累退性，同时保持价格尺度预测性能，Direct法已纳入CCAO公开的住宅估值软件。

Abstract: Advanced machine-learning models are increasingly used for property valuation in tax assessment. Although these models can achieve high predictive accuracy, they may systematically assign higher valuation-to-sale ratios to lower-priced properties, a pattern known as regressivity. In collaboration with the Cook County Assessor's Office (CCAO), we develop and evaluate two training methods that substantially reduce this regressive pattern while maintaining price-scale predictive accuracy. The Direct method has been incorporated into CCAO's public residential valuation software. Retaining the LightGBM model class used at CCAO, we propose a Direct penalty on squared covariance between log valuation errors and log sale prices, and an observation-additive Surrogate that yields a weighted squared-error loss. We evaluate both methods in our implementation of the CCAO workflow, using seven chronological validation folds, a held-out test set, and a 2025-forward test set. Measured by the magnitude of price-related bias (PRB), regressivity falls by approximately 49% on held-out sales and 39% on 2025 sales at a Surrogate setting selected by cross-validation, relative to the same LightGBM model fitted without the penalty. Price-scale $R^2$ is essentially unchanged. Its small rise, from 0.893 to 0.897 on held-out sales and from 0.904 to 0.908 on 2025 sales, reflects overall valuation level. Log-price RMSE rises by 3.1% and 1.7%, respectively. Paired sale-bootstrap 90% intervals for the PRB reductions lie within about two percentage points of these estimates. In an additional eight-county benchmark, both methods reduce regressivity in every county, with higher log-price prediction error as the expected tradeoff. These findings demonstrate a practical way to reduce measured regressivity within an existing assessor workflow while preserving price-scale predictive performance.

</details>


### [324] [Ruby: State-of-the-Art Potency Prediction across Discovery Regimes Using Learned Structural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27015)
*Michael Atkin,Dominique Beaini,Jules Berman,Rebecca Boiarsky,Jamin Chen,Alejandro Dobles,Sergey Edunov,Evan Feinberg,Nate Gruver,Will Harvey,Deidre Johns,Nina Jovic,Steven Kearnes,Allison Keys,Rohit Kundu,Ken Leidal,Gerald Lelais,Michael V. Levine,Indrawan McAlpine,Gabriel Monteiro da Silva,Igor Morozov,Pranav Murugan,Adi Nagle,Frank Pickard,Wesley Qian,Gianluca Scarpellini,Ansh Sharma,Richard Strong Bowen,Wojtek Swiderski,Laura Tapias,Carl Tilbury,Cyr Vetcher,Han Wang,William Wang,David Charles Williams,Drausin Wulsin,Han Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Ruby 是一个基于结构基础模型 Pearl 中间表示、无需显式结合构象的通用结合亲和力预测器，并配套发布经审计的公开训练集 GenesisPotency-2026，在命中识别富集、多项公开基准与跨项目零样本预测上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小分子药物发现要经历反复的设计-合成-测试-分析循环，目标是最大化效力同时把其他性质控制在成药范围内。随流程从 hit 发现推进到 hit-to-lead 和先导优化，计算预算变化、靶点实验数据逐渐增多，对化合物排序方法提出不同要求，而现有方法往往需要每个化合物的显式结合构象，难以适配全流程。

Method: 提出 Ruby（Robust Universal Binding affinitY predictor），使用结构基础模型 Pearl 的中间表示作为输入，无需为每个化合物提供显式结合位姿。训练数据由专有数据与 GenesisPotency-2026 组成，后者是经审计、源自公开数据的效力训练集，以 CC BY-SA 4.0 发布并提供行级来源与审计标注。推理时计算投入可调。

Result: 在七个公开命中识别靶点上取得最高的整体早期富集，且可在两个数量级范围内调节推理投入以权衡富集与计算成本；在 OpenFE、CASP16、FEP+ 4、OpenBind 四个公开基准上平均相关性最高；在来自五个进行中 Genesis-Incyte 项目的八个生化与细胞实验中，零样本预测显著优于其他方法，优势比公开基准更大；在允许所有方法用早期实验数据重新校准后，Ruby 的提升幅度仍最大。

Conclusion: 该工作同时贡献了一个带标注的训练资源和一个先进的效力预测器，适用于广泛筛选、同类化学系列内排序，以及随实验数据积累的特定实验预测。

Abstract: Small-molecule drug discovery proceeds through repeated design-make-test-analyze cycles. The goal is often to maximize potency while keeping other properties within the ranges required for a viable therapeutic candidate. Compound prioritization places different demands on computational methods as drug discovery progresses from hit identification through hit-to-lead and lead optimization, with changing computational budgets and increasing amounts of on-target experimental data. We introduce Ruby, a Robust Universal Binding affinitY predictor that uses intermediate representations from the structural foundation model Pearl without requiring an explicit binding pose for every compound. Ruby is trained on proprietary data plus GenesisPotency-2026, an audited public-source potency training dataset released for academic and commercial use under a CC BY-SA 4.0 license with row-level provenance and audit annotations. Ruby achieves the highest aggregate early enrichment across seven public hit-identification targets among evaluated methods, with adjustable inference effort trading off enrichment and computational cost over more than two orders of magnitude. Ruby achieves the highest average correlations across four public benchmarks: OpenFE, CASP16, FEP+ 4, and OpenBind. Ruby significantly outperforms other methods in zero-shot prediction across eight biochemical and cellular assays from five ongoing Genesis-Incyte programs, widening its performance advantage relative to the public benchmarks. Ruby continues to improve more than other methods when all are given the opportunity to recalibrate using earlier assay measurements. Together, these contributions provide an annotated training resource for the field and a state-of-the-art potency predictor for broad screening, prioritization within related chemical series, and assay-specific prediction as experimental measurements accumulate.

</details>


### [325] [Toward a digital twin of the ICU patient: Forecasting and treatment response in a generative event model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27028)
*Zhuoyang Lyu,Yiming Li,Peter C. Hou,Pengyu Hong,Li Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TWICE是一个79M参数的解码器Transformer,在单中心ICU电子病历(127,332患者、15.7亿token)上训练,作为生成式事件模型模拟患者未来轨迹。无需任务特定训练,从50条模拟未来中读取19种临床结局即可匹配144种监督配置中的最佳表现(AUROC 0.836 vs 0.834),生成记录逼真且具患者特异性,但反事实治疗效果方向存在缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICU患者的"数字孪生"可预测病程及治疗会带来何种改变,从而辅助临床决策。生成式事件模型(训练其续写患者EHR)被视为实现此类孪生的可能路径,但需验证其预测能力、生成保真度与反事实有效性。

Method: 训练TWICE——79M参数、仅解码器的Transformer,基于某学术健康系统ICU记录(127,332训练患者、1.57B token)。评估涵盖:临床预测任务(从50条模拟未来读取19项结局,与144种监督配置对比)、生成保真度、个体轨迹,以及预设的反事实验证(药物副作用、惰性药物对照、治疗纠正指标的干预方向)。

Result: 在留出患者上,零样本读取模拟未来的平均AUROC 0.836,与144种监督配置最佳(0.834)相当;在一天内改变治疗的事件上领先,在死亡预测上落后。数据不变时,增加/延长模拟未来比扩大模型6.8倍更有价值。生成记录与真实记录高度吻合且具患者特异性;预测风险随事件临近而上升;能再现药物副作用,对惰性药物几乎无反应。但在治疗本应纠正的指标上,相反治疗与正确治疗的预测方向相似(反事实失败)。

Conclusion: TWICE在ICU事件预测上可与任务特定模型媲美,是迈向ICU数字孪生的一步;但其反事实治疗效果(方向性)不可靠,提示生成式EHR模型作为"孪生"仍需改进因果有效性。

Abstract: A digital twin of a patient in intensive care would forecast the patient's course and what a treatment would change. Generative event models, trained to continue a patient's electronic health record (EHR), have been proposed as a path toward such twins. We trained TWICE, a 79M-parameter decoder-only transformer, on the ICU records of one academic health system (127,332 training patients and 1.57 billion tokens). We evaluated it on clinical prediction tasks, generation fidelity, individual trajectories and counterfactual validation. Reading 19 clinical outcomes off 50 simulated futures, with no task-specific training, TWICE matched the best of 144 supervised configurations on held-out patients (mean AUROC 0.836 vs. 0.834). It led on events that change care within a day and trailed on death. With the training data fixed, more and longer simulated futures were worth more than a 6.8-fold larger model. Its generated records matched real ones closely and were as specific to each patient as its own variability allowed. Its predicted risk rose as events approached. Pre-specified controls showed that it reproduced the side effects of drugs and was barely moved by an inert drug. On the values that treatments are given to correct, however, an opposite treatment moved its forecasts like the right one. TWICE forecasts ICU events about as well as task-specific models, and we see it as a step toward a digital twin of the ICU patient.

</details>


### [326] [Mind World Models: Joint Learning of Motor EEG Representations and Action-Conditioned World Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27029)
*Weikang Qiu,Zheng Huang,Tinglin Huang,Yujun Yan,Rex Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Mind World Model (MWM)，用自然istic EEG 与第一视角视频做无动作标注的预训练：将当前帧前的 EEG 视为世界模型的 latent action，预测未来帧表征，并配以 Lateralization-Aware Transformer 建模左右半球对应。在下游 9 个分类与 7 个回归任务上显著优于最强基线，世界模型对真实动作条件化的利用效率也远高于逆动力学基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 EEG 运动解码依赖成本高的提示式试次与粗粒度类别标签，或采用无法直接刻画"运动如何展开"的预训练目标，难以学到可迁移的运动表征。希望从可扩展的自然istic EEG 与自我中心视频中，在无动作标注条件下学习运动grounded表征。

Method: MWM 框架：mind model 编码截至当前帧的 EEG 作为世界模型的潜在动作，world model 据此预测未来帧的表征，构造成运动grounded 的预训练目标；引入 Lateralization-Aware Transformer (LAT)，通过帧平均分离双侧共享特征与偏侧特异特征，编码半球对应关系以减少从数据中学习左右关系的学习负担。预训练产出 mind model（下游解码）与 world model（可适配为以真实动作条件化的预测）。

Result: 在 9 个分类与 7 个回归任务上，mind model 相对各任务族最强基线，平衡准确率平均提升 5.9%，Pearson 相关提升 16.2%。相对逆动力学潜在动作基线，world model 预测误差更低，在录制内从匹配真实动作中获益多 3.3 倍，在留出被试上多 4.7 倍。

Conclusion: 以 EEG 为潜在动作的世界模型预训练可获得可迁移的运动表征，兼顾下游解码与世界模型预测两用；为具身智能可扩展、灵活地采集自我中心数据提供路径。

Abstract: Motor decoding from electroencephalography (EEG) supports non-invasive brain-computer interfaces, yet existing methods either rely on costly cue-based trials and coarse categorical labels or use pretraining objectives that do not directly capture how movement unfolds, limiting their ability to learn transferable motor representations. To this end, we propose the Mind World Model (MWM), a framework that learns motor EEG representations from scalable naturalistic EEG and egocentric video without action annotations. MWM treats EEG observed up to the current frame as the latent action of a world model: a mind model encodes the EEG, and a world model uses it to predict the representation of a future frame, yielding a movement-grounded pretraining objective. We further introduce the Lateralization-Aware Transformer (LAT), which uses frame averaging to separate shared bilateral from side-specific lateral features, encoding hemispheric correspondence to reduce the burden of learning left-right relationships from data. MWM pretraining produces two models: a mind model for downstream motor decoding and a world model that can be adapted to prediction conditioned on real actions, opening a scalable and flexible path to collect egocentric data for embodied AI. Across 9 classification and 7 regression tasks, the mind model improves task-averaged balanced accuracy by 5.9% and Pearson correlation by 16.2% relative to the strongest baseline in each task family. Compared with an inverse-dynamics latent-action baseline, the world model achieves lower prediction error and benefits 3.3 times more from matched real actions within recordings and 4.7 times more on held-out subjects.

</details>


### [327] [Hypergraph Structural Entropy-Enhanced Hierarchy-Aware Semi-Supervised Graph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27043)
*Guangjie Zeng,Hao Peng,Angsheng Li,Li Sun,Chunyang Liu,Yicheng Pan,Shengze Li,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HypSEE框架,利用超图结构熵引导的层次化超图学习与锚图一致性对齐,提升半监督图分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半监督图分类可缓解标签稀缺问题,但现有方法忽视了顶点间超越成对边的复杂关系,尤其是层次结构,这些结构尚未被充分挖掘。

Method: 1) 构建游走诱导与嵌入派生两类超图,捕捉层次感知的局部与长程高阶关系;2) 设计以超图结构熵(HSE)为引导的层次化超图池化,实现层次表示学习;3) 采用锚图一致性策略对齐两个超图视图表示,增强无标签数据的学习效果。

Result: 在8个基准数据集上取得最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入超图与层次结构熵建模高阶层次关系,并结合双视图一致性,可有效提升半监督图分类,验证了挖掘层次结构的重要性。

Abstract: Semi-supervised graph classification aims to learn graph-level representations by leveraging a large number of unlabeled graphs alongside a small set of labeled ones, effectively mitigating the issue of label scarcity. Despite significant progress by various semi-supervised learning strategies, complex relationships among graph vertices beyond pairwise edges, especially hierarchical structures, remain underexplored by existing methods. In this work, we propose a Hypergraph Structural Entropy-Enhanced hierarchy-aware framework, namely HypSEE, for semi-supervised graph classification. Specifically, we first construct walk-induced and embedding-derived hypergraphs to capture hierarchy-aware local and long-range higher-order relationships within graphs. Then, we devise a hierarchical hypergraph pooling approach guided by hypergraph structural entropy (HSE) to facilitate hierarchical representation learning in hypergraphs. Lastly, we employ an anchor graph-based consistency strategy to mutually align the representations from these two hypergraph views, enhancing the learning efficacy on unlabeled data. Extensive experiments on 8 benchmark datasets demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of our approach.

</details>


### [328] [Action-Conditioned Spatio-Temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27044)
*Yutong Xia,Yangyou Liu,Yuxuan Liang,Yuankai Wu,See-Kiong Ng,Roger Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PairST基准（含配对参考/干预未来）与ActionST模型，将动作响应显式写入干预无关的参考预测，实现受影响区域准确且安静区不产生虚假变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络化系统中指定动作的时空预测难点在于：既要刻画动作效应，又不能对未受影响区域产生虚假变化；而直接监督需成对的有/无动作未来，观测轨迹只有已实现的未来。

Method: 构建PairST：涵盖交通、输电、配水共6个数据集，从同一系统状态模拟出配对的参考与干预未来，可分别评估受影响区精度与安静区响应误差；提出ActionST：在干预无关的参考预测上显式"写入"响应，采用节点-时间寻址为每个响应位置构造查询、状态条件响应生成器产生候选响应、图感知局部性门控调节强度。

Result: 在6个数据集上验证ActionST对新动作目标的有效性，在5个数据集上取得最低MAE，平均排名最优。

Conclusion: 配对仿真为动作效应提供可分离评估，显式写入响应的框架在受影响区精度与安静区稳定性之间取得平衡；代码与基准已开源。

Abstract: We study spatio-temporal forecasting in networked systems under specified actions. The challenge is to capture the action's effects without predicting spurious changes in unaffected regions. However, direct supervision and evaluation of these effects require paired futures with and without the action, whereas observational trajectories reveal only the realized future. We introduce PairST, a benchmark comprising six datasets across traffic, power transmission and water distribution, with paired reference and intervened futures simulated from the same system state. These pairs enable separate evaluation of affected-region forecast accuracy and quiet-region response error. We further propose ActionST, which forecasts the intervened future by writing an explicit response onto an intervention-independent reference forecast. Specifically, node-time addressing constructs a query for each response position, a state-conditioned response writer computes candidate responses, and a graph-aware locality gate controls their strength. Experiments on six datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of ActionST at new action targets, with the lowest mean absolute error on five datasets and the best average rank. Our source code and the PairST benchmark are available at https://github.com/yutong-xia/ActionST.

</details>


### [329] [Structural Agreement Indicates Prediction Accuracy For Protein-Ligand Co-Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27058)
*J. Henry Broster,Bojana Popovic,Diana Kondinskaia,Charlotte M. Deane,Fergus Imrie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 共折叠模型的置信分数几乎无法帮助挑选可信预测；预测间结构一致性更可靠，作者提出CORE Score并在区分成功预测上显著优于ipTM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共折叠模型能预测蛋白-配体结构，但准确性因靶点而异，用户必须知道哪些预测可信。现有置信分数被认为可指导选择，但其实际有效性未被系统检验。

Method: 在七种共折叠模型上评估基于置信度的选择效果；分析预测正确/错误的分布与高置信度的判别力；提出CORE Score（Concentration of Repeated Ensembles），将重复预测的聚类紧密度与集成支持度结合，无需训练额外不确定性估计器或实验配体构象。

Result: 置信度选择相较随机选择提升极小；约四分之三结构为全对或全错，无排序空间；高置信度难以区分正确与错误top-1。CORE Score在top-1成功与oracle成功判别上平均ROC AUC为0.866和0.838，ipTM为0.757和0.759；并优于配体Tanimoto、蛋白序列及Runs N' Poses等训练集相似度指标。

Conclusion: 结构一致性是预测可靠性的实用指标，也是评估未来置信估计器的基线，无需额外训练不确定性模型。

Abstract: Co-folding models predict the structures of proteins and their bound ligands, but their accuracy varies across targets. Their usefulness therefore depends on knowing which predictions a user can trust. Confidence scores are intended to guide that choice. However, across seven co-folding models, we show that confidence-based selection offers little improvement over choosing a prediction at random. For approximately three-quarters of structures, predictions are either exclusively correct or incorrect, leaving no opportunity for ranking to change the outcome. Moreover, high confidence does not reliably discriminate correct top-ranked predictions from incorrect ones. We find that structural agreement among predictions is a more accurate measure of likely success than current confidence scores. We capture this with the Concentration of Repeated Ensembles (CORE) Score, which combines cluster tightness with ensemble support. CORE Score discriminates top-1 success and oracle success, achieving mean ROC AUCs of 0.866 and 0.838, compared to 0.757 and 0.759 for ipTM. CORE Score also outperforms training set similarity measures, including ligand Tanimoto, protein sequence, and Runs N' Poses similarity, despite requiring no experimental ligand pose. Structural agreement is therefore a practical indicator of reliability and a baseline for evaluating future confidence estimators, without requiring an additional trained uncertainty estimator.

</details>


### [330] [COMPASS: Cross-modal Observation with Multi-Path Adaptive Stopping and Selection for Early Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27065)
*Cheng-En Chung,Yujia Wu,Oscar Tai-Yuan Chen,Vincent S. Tseng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出COMPASS两阶段框架，用于多模态早期时间序列分类中联合决定"何时停止观测"与"由哪个分支给出预测"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期时间序列分类需在预测性能与观测数据量间权衡；多模态场景下，各模态关联分支与融合分支在同一前缀下的预测能力可能不同，因此需同时决策停止时机与最终采用哪一分支。

Method: 两阶段框架，保留两个模态关联时序分支和一个融合分支。第一阶段：跨模态特征交换+门控融合，并在每个前缀处进行分支级监督。第二阶段：优化一个共享的四动作控制器，决定继续观测或通过某个冻结分支终止。

Result: 在6个生理与可穿戴数据集上，与5个ETSC基线的多模态适配版本比较，COMPASS在准确率-早期性调和平均、AUROC和早期性上平均排名最佳。在学习到的相同时刻，分支选择在6个数据集中的5个上优于融合读出，ISRUC-Sleep和UCDDB分别提升2.35和2.16个百分点。

Conclusion: 据称是首个面向多模态早期时间序列分类的联合停止与分支选择工作，验证了分支选择相较于融合读出的额外收益。

Abstract: Early time-series classification (ETSC) balances predictive performance against the amount of observed data. In multimodal settings, modality-associated and fused branches may differ in predictive ability at the same observed prefix, motivating joint decisions about when to stop and which branch should provide the final prediction. We propose COMPASS, a two-stage framework that preserves two modality-associated temporal branches and one fusion branch. The first stage combines cross-modal feature exchange and gated fusion with branch-wise supervision at every prefix. The second stage optimizes a shared four-action controller to continue observation or terminate through one of the frozen branches. Experiments on six physiological and wearable datasets compare COMPASS with multimodal adaptations of five ETSC baselines. COMPASS achieves the best average ranks in accuracy-earliness harmonic mean, AUROC, and earliness within this comparison set. At identical learned stopping times, branch selection improves mean accuracy over fusion readout on five of the six datasets, with gains of 2.35 and 2.16 percentage points on ISRUC-Sleep and UCDDB, respectively. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work on joint stopping and branch selection for multimodal early time series classification.

</details>


### [331] [Online Learning for Minimizing Maximum Tardiness in Single-Machine Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27067)
*Yiyang Chen,Kohei Hatano,Eiji Takimoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究单机调度中最小化最大拖期（OMMT）的在线学习问题，分别分析随机与对抗环境下的α-regret，证明FTL的1-regret界及对抗下α下界的紧致性，并给出基于FTPL的O(n²√T)界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在每轮需给出固定n个作业的调度排列、随后才揭示处理时间与截止期的在线设定下，最小化最大拖期缺乏理论保障；现有工作未刻画最大（minimax）型损失下的regret与α-approximation权衡，尤其是随机与对抗两种环境。

Method: 形式化OMMT为在线学习：每轮选排列、损失为各作业拖期向量，最小化α-regret。随机设定下分析Follow-the-Leader（FTL），给出期望1-regret的O(n log n/Δ²)界及高概率界；对抗设定下证明α<T log₂n/16−c时α-regret为Ω(T)下界，并将OMMT归约为替代在线问题，采用Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader（FTPL）得到α=O(log n)时O(n²√T)的期望界。

Result: 随机环境：FTL期望1-regret为O(n log n/Δ²)并有高概率界，第t轮时间O(tn²)、空间O(tn)。对抗环境：任意0<c<log₂n/16，若α<log₂n/16−c则α-regret为Ω(T)；FTPL对α=O(log n)取得O(n²√T)期望α-regret；每次FTPL更新可写为规模多项式于n与t的混合整数规划。

Conclusion: α对n的对数依赖在常数因子内是紧的；FTL适用于随机情形，FTPL在对抗情形提供有效保障，且更新可实际求解为多项式规模MIP。

Abstract: We study online learning for minimizing maximum tardiness (\OMMT) in single-machine scheduling. In each round, the learner selects a permutation of \(n\) fixed jobs, representing a schedule. The environment then reveals a processing time and a due date for each job, and the learner incurs a vector-valued loss whose components correspond to the tardiness of the individual jobs. The goal of the learner is to minimize \(α\)-regret, the difference between the learner's maximum cumulative tardiness and \(α\) times that of the best fixed permutation in hindsight, for as small \(α\) as possible. We investigate OMMT under both stochastic and adversarial environments. In the stochastic setting, we prove that the expected \(1\)-regret of the Follow-the-Leader (FTL) algorithm is \(
  O\!\left(\frac{n\log n}{Δ^2}\right) \), together with a corresponding high-probability bound, where \(Δ\) is a distribution-dependent gap parameter. At round \(t \in \{1,\dots,T\}\), FTL runs in \(O(tn^2)\) time and \(O(tn)\) space. In the adversarial setting, we prove that, for any \(0<c<\log_2 n/16\), the \(α\)-regret is \(Ω(T)\) whenever \(α<\log_2 n/16-c\). We then reduce OMMT to a surrogate online learning problem and apply Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader (FTPL), obtaining an expected \(α\)-regret bound of \(O(n^2\sqrt{T})\) for \(α=O(\log n)\). Thus, the logarithmic dependence of \(α\) on \(n\) is tight up to constant factors. Each FTPL update admits a mixed-integer programming formulation whose size is polynomial in \(n\) and \(t\).

</details>


### [332] [Accelerating Muon with Spectral Residual Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27077)
*Zhixin Ren,Yao Lyu,Shengbo Eben Li,Liping Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIMuon 在保留 Muon 动量递归的前提下，用梯度-动量残差修正参数步长，实现更快的优化收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加速 Muon 的方法多集中在变换前修改梯度或动量，本文探索互补方向：改进参数更新步本身。

Method: 从 ADMM-Inspired Momentum 框架的参数子问题推导，用梯度-动量残差修正试探性谱步；残差经独立矩阵符号变换，由谱-核二象性确定动量方向与残差方向的相对权重；两方向均用 Newton-Schulz 迭代近似，并重新缩放到与 Muon 更新幅度一致；对精确迭代给出随机平稳性界。

Result: 在 GPT-2-small、GPT-2-medium、Qwen3-0.6B 上，AIMuon 分别以少 8.98%、10.05%、6.49% 的优化步数达到 Muon 的最终验证损失，且最终验证损失低于 Muon、Muon-Nesterov 和 MONA。

Conclusion: 在 Muon 动量框架内精炼参数步是有效的加速途径，AIMuon 在多个模型上优于现有基线。

Abstract: Muon constructs matrix updates by applying a matrix-sign transformation to momentum. A common route to accelerating Muon is to modify the gradient or momentum before this transformation. We explore a complementary direction: refining the parameter step while retaining Muon's momentum recursion. Derived from the parameter subproblem of the ADMM-Inspired Momentum framework, AIMuon uses the gradient--momentum residual to correct the tentative spectral step. The residual undergoes a separate matrix-sign transformation, while spectral--nuclear duality determines the relative weighting of the momentum and residual directions. In practice, both directions are approximated with Newton--Schulz iterations and their combination is rescaled to match Muon's update magnitude. We establish a stochastic stationarity bound for the corresponding exact iteration. On GPT-2-small, GPT-2-medium, and Qwen3-0.6B, AIMuon reaches Muon's final validation loss with 8.98\%, 10.05\%, and 6.49\% fewer optimization steps, respectively, while achieving lower final validation loss than Muon, Muon-Nesterov, and MONA.

</details>


### [333] [Auditing and Adapting kNNGuard for Long-Context Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27084)
*Niccolò Ronchetti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: kNNGuard等免训练激活护栏在长输入（>1000 token）上AUROC大幅下降，作者归因于隐藏状态漂移、MiniLM 256截断与信号稀释，并用长度路由双银行、长上下文嵌入模型与跨位置Strided Window Pooling三种免训练改造显著恢复长上下文性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练激活护栏（kNNGuard LE / Embedding-kNN / 融合FE）在≤256 token短输入上有效，但长输入上性能严重退化（如LE AUROC从0.742降至0.554，RAG幻觉检测从0.994降至0.556），需定位原因并给出免训练修复方案。

Method: 先诊断三类退化原因：(1) 序列变长导致隐藏状态漂移；(2) all-MiniLM-L6-v2的硬性256 token截断；(3) 长文档压缩为单一末token向量的信号稀释。随后评估三种免训练适配：Length-Routed Dual Bank（按长度路由参考库）、替换为长上下文嵌入模型Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B、以及在中间token位置上做Strided Window Pooling。

Result: 内容有害性审核（Aegis-2.0）上，双库路由+跨窗池化使长上下文LE AUROC由0.554升至0.828（+0.274），融合FE由0.721升至0.886（+0.164），且库路由无额外延迟。RAG幻觉检测（HaluBench）上，句嵌入kNN在各长度均接近随机；长度匹配的LE在中等长度恢复至AUROC 0.853，但>1000 token仍受末token稀释限制，仅0.607。

Conclusion: 长上下文护栏退化可归因于状态漂移、截断与末token稀释三类机制；Length-Routed Dual Bank与Strided Window Pooling等免训练方案能大幅恢复内容审核性能且几乎零延迟代价，但RAG幻觉检测这类需跨段落逻辑一致性的任务仅靠末token表示仍不足，需要更细粒度或长度匹配的表示策略。

Abstract: Training-free activation guardrails such as kNNGuard score prompts by comparing last-token LLM hidden states (the Layer Ensemble branch, kNNGuard LE) and sentence embeddings (all-MiniLM-L6-v2, Embedding-kNN) against a small reference bank (50 examples per class), combining the two branches into a Fused Ensemble (kNNGuard FE). While effective on short inputs (up to 256 tokens), standard kNNGuard degrades substantially on long inputs (over 1000 tokens): averaged across Qwen3.5-4B and Gemma-4-E4B-it, kNNGuard LE AUROC drops from 0.742 (0.777 and 0.706) to 0.554 (0.587 and 0.521) on Content Harm Moderation (Aegis-2.0), and from 0.994 (0.995 and 0.992) to 0.556 (0.598 and 0.513) on RAG Hallucination Detection (HaluBench). We trace this degradation to three causes: (1) hidden-state drift as sequence length increases, (2) hard 256-token truncation in MiniLM, and (3) signal dilution when a long document is compressed into a single final-token vector. We then evaluate three training-free adaptations: a Length-Routed Dual Bank, a long-context embedding model (Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B), and Strided Window Pooling across intermediate token positions. On Content Harm Moderation, dual-bank routing and strided window pooling raise long-context kNNGuard LE AUROC from 0.554 to 0.828 (+0.274 AUROC) and fused kNNGuard FE AUROC from 0.721 to 0.886 (+0.164 AUROC) with no latency overhead from bank routing. On RAG Hallucination Detection, where the guardrail must verify logical consistency between a generated answer and a retrieved passage, sentence-embedding kNN remains near random chance across all lengths, while length-matched LLM hidden states (kNNGuard LE) recover medium-length detection (AUROC = 0.853) but remain limited on over-1000-token passages (AUROC = 0.607) due to final-token dilution over long contexts.

</details>


### [334] [ORION: Exact Canonical-State Supervision for Systematic Generalization in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27087)
*Sibi Gokul,Jyoti Prakash Meher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ORION框架，通过辅助头监督精确的规范执行状态以锚定潜轨迹，解决自回归Transformer的OOD推理失败。2x2因子实验表明RoPE与ORION作用于正交轴：RoPE恢复长度外推(59.8%)，ORION恢复深度外推(66.7%)，二者组合在长度-深度联合外推上获得严格可加的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归Transformer在分布外推理上普遍失败，作者归因于两个原因：长序列中的位置信息损坏，以及深层执行步骤中的结构状态漂移。

Method: ORION框架利用辅助头监督精确的规范执行状态，将潜轨迹锚定到有效的计算流形上。在Folding、GridShift和CLRS-30上开展预注册的2x2因子实验，对照RoPE与ORION两个因子，并设置负对照（哈希化状态统计）验证增益来源。

Result: RoPE恢复长度外推(59.8%)，ORION恢复深度外推(66.7%)，二者组合在联合长度-深度外推上呈现严格的加性增益；单独使用任一方法接近满意效果。负对照表明增益严格依赖精确的规范对齐，哈希化状态统计无法约束深度外推。

Conclusion: 位置编码修复与规范状态监督针对正交的失败轴，二者互补且增益可加，为提升Transformer的OOD推理提供了可分解的机制性方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive Transformers generally fail on out-of-distribution reasoning due to two primary reasons: positional corruption over long sequences and structural state drift across deep execution steps. We introduce ORION, a framework that supervises exact canonical execution states via auxiliary heads to anchor latent trajectories to valid computational manifolds. Across Folding, GridShift, and CLRS-30, a pre-registered 2 x 2 factorial shows that RoPE and ORION address orthogonal axes. RoPE recovers length extrapolation (59.8%), while ORION recovers depth extrapolation (66.7%). Moreover, their combination complementarily achieves strict additive gain in joint length-depth extrapolation, while either method alone offers near satisfactory results. Negative controls confirm that the gain depends strictly on exact canonical alignment; hashed state statistics fail to constrain depth extrapolation.

</details>


### [335] [PLoQ: A Prompt-Specific Low-Rank Basis for 4-bit Quantization of LLM Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27092)
*Faraz Tahmasebi,Michael Pellauer,Hyoukjun Kwon,Sitao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PLoQ提出一种提示自适应的低秩分解方法，在量化前提取激活中的主导分量并单独量化，在层内GEMM中恢复，无需修改权重，实现更优的低比特激活量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型低比特激活量化困难，因为少数主导方向决定了量化范围。现有SOTA方法通过离线校准并折叠进权重的变换将这些方向隔离到高精度子空间，但其子空间对所有提示固定，无法保护每个提示特有的方向，且在没有权重可吸收变换的场景下不可用。

Method: PLoQ是一种提示自适应的低秩分解：在量化前提取激活的主导分量，对其单独量化，并在层的GEMM中恢复，不修改权重。低秩分量的基在每个提示的prefill阶段估计，并在整个解码过程中复用。

Result: 在与SOTA混合精度量化相同的比特宽度下（4比特权重、激活和K/V缓存，1/8通道为8比特，激活平均约4.2比特），PLoQ在六个Llama和Qwen模型（1B到70B）上全部优于SOTA，支持最长8k token（其中两个模型16k），最多恢复SOTA相较4比特激活仍损失的46%困惑度。在Llama-3.1-8B、8k token下，prefill仅增加4%，解码增加8%。

Conclusion: PLoQ通过提示自适应的低秩分解克服了固定子空间的局限，无需修改权重即可在多种模型上超越现有混合精度量化方法，且开销极小。

Abstract: Low-bit activation quantization of large language models remains challenging, as a small number of dominant directions dictate the quantization range. State-of-the-art methods isolate these directions in a high-precision subspace through transformations calibrated offline and folded into the weights, which fixes the subspace for all prompts, leaving each prompt's few unique directions unprotected, and rules it out wherever no weight can absorb the transformation. We present PLoQ, a prompt-adaptive low-rank decomposition that extracts the dominant activation components before quantization, quantizes them separately, and restores them within the layer's GEMM, without modifying the weights. The low-rank component's basis is estimated from each prompt during prefill and reused throughout decoding. At the same bit width as state-of-the-art mixed-precision quantization (4-bit weights, activations and K/V cache with an eighth of the channels at 8 bits, about 4.2 bits per activation on average), PLoQ outperforms it on all six Llama and Qwen models from 1B to 70B parameters, at up to 8k tokens (16k on two of them), recovering up to 46% of the perplexity that the state of the art still loses to 4-bit activations. On Llama-3.1-8B at 8k tokens it adds only 4% to prefill and 8% to decoding.

</details>


### [336] [Reliable Behavior Discovery via False Discovery Rate Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27104)
*David Madras,Zi Wang,Marco Andreetto,Ken Burke,Caroline Pantofaru,Utsav Prabhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DISCO方法，将大生成模型中的行为发现形式化为假设检验问题，利用粗化e-过程在温和条件下可证明地控制FDR，且在合成数据与生成模型实验中比已有eBH类方法具有更高检验功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成模型日益复杂和强大，发现其行为模式需要可靠方法。作者将"行为发现"形式化为一类问题，并指出控制错误发现率（FDR）是保证发现可靠性的关键，而现有方法在此任务上功效不足。

Method: 基于多重假设检验文献，提出DISCO（Discovery through Iterative Search with COarsened e-processes），通过迭代搜索与粗化e-过程实现FDR控制，并允许对数据依赖的假设数量进行自适应，从而提升功效。

Result: 理论上可证明在温和条件下控制FDR；实验中在合成数据以及"生成模型既是发现引擎又是目标"的新型实证设置下，DISCO均以更高功效实现FDR控制，优于可比的eBH类方法。

Conclusion: DISCO为生成模型行为发现提供了可靠且高效的FDR控制框架，兼顾理论保证与经验功效，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reliable methods for discovering behavior patterns in large generative models are increasingly essential as models become both more complex and capable. We formalize behavior discovery as a problem class of interest and highlight the task of controlling false discovery rate (FDR) as crucial for guaranteeing reliability in behavior discovery. Building upon the multiple hypothesis testing literature, we propose DISCO (Discovery through Iterative Search with COarsened $e$-processes), a method which provably controls FDR in behavior discovery under mild conditions. DISCO improves power over previous related eBH-based approaches by allowing for adaptivity to a data-dependent number of hypotheses to be tested. We demonstrate empirically that DISCO controls FDR with higher power than comparable approaches on both synthetic data and a novel empirical evaluation of FDR-controlling methods in settings where large generative models act as both discovery engines and targets. Code is available at https://github.com/google-deepmind/disco .

</details>


### [337] [Federated and Balanced $k$-means: Communication Efficiency, Privacy, and Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27114)
*Feiteng Nie,Shengkun Zhu,Sheng Wang,Yuan Sun,Feiping Nie,Wei Song,Zhiyong Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Fabric，一种平衡且隐私保护的纵向联邦 k-means 算法，通过 ADMM 分解平衡聚类问题，并在目标函数评估中注入校准噪声实现可证明的隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨机构（医疗、金融）数据协作需要隐私保护下的有效分析。现有纵向联邦学习（VFL）聚类方法存在两大缺陷：易受信息泄露，以及产生不平衡的簇。

Method: 将平衡聚类问题分解为各客户端独立的子问题，用交替方向乘子法（ADMM）高效处理平衡约束；在半诚实模型下，通过对本地目标函数评估注入校准噪声提供可证明的隐私保证。

Result: 在十个真实数据集上评估，Fabric 相比最先进方法取得更优的效用-隐私权衡并收敛更快：SSE 平均降低 29.1%，平衡性能提升 1.41 倍，平均速度提升 26 倍。

Conclusion: Fabric 同时解决隐私泄露与簇不平衡问题，兼顾效用、隐私与效率，适用于跨机构联邦聚类场景。

Abstract: Collaborating on data across organizations in healthcare and finance demands methods that preserve privacy while enabling effective analysis. Vertical Federated Learning (VFL) facilitates clustering across data where different parties hold different features of the same entities without sharing raw data. However, existing VFL clustering methods suffer from two key limitations: vulnerability to information leakage and the creation of imbalanced clusters. To address these limitations, we propose Fabric, a balanced and privacy-preserving federated $k$-means algorithm. Our method decomposes the balanced clustering problem into independent subproblems for each client, using the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) to efficiently handle the balance constraints. Under the semi-honest model, Fabric provides provable privacy guarantees by injecting calibrated noise into local objective evaluations. Extensive evaluations on ten real-world datasets show that Fabric achieves a superior utility-privacy trade-off compared with state-of-the-art methods and converges faster. Specifically, Fabric reduces SSE by 29.1\% on average and improves balance performance by 1.41$\times$, while being 26$\times$ faster on average.

</details>


### [338] [A Plan or an Adversarial Example? A Round-Trip Check for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27124)
*Huiyuan Tian,Chih-Yung Wen,Shijian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 潜世界模型规划中更多CEM迭代会降低预测代价，却丢失已达一半的已解问题；作者提出执行检验与往返一致性检查，以极小开销显著提升CEM/MPPI成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像优化可产生分类器的对抗样本而非真实目标；同理，在潜世界模型中搜索"预测未来最接近目标"的动作，结果可能是真实可执行的计划，也可能是世界模型的对抗样本。作者指出这一隐患：模型的自身预测无法揭示此类失败，需识别并缓解。

Method: 1）提出简单检验：每次迭代后执行计划，统计"先解后丢"的问题数，揭示CEM迭代加深导致的性能回退；2）提出往返检查（round-trip check）：利用计划自带动作，用小网络从预测状态中回读动作，惩罚不一致；具体回读每5个动作块的和（由相邻两个预测状态唯一确定）；3）不重训世界模型，作为附加判据嵌入CEM与MPPI。

Result: 规划时间仅增加0.2%–3.1%，在三个已发布世界模型上提升CEM与MPPI性能，且在规划器丢问题更多处收益更大。无重规划时：DINO-WM上Wall成功率0.39→0.76；JEPA-WM上MPPI 0.56→0.94。有重规划（MPC）时：LeWM上Cube 0.70→0.81。

Conclusion: 潜世界模型规划存在"对抗样本"式失败，且被预测代价掩盖；基于动作可回读性的轻量一致性检验是通用、低成本且有效的修复手段，可与主流规划器即插即用。

Abstract: If we optimize an image until a classifier is confident it shows a cat, we may get an adversarial example, not a cat. Planning with a latent world model is a similar search for actions whose predicted future is closest to the goal. Is the result a plan, or an adversarial example of the world model? We show that it can be either. On the released DINO-WM and LeWM, more iterations of the cross-entropy method (CEM) lower the predicted cost but lose up to half of the problems CEM had already solved. The model's own predictions do not reveal these failures. We propose a simple test that executes the plan after every iteration and counts the problems a planner solves and then loses. Unlike an image, a plan comes with its actions, which our round-trip check reads back from the predicted states with a small network, penalizing mismatches. The check reads back the sum of each block of five actions, the part that two consecutive predicted states determine. Without retraining the world model and at only 0.2% to 3.1% more planning time, it improves the widely used planners CEM and MPPI on three released world models, and gains more where planners lose more problems. Without replanning, success rises from 0.39 to 0.76 on Wall with DINO-WM and from 0.56 to 0.94 for MPPI on JEPA-WM. With replanning (MPC), it rises from 0.70 to 0.81 on Cube with LeWM.

</details>


### [339] [Distillation with Errors: Sparse Unbiased Backpropagation for Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27131)
*Yuyang Zhang,Yuxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Distillation with Errors (DWE)，利用 logit 梯度的零和结构构造稀疏成对梯度估计，在期望上等价于全词表 KD 梯度，从而降低输出头反传开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全词表知识蒸馏需要对整个输出头反向传播，计算代价高；而小批量训练本身已近似训练梯度，逐位置反传无需精确。

Method: 基于 logit 梯度的零和结构构造稀疏成对梯度估计使期望匹配全词表梯度，并结合几何感知配对与分层采样降低估计方差，同时证明估计无偏。

Result: 相同采样预算下，隐藏态与输出权重梯度 MSE 相比重要性采样分别降低 63.5% 和 57.9%；离策略 KD 输出头最高 1.95× 加速、训练时间减少 10.4–15.7%；在线蒸馏(OPD)输出头最高 2.20× 加速；下游性能保持竞争力。

Conclusion: DWE 以无偏稀疏梯度估计有效替代全词表反传，在大幅降低 KD 训练开销的同时保持竞争性下游性能。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) trains a student to match a teacher's output distribution. However, full-vocabulary KD requires costly output-head backpropagation. To reduce this cost, we introduce Distillation with Errors (DWE). Our key insight is that minibatch training already approximates the training gradient, so per-position backpropagation does not need to be exact. DWE therefore exploits the zero-sum structure of logit gradients to construct sparse paired gradient estimates whose expectation matches the full-vocabulary gradient. It then uses geometry-aware pairing and stratified sampling to further reduce estimation variance. We prove that the estimator is unbiased and evaluate DWE's gradient estimation accuracy and training efficiency. At the same sampling budget, DWE reduces hidden-state and output-weight gradient MSE by 63.5% and 57.9%, respectively, compared with importance sampling. In off-policy KD, it achieves up to a $1.95\times$ output-head speedup and reduces training time by 10.4-15.7% relative to full-vocabulary KD. In on-policy distillation (OPD), it achieves up to a $2.20\times$ output-head speedup. DWE maintains competitive downstream performance in both settings. Our code is available at https://github.com/dwe-lab/DWE.

</details>


### [340] [Dynamic Memory-enhanced Pattern Relevance Prediction for Long-tailed Graph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27141)
*Jiawei Chen,Wenjun Wang,Wei Yu,Qiyao Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DMPRP方法，利用动态专家记忆模块将头部图的结构模式迁移到尾部图，提升长尾图分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾图分类中，少量大头部图含丰富结构信息，大量小尾部图结构线索少，导致尾部图难以学到判别性表示，需解决这种基于规模的类别不平衡。

Method: 以图表示为查询，设计受KDEX启发的动态专家记忆模块生成残差向量增强原表示；并用从头部图在节点、根子图、随机游走邻域三个层次采样的共现三元组训练该模块。

Result: 在五个图分类基准上提升尾部性能；消融表明专家模块与随机游走监督均有贡献；PTC受控实验进一步考察小图更稠密时的模式迁移。

Conclusion: 动态专家记忆与多层次共现监督能有效实现头部到尾部图的结构模式迁移，缓解长尾图分类问题。

Abstract: Long-tailed graph classification (LTGC) is common in real-world graph datasets, where a few large head graphs provide rich structural information while many smaller tail graphs offer fewer structural cues, making it harder to learn discriminative representations for tail graphs. To address this size-based imbalance, we propose Dynamic Memory-enhanced Pattern Relevance Prediction (DMPRP), which transfers structural patterns from head graphs to tail graphs. Given a graph representation as a query, a dynamic expert memory module inspired by Knowledge-diverse Experts (KDEX) generates a residual vector that augments the original representation. We train the module using co-occurrence triplets sampled from head graphs at the node, rooted subgraph, and random-walk neighborhood levels. Experiments on five graph classification benchmarks show that DMPRP improves tail performance, and ablation studies show that both the expert module and random-walk supervision contribute to these gains. A controlled experiment on PTC further examines pattern transfer when smaller graphs are denser than larger ones. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DMPRP-73A5.

</details>


### [341] [GaLF-ST: Gaussian Latent Fields for Spatial Transcriptomics Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27143)
*Baichuan Yang,Yifan Luo,Yong Liu,Bing Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GaLF-ST：用可学习高斯潜场对空间转录组表达进行端到端建模与预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组存在空间采样稀疏、观测稀疏问题；现有方法多以坐标网络直接建模且缺乏显式空间参数化，或采用"先拟合中间嵌入、再训练预测器"的两阶段流程，两阶段对潜表示要求不一致，导致潜在表示难以从坐标预测。

Method: 提出高斯潜场方法GaLF-ST：由可学习的高斯中心与各向异性协方差决定各位置潜向量的权重，共享非线性解码器将加权平均潜向量映射为多基因表达；观测表达误差联合更新高斯几何、潜向量与解码器参数（单阶段端到端）。并构建覆盖7个数据集组、3种留出比例的统一基准，在相同位置、划分、基因面板和评分规则下与4种代表性方法比较。

Result: 跨多种组织与观测密度取得领先的整体重建性能，能在有限观测下恢复空间基因表达模式并保持组织域结构，同时显著降低运行时间与GPU内存占用。

Conclusion: 显式高斯空间参数化加端到端联合优化可有效缓解ST稀疏采样与两阶段不一致问题，实现高效、高质量的空间表达重建，并提供可复现的统一评测基准。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) measures gene expression at known tissue locations to reveal tissue organization and cellular function. Expression prediction requires modeling both local spatial relationships and associations among genes. Existing methods typically model expression using coordinate networks without explicit spatial parameterization. To address limited spatial sampling and sparse expression observations of ST, some methods firstly fit intermediate embeddings and then train expression predictors, but a latent representation suited to expression reconstruction may be difficult to predict from spatial coordinates. The two training stages can impose different demands on the latent representation. In this paper, we propose GaLF-ST, a Gaussian latent field method in which learnable Gaussian centers and anisotropic covariances determine the weights of latent vectors at each location. A shared nonlinear decoder maps their weighted average to multigene expression. In GaLF-ST, observed expression error jointly updates the Gaussian geometry, latent vectors, and decoder parameters. We further establish a unified benchmark across seven dataset groups and three held-out fractions, comparing GaLF-ST with four representative methods using the same locations, splits, gene panels, and scoring rules. Across tissues and observation densities, GaLF-ST achieves leading overall reconstruction performance, recovering spatial gene expression patterns and preserving tissue domains from limited observations while substantially reducing runtime and GPU memory usage.

</details>


### [342] [Structure Over Scale: Rethinking Adaptation for Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27148)
*Allan Kazakov,Abdurrahman Javat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在RLVR中，LoRA会出现"秩坍缩"：低秩稳定但随秩增大突然崩溃，而DoRA、QuanTA等结构化适配器在更宽优化区间内稳定且接近FFT，说明更新几何结构比名义秩/参数量更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究把RLVR的适配简化为"FFT的表达力 vs LoRA的效率"二元选择，忽略了更新几何这一重要变量，作者希望揭示参数效率之外的结构性因素。

Method: 在统一冷启动GRPO协议下，对比FFT、LoRA（多秩）、DoRA、QuanTA在DeepMath-Hard等任务上的表现，并进行谱分析考察更新能量分布；同时在三个8B基座上比较适配器行为与冻结权重谱结构及预训练规模。

Result: LoRA秩坍缩显著（DeepMath-Hard上r=64准确率73.1%骤降至r=128的4.7%）；DoRA与QuanTA稳定且以<0.6%参数更新逼近FFT；坍缩的LoRA更新能量集中于少数有效方向，结构化适配器更新更分散。

Conclusion: 仅用名义秩和参数量不足以刻画RLVR中的适配，可达更新流形的结构同样重要；提出可证伪的"模型成熟度假说"——最佳适配几何可能取决于预训练阶段已编码的性质。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is often framed as a choice between the expressivity of Full Fine-Tuning (FFT) and the efficiency of Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). We find that this framing misses an important variable: the geometry of the update. Under a common cold-start GRPO protocol, LoRA exhibits rank collapse: performance remains broadly stable at low rank, then fails abruptly as rank increases. On DeepMath-Hard, for example, accuracy drops from 73.1% at r=64 to 4.7% at r=128. In contrast, Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation (DoRA) and Quantum-Informed Tensor Adaptation (QuanTA) remain stable over a wider tested optimization region and are competitive with FFT while updating less than 0.6% of the model parameters. Spectral analysis reveals a corresponding difference in learned update structure: collapsed LoRA concentrates its update energy into relatively few effective directions, whereas structured adapters produce substantially more distributed updates. These results suggest that nominal rank and parameter count alone are insufficient to characterize adaptation in RLVR - the structure imposed on the reachable update manifold also matters. Finally, across three 8B base models, adapter behavior aligns with both frozen-weight spectral structure and reported pre-training scale, motivating the Model Maturity Hypothesis: the adaptation geometry that works best may depend on properties already encoded during pre-training. We present this as a falsifiable hypothesis rather than a causal conclusion. The code can be found here https://github.com/Shrimpstanot/structure-over-scale

</details>


### [343] [The Vulnerability Window: Decision Instability in Quantized Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27155)
*Omkar Shewale,Deepak Kumar,Divakar Kumar Yadav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 4比特量化的奖励模型/LLM判官在基准准确率"几乎不变"的表象下，改写了1.1-12.3%的成对偏好决策，其中"稳定"来自错误互相抵消；这在下游导致选择大幅变化，因此应以churn/margin而非聚合准确率作为验收与路由依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励模型与LLM判官常被量化到4比特以降低打分成本，而业界验收标准是"benchmark聚合准确率几乎不变"。该标准近乎失效：聚合准确率的稳定性掩盖了量化对成对偏好决策的大规模改写，作者要揭示这一盲区并给出可用的诊断信号。

Method: 跨两族8个奖励模型（0.6B-8B）、3个生成式判官、4种4比特量化器，在RewardBench上统计成对偏好翻转率与聚合准确率变化；用高斯近似Φ(-|m|/σ)刻画翻转的聚合速率与"脆弱窗口"占用率随模型规模的变化；再做best-of-16/64下游选型实验，以及在对抗压力下有害/有益翻转的符号分析；最后比较margin类预测器与直接测得churn率的排序能力（AUC、rho）。

Result: 量化改写了1.1-12.3%的成对偏好决策，而聚合准确率仅变动0.03点（p=1.00），因修正与引入的错误相互抵消。冒烟率在全部30个标量配置中被Φ(-|m|/σ)高估，且不适用于生成式判官；脆弱窗口占用率随模型规模从25%降到7%，解释小模型更脆弱。下游：6个(模型,量化器)对中14.6-65.7%的问题best-of-64选择改变，而最终答案仅2.6-9.6%改变，选择变化约为答案变化的5-8倍；"准确率相同"的那一对仍有34.5%的选择改变。benchmark churn对下游变化的排序rho=0.89，而准确率不具备可靠排序力。margin类预测器对翻转的排序完全一致（AUC 0.927），分布内直接测churn率在水平上更优，作为路由信号与普通top-k相当。

Conclusion: benchmark聚合准确率是量化奖励模型/判官的盲指标，应改用churn类与margin类诊断；该工作贡献在于对这一现象的系统刻画，而非提出新模型。

Abstract: Reward models and LLM judges are routinely quantized to 4 bits so that scoring many candidates stays affordable, and the usual check is that benchmark accuracy barely moves. That check is close to blind. Across eight reward models from two families (0.6B-8B), three generative judges and four 4-bit quantizers, quantization rewrites 1.1-12.3% of pairwise preference decisions on RewardBench while aggregate accuracy moves by as little as 0.03 points (p = 1.00), because the errors it fixes and introduces cancel. Flips concentrate where the full-precision margin is small relative to the quantization noise scale sigma: a Gaussian approximation Phi(-|m|/sigma) describes the aggregate rate, over-predicting it in all 30 scalar settings and failing for generative judges, and occupancy of the resulting vulnerability window falls from 25% to 7% with model size, which is what makes small judges fragile. The churn stays within noise under benign best-of-16 and becomes clear under adversarial pressure for some quantizers (146:54 harmful:helpful flips on one family, reversing sign on another). Downstream it changes what ships: across six (model, quantizer) pairs from two families, 14.6-65.7% of problems get a different best-of-64 selection while only 2.6-9.6% get a different final answer, with selections changing 5-8x more often than answers in every pair, and the pair the benchmark calls identical (0.03 points, p = 1.00) still changes 34.5% of them. Benchmark churn ranks that downstream change (rho = 0.89); benchmark accuracy does not order it reliably. Held-out audits then locate the useful diagnostic in the margin rather than the Gaussian: every margin-based predictor orders flips identically (AUC 0.927), and on the level a directly measured churn rate wins in-distribution. As a routing signal it is matched by plain top-k. The contribution is the characterization, not the model.

</details>


### [344] [Hierarchical Hybrid-Coordinate Flow Matching for Accurate and Physically Plausible Apo-to-Holo Protein Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27162)
*Minjong Kim,Jaemin Sim,Dongwoo Kim,Myunghyun Jang,Eun-Sol Kim,Juyong Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CrypticFlow：层级流匹配模型，先用E(3)等变图网络在笛卡尔空间生成稀疏Cα锚点，再用条件中点Transformer在内部坐标中递归填充，兼顾全局精度与局部几何合理性，在D3PM上取得最低Cα RMSD并提升局部保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从apo结构预测holo构象对基于结构的药物设计很重要。现有方法要么在笛卡尔空间建模（全局精度高但局部主链几何不一定物理合理），要么用内部坐标（局部可控但存在杠杆臂效应导致角度误差累积）。作者希望结合两类表示的优势。

Method: 提出CrypticFlow层级流匹配模型：1）ESM-3条件化的E(3)等变图网络在笛卡尔空间生成稀疏Cα锚点；2）条件中点Transformer在局部内部坐标中递归填补间隙；递归生成时约束每个中点与序列相邻父锚点间距离等于对应的apo Cα-Cα距离。最后用cg2all将Cα骨架重建为全原子结构，并用MolProbity评估。

Result: 在D3PM测试集、已发表基线容量下取得最低中位数与均值Cα RMSD；5个独立训练模型上直接RMSD中位数1.333±0.056 Å、均值2.766±0.041 Å。局部几何显著改善：相邻距离保真度99.58±0.02%，伪角保真度95.87±0.25%（容差0.2 Å与20°）。cg2all重建后MolProbity得分3.034±0.054，优于所评估基线。

Conclusion: 实验表明，采用混合坐标的层级流匹配模型能在恢复全局精度的同时保持局部保真度，为apo-to-holo构象预测提供了兼顾两类表示优势的有效方案。

Abstract: Predicting holo-like protein conformations from apo structures is essential in structure-based drug design. Most existing apo-to-holo models operate in Cartesian space and achieve strong global accuracy. However, high global accuracy does not ensure physically valid local backbone geometry. Internal-coordinate models offer local control and validity, but regressive generation can amplify angular errors, known as the lever-arm effect. We introduce CrypticFlow, a hierarchical flow-matching model that combines these two complementary representations. An ESM-3-conditioned E(3)-equivariant graph network first generates sparse C$α$ anchors in Cartesian space. A conditional midpoint Transformer then recursively fills the gaps in local internal coordinates. During recursive generation, the decoder constrains the distance between each midpoint and any sequence-adjacent parent anchor to the corresponding apo C$α$-C$α$ distance. On the D3PM test split, CrypticFlow achieves the lowest median and mean C$α$ RMSD at published baseline capacities. Across five independent training models, the median and mean direct RMSD are 1.333 $\pm$ 0.056 angstroms and 2.766 $\pm$ 0.041 angstroms, respectively. The quality of local geometry also improves substantially. Our model achieves 99.58 $\pm$ 0.02% adjacent-distance fidelity and 95.87 $\pm$ 0.25% pseudo-angle fidelity, measured within 0.2 angstroms and 20° of the holo reference. Then, we reconstruct the predicted C$α$-only traces into all-atom structures using the cg2all coarse-to-all-atom backmapping method and evaluate their physical and geometric quality with MolProbity. Our model achieves a MolProbity score of 3.034 $\pm$ 0.054, outperforming the evaluated baselines. These results show that a hierarchical flow-matching model with hybrid coordinates can recover global accuracy while retaining local fidelity.

</details>


### [345] [BusemannFormer: Horospherical Self-Attention for Hyperbolic Graph Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27167)
*Youheng Yao,Ziyao Zeng,Wenbo Liao,Tianqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双曲自注意力HSA：查询选理想边界方向、按Busemann深度给键打分，构建全双曲图Transformer BusemannFormer，含对数深度变体与线性复杂度全局注意力扩展，节点分类上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双曲Transformer多直接改造常规注意力，未显式利用与树层次密切相关的理想边界几何结构，缺乏贴合双曲几何的注意力机制。

Method: HSA中每个查询选择理想边界方向，用键沿该方向的Busemann深度打分，形成token-token双曲注意力核；理论上建立其与点积注意力的平坦极限联系、将分数水平集刻画为horosphere并给出键侧梯度界。集成到全双曲图Transformer BusemannFormer，提出以查询深度调制注意力集中度的log-depth变体，并给出有限边界字典扩展实现线性复杂度全局注意力。

Result: 在层次图与引文图实验中，BusemannFormer-LD在多个节点分类基准上优于所评估基线，并在多种图结构上保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 显式利用理想边界与Busemann深度的双曲注意力是有效且有理论支撑的方向，兼顾几何归纳偏置与可扩展性。

Abstract: Hyperbolic neural networks excel on hierarchical graph data, yet existing hyperbolic Transformers largely adapt conventional attention without explicitly exploiting the ideal boundary, a geometric structure closely tied to tree hierarchy. We propose Horospherical Self-Attention (HSA): each query selects an ideal-boundary direction and scores keys by their Busemann depth along that direction, yielding a token-to-token hyperbolic attention kernel. We establish a flat-limit connection to dot-product attention, characterize score level sets as horospheres, and bound key-side gradients. HSA is integrated into BusemannFormer, a fully hyperbolic graph Transformer, with a log-depth variant that uses query depth to modulate attention concentration. We further introduce a finite-boundary-dictionary extension supporting linear-complexity global attention. Experiments on hierarchical and citation graphs show that BusemannFormer-LD outperforms the evaluated baselines on several node-classification benchmarks and achieves competitive performance across diverse graph structures.

</details>


### [346] [Lift Out, Kick In: Decoupling Event Effects for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27191)
*Yunying Zhu,Zichun Cao,Siwei Sun,Xiaocen Guo,Shengsheng Lin,Jing Hu,Enyun Yu,Zhichao Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Loki 是一个基于共享事件响应函数（ERF）的事件感知预测框架，通过"Lift Out"剥离历史事件偏差、"Kick In"注入未来事件响应，实现事件建模与时序预测的解耦并共享机制耦合，在五个基准上均取得最优标准化 MAE，误差降低约 2.2%-11.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实时间序列受外生事件影响，历史观测中持续动态与事件诱发的偏差相互纠缠，难以判断哪些模式应被外推、哪些应归属特定事件。现有事件感知预测方法将历史事件归因与未来事件生成割裂处理，缺乏统一机制。

Method: 提出 Loki 框架，构建共享事件响应函数（ERF），按事件类型和相对时间位置建模事件效应，并在历史窗口与预测窗口间共享。推理时先用 ERF 从历史观测中"Lift Out"（剥离）估计的事件偏差，再进行时序预测；随后用同一 ERF"Kick In"（注入）已知未来事件的响应。该形式化将事件响应建模与时序预测分离，同时通过共享机制耦合历史归因与未来生成。

Result: 在五个预测基准上均取得最优标准化 MAE，相比最强的非 Loki 基线误差降低约 2.2%-11.7%；消融与受控实验验证了 Lift Out、Kick In、共享响应建模的作用，以及对不同预测骨干网络的兼容性。

Conclusion: 事件响应建模与时序预测可有效解耦，并通过共享 ERF 统一历史归因与未来生成，Loki 作为通用框架在性能与骨干兼容性上均表现良好。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often shaped by exogenous events whose effects are superimposed on the system's underlying temporal dynamics. Historical observations therefore entangle persistent dynamics with event-induced deviations, making it difficult to determine which patterns should be extrapolated and which should be attributed to specific event contexts. Existing event-aware forecasting methods can incorporate event information or model event-related variation, but typically treat historical event attribution and future event generation separately. We propose Loki (Lift Out, Kick In), a general event-aware forecasting framework built on a shared Event Response Function (ERF). The ERF models event effects according to event type and relative temporal position and is shared across the historical and forecast windows. Loki lifts out estimated event-induced deviations from historical observations before forecasting, then uses the same ERF to kick in responses to known future events. This formulation separates event-response modeling from temporal forecasting while coupling historical attribution and future generation through a shared mechanism. Across five forecasting benchmarks, Loki achieves the best standardized MAE on all five datasets, reducing error by approximately 2.2%-11.7% over the strongest non-Loki baseline. Ablations and controlled analyses further support the roles of Lift Out, Kick In, shared response modeling, and compatibility across forecasting backbones.

</details>


### [347] [cmHC: Balancing Stream Mixing in Hyper-Connections via Geometric Contraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27193)
*Haowei He,Ziwen Zhao,Chenshuo Pan,Gang Lin,Zhihao Yang,Chao Wang,Xinzhang Liu,Kaidong Yu,Han Gao,Dongfang Li,Menglin Yang,Shuangyong Song,Yongxiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究如何在Birkhoff多胞体内有效混合残差流，揭示"混合权衡"问题，并提出几何收缩方法cmHC以缓解欠混合，同时在大规模预训练中保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: mHC将残差连接扩展为多流并约束混合矩阵在Birkhoff多胞体上，但如何在多胞体内有效混合残差流尚不清楚：欠混合时矩阵靠近多胞体顶点，超连接退化为近乎独立的流；过混合时矩阵聚集在多胞体均匀重心附近，各流难以区分。

Method: 通过界定跨层的累积残差混合、流差异与混合系数梯度差异，理论验证混合权衡；提出cmHC——一种精确双随机方法，几何上将Birkhoff多胞体向其内部收缩，迫使残差矩阵更深入多胞体探索，具有可证明的流混合界且无新增参数化；在浅层施加适度收缩以防过混合；并为Ascend NPU开发融合算子。

Result: 在多种参数规模的稠密模型与MoE模型上验证了有效性与可扩展性；cmHC支持超大规模模型预训练的稳定梯度传播；Ascend NPU融合算子将超连接执行延迟最多降低44.8%。

Conclusion: cmHC通过几何收缩Birkhoff多胞体，在无新参数下平衡欠混合与过混合，实现可证明的流混合界与大规模预训练稳定性，兼具高效硬件实现。

Abstract: Manifold-constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC) extends residual connections to multiple streams and stabilizes residual propagation by constraining stream-mixing residual matrices to the Birkhoff polytope, the set of doubly stochastic matrices. In this work, we investigate how to effectively mix residual streams within the Birkhoff polytope. We reveal a mixing trade-off: when under-mixed residual matrices stay close to the vertices of the Birkhoff polytope, hyper-connections will degenerate into a set of nearly independent streams; when over-mixed residual matrices are concentrated around the uniform barycenter of the polytope, streams will become less distinguishable from each other. We theoretically validate our findings by bounding the cumulative residual mixing, stream difference and gradient difference of mixing coefficients across layers. Motivated by these findings, we propose Contraction for Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections (cmHC), an exact doubly stochastic method which geometrically contracts the Birkhoff polytope toward its interior. This contraction mitigates under-mixing by forcing the residual matrices to explore deeper into the polytope, with a provable stream mixing bound and no new parameterization. Moreover, we apply a moderate contraction at the shallow layers to prevent over-mixing, enabling stable gradient propagation on extremely large-scale model pretraining. Extensive experiments across dense and mixture-of-experts models at various parameter scales demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of cmHC. We also develop fused operators for cmHC on Ascend NPUs, reducing hyper-connection execution latency by up to 44.8%.

</details>


### [348] [Improved Accuracy Scaling with Problem Complexity in LLM Arithmetic Reasoning Using Executable Symbolic Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27203)
*Yu-Bin Liu,Shi-Ju Ran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出可控基准GSM-AB，独立变化操作数位数与运算数量，发现可执行符号表示（Lean、Python-PoT）能提升算术准确率并减缓随复杂度上升的衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学基准上的高准确率无法说明推理在问题复杂度增加时是否仍然可靠，需探究表示形式如何影响准确率的缩放规律。

Method: 构建GSM Arithmetic Benchmark（GSM-AB），将操作数数字长度与运算个数作为两个独立维度进行控制变量测试，比较自然语言推理与可执行符号表示（Lean、Python程序化思维PoT对照）在不同模型规模下的表现。

Result: 可执行表示提升准确率并减缓其下降；Lean在各测试模型规模下均降低对操作数位长的平均敏感度；对运算数量的衰减减缓仅在较大模型规模出现，Python-PoT对照呈相似运算轴趋势，说明并非Lean特有。

Conclusion: 推理表示形式塑造算术准确率的缩放行为，支持采用可执行符号表示以在复杂算术任务上获得更可靠的推理。

Abstract: High accuracy on mathematical benchmarks does not establish whether reasoning remains reliable as problem complexity increases. We examine how executable symbolic representations change accuracy scaling in large language models compared with natural-language reasoning. To address this limitation, we introduce the GSM Arithmetic Benchmark (GSM-AB), a controlled benchmark that independently varies operand digit length and operation count to probe numerical scale and compositional complexity. We find that executable representations can improve accuracy and slow its decline as complexity increases. Lean reduces average sensitivity to operand digit length at every tested model size. By contrast, slower degradation with increasing operation count emerges at the larger model sizes. A Python program-of-thought (Python-PoT) control shows a similar operation-axis pattern, suggesting that this finding is not specific to Lean. These findings show that reasoning representation shapes arithmetic accuracy scaling and support executable symbolic representations for more reliable reasoning on complex arithmetic tasks.

</details>


### [349] [LingSante-Pulse: Unified Physiological Pretraining through Random-Feature Prediction and Cross-Modal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.27206)
*Linxiao Yang,Wenwei Wang,Tian Zhou,Bingqing Peng,Zihao Zhang,Zuyi Zhu,Jinjie Gu,Liang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一、通用的生理信号表征框架 LingSante-Pulse，用共享信号条件编码器处理异质多模态生理记录，在睡眠分期、呼吸暂停筛查等38个任务上取得优异性能。


<details>
